## DLC_FIRST_CONTACT_RECOMMENDATION_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADLC_FIRST_CONTACT_RECOMMENDATION_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§SFirst Contact§!是一个探索与前超光速文明早期遭遇的故事包。该故事包同时还引入了隐形科技！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:3`

### English

§SFirst Contact§! is a story pack that explores early encounters with pre-FTL civilizations. This story pack also introduces Cloaking technology!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:4`

## DLC_FIRST_CONTACT_RECOMMENDATION_REC_DIPLO

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADLC_FIRST_CONTACT_RECOMMENDATION_REC_DIPLO&lang=en)

### 中文

我们推荐§SFirst Contact§!，因为该故事包所包含的新特色可以让你与前超光速社会的交互更加动态和具有沉浸感。

## DLC_FIRST_CONTACT_RECOMMENDATION_REC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ADLC_FIRST_CONTACT_RECOMMENDATION_REC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们推荐§SFirst Contact§!因为你所选的部分变量适用于其中的内容。该故事包着重于与前太空文明的外交、谍报和对其发展的观测。

## personality_decadent_capitalists_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apersonality_decadent_capitalists_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

弥那玛特种实业集团自诩为一个仁善的企业，致力于向欠发达的文明提供技术以促进其进步。多亏了公司激进的高利贷业务和横跨整个星系的契约劳工，股东们受到保证，能够收取丰厚的红利。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:17`

### English

Styling themselves a benevolent corporation, Minamar Specialized Industries offers technological advancement to underdeveloped civilizations. Thanks to the company's aggressive usury and a galaxy-spanning pool of indentured labor, shareholders are guaranteed a handsome return on investment.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:20`

## origin_slavers_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin_slavers_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

自打$NAME_MSI_country$的董事会第一次将我们的文明送抵星辰，我们就明白这样一个道理，宇宙是如此瑰丽璀璨，不将其分享给他人乃是暴殄天物。从那时起，我们就不遗余力地帮助欠发展的文明实现超光速旅行。\n\n尽管启蒙并不是免费的，但在$NAME_MSI_country_initials$，这是一笔值得的投资。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:19`

### English

From the moment the Board of Directors at $NAME_MSI_country$ first delivered our civilization to the stars, we have understood that the galaxy was too beautiful not to be shared. Since then, we have worked tirelessly to help less developed civilizations achieve FTL travel.\n\nEnlightenment may not be free. But at $NAME_MSI_country_initials$, it is always worth the cost.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:23`

## origin_tooltip_slavers_effects

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin_tooltip_slavers_effects&lang=en)

### 中文

—游戏开始时成为§H高级帝国§!\n—开局时拥有一些被奴役的£pop£§H人口§!，它们是从全星系的各个§H前超光速文明§!处绑架得来。\n—允许采用§H$bombardment_raiding$§!轰炸姿态，能够从行星上绑架£pop£§H人口§!，并将其重新迁移到你的殖民地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:20`

### English

- Starts as an §HAdvanced Empire§!\n- Start with a number of enslaved £pop£ §HPops§! abducted from §HPre-FTL Civilizations§! across the galaxy.\n- Allows the §H$bombardment_raiding$§! bombardment stance, which abducts £pop£ §HPops§! from planets and resettles them to your colonies.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:24`

## first_contact.4000.desc.broken_shackles

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.4000.desc.broken_shackles&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已与[MSI_country.GetName]取得联系。当务之急的事情是，我们必须要小心翼翼地驾驭不断变化的局势。我们应该派遣一名特使来代表我们的利益。无论与我们以前的主人进行任何互动，这肯定会对我们的人民产生深远的心理影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:27`

### English

Contact has been made with [MSI_country.GetName]. It's imperative that we navigate the evolving situation carefully. An envoy should be be dispatched to represent our interests. Any interaction with our former masters is sure to have a profound psychological effect on our people.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:33`

## first_contact.4000.desc.payback

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.4000.desc.payback&lang=en)

### 中文

在§H[This.System.GetName]§!遭遇的外星飞船上得到的数据让我们不寒而栗。它的外观我们再熟悉不过了，上面的徽章宛如我们恐惧的象征：§H$NAME_MSI_country$§!的带翼图章。\n\n我们找到了曾经的侵略者。他们发现我们这些舰船的身份只是时间的问题，因为使用的完全都是他们的科技。新的接触不可避免，但我们绝不会重复过去的错误。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:28`

### English

Data on the alien vessels we encountered in the §H[This.System.GetName]§! was met with a shudder of recognition. Familiar ship designs, marked by an emblem we learned to fear: the winged logo of the §H$NAME_MSI_country$§!.\n\nOur former invaders have been found. It's only a matter of time until they realize the ships they sighted are ours, fueled by their own technology. A new contact may be unavoidable, but we can avoid repeating past errors.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:34`

## origin_payback_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin_payback_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§R-警告：具有挑战性的起源§!\n这个文明曾被一个异星帝国造访过，这个帝国强行启蒙了他们，之后奴役了他们。如今他们击退了入侵者，但那些入侵者有朝一日还会回来吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:36`

### English

§RWARNING: Challenging Origin§!\nThis civilization has been visited by an alien empire who forcibly enlightened and enslaved them. They have repelled their invaders, but will they return?

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:44`

## START_SCREEN_PAYBACK

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTART_SCREEN_PAYBACK&lang=en)

### 中文

我们曾经做过一个交易。\n\n当来自外部星辰的访客显露身形时，他们向我们提供了一份公平的合同：技术启蒙，分期偿还。在一代人的时间里，我们的行星被转变成为了一个所谓的“先进文明”。\n\n然后我们才了解到小字条款：为了偿还我们的“债务”，他们坚持要我们为他们劳作，而且是无限期的。我们的领袖对此反对，但这些外来者马上翻脸动用了暴力。我们奋起反抗。\n\n我们赢了，但是赢得十分艰难，并且为此付出了惨烈得代价。现在，我们的科学家终于从过去我们摧毁的飞船上，通过逆向工程攻破了超光速旅行的秘密。宇宙很大，但迟早我们肯定会再次遇到我们的“恩人”。而实际上，我们也迫不及待，摩拳擦掌。\n\n现在是时候血债血偿了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:41`

### English

We had a deal.\n\nWhen the visitors from the stars revealed themselves, they offered us a fair contract: technological enlightment, to be repaid in installments. Within a generation, our planet was transformed into a so-called 'advanced civilization.'\n\nThen we read the fine print: to repay our 'debt', they insisted we work for them, on an indefinite term. When our leaders protested, the outsiders turned violent. We fought back.\n\nWe won, though barely, and only after suffering sizeable losses. Now, our scientists have finally reverse-engineered the secrets of FTL travel from the ships we destroyed. It's a big galaxy out there, but sooner or later we're sure to meet our 'benefactors' again. In fact, we're looking forward to it.\n\nIt's time to get some payback.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:51`

## payback_unified_purpose_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apayback_unified_purpose_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

侵略者的到来使我们的星球团结统一起来。现在我们已经重新掌握了自己的命运，要做的工作有很多。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:51`

### English

The arrival of the invaders unified our planet. Now that we have regained control of our destiny, there is work to be done.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:65`

## payback_debris_field

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apayback_debris_field&lang=en)

### English

Debris Field

## payback_debris_field_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apayback_debris_field_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由损毁的弥那玛飞船产生的轨道残骸频繁地掉落到下方的行星上。任何地表基础设施都需要更加频繁和更多投入的修复工作，才能恢复造成的损害。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:53`

### English

The orbiting debris from the wrecked Minamar ship frequently rains down upon the planet below. Any surface infrastructure will require more regular and intensive maintenance to repair the damage done.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:68`

## d_payback_habitat_research

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_payback_habitat_research&lang=en)

### English

Technological Cache

## d_payback_habitat_research_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_payback_habitat_research_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在前弥特集团的战舰内部，我们遇到了一个千载难逢的机会，可以解锁他们科技的奥秘。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:55`

### English

The interior of the former MSI warship provides us with an unmatched opportunity to unlock the secrets of their technology.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:71`

### English

## d_msi_air_freshener_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_msi_air_freshener_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些通风装置结合了最新的芳香疗法和先进的化学技术，掩盖了绝望的恶臭，取而代之的是各种令人振奋和愉快的气味。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:57`

### English

These ventilation units combine the latest advances in aromatherapy with advanced chemistry to mask the malodorous stench of despair and replace it with a variety of invigorating and delightful scents.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:74`

## origin.4200.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4200.name&lang=en)

### English

Board Meeting

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:83`

## origin.4200.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4200.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

同我们前入侵者的会面促使我们的政坛领袖、宗教人士、科学家、艺术家和外交官之间举行了一场紧急会议。各界人士纷纷就各种战略进行讨论、否定、重新论证、改进、投票表决。经过几个月的高强度磋商，我们帝国最聪明的头脑已经达成了类似于共识的东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:64`

### English

The sighting of our former invaders has prompted an emergency meeting between political leaders, religious figures, scientists, artists, and diplomats. Strategies have been discussed, rejected, reconsidered, improved, voted on. After months of tense consultations, the brightest minds of our empire have reached something resembling a consensus.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:84`

## origin.4200.a.auth

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4200.a.auth&lang=en)

## origin.4205.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4205.name&lang=en)

大众共识

### English

Public Consensus

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:94`

## origin.4205.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4205.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管采取了一些安管措施，我们与§H$NAME_MSI_country$§!发生遭遇的新闻依然传播给了公众，给这个刚刚开始从苦痛的过往中恢复过来的行星造成了不小的混乱。公众舆论……难以统一：有许多持不同看法的声音，从出离的愤怒到不怀好意的怀旧之情。无论我们作出什么样的决策，都会需要一定的暴力阻力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:74`

### English

Despite security measures, news of our encounter with the §H$NAME_MSI_country$§! is spreading to the public, bringing chaos to a planet that was just beginning to recover from its past. Public opinion is... Fragmented: many dissenting voices, expressing everything from rage to ill-aimed nostalgia. Regardless of the decision, our actions will be met with violence.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:95`

### English

They have the right to express their views.

### English

### English

## origin.4210.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4210.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

经过长时间的周密准备，我们的特使已经向$NAME_MSI_country_initials$呈递了一条信息。我们已经准备好按照自己的条件会见前主人，为我们人民的意愿发声。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:78`

### English

After extended preparations, our envoys have sent a message to the $NAME_MSI_country_initials$. We are ready to meet our former masters on our terms, giving voice to the will of our people.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:100`

## payback_out_of_warranty_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apayback_out_of_warranty_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

前入侵者为我们建造的§Y$building_medical_2$§!已经年久失修。其中的大部分科技超出了我们目前的认知，但我们还是可以试着§S修复§!一下。\n\n§L与其他的帝国签订科研协议将会提高我们成功的概率。[GetAgreementStatus]§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:81`

### English

The §Y$building_medical_2$§! our invaders built for us are suffering from lack of maintenance. While most of the technology is beyond our current understanding, we could try to §Srepair§! it.\n\n§LSigning research pacts with other empires will increase our chances of success. [GetAgreementStatus]§!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:104`

## origin.4300.base.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4300.base.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“我们要终结疾病。”\n\n这就是入侵者们赢得我们的方式。我们允许他们在[Root.GetHomeWorldName]上建造§Y$building_medical_2$§!，在一代人的时间里根除了绝大多数最要命的疾病。然而现在，这些诊所正在停摆，多年的高强度使用导致里面的机器陷入了停滞状态。我们无法得知这些入侵者的先进技术是如何正常运作的。我们能否对其进行逆向工程？或者我们应该把这个有毒的礼物改造成某种真正属于我们的东西？\n

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:94`

### English

"We are going to end illness."\n\nThat's how the invaders won us over. We allowed them to build §Y$building_medical_2$§! on [Root.GetHomeWorldName], eradicating most of our worst diseases within a generation. Now, those clinics are breaking down, years of intense use causing the machines within to grind to a halt. The proper functioning of the invader's technology is beyond us. Can we reverse-engineer it? Or should we repurpose this poisoned gift into something truly our own?\n

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:119`

## origin.4300.aliens.phobe.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4300.aliens.phobe.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$origin.4300.base.desc$尽管其他国家可能也有类似的设施，但是向异种求助无疑会让我族蒙羞。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:95`

### English

$origin.4300.base.desc$Other empires might possess similar facilities, but asking the xenos for help would only bring us shame.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:120`

$origin.4300.base.desc$Other empires might possess similar facilities. We should ask for help.

## origin.4301.base.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4301.base.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的前入侵者或许会将这一切视作来去无踪的狗屎运，或者宣称修复工作是由他们主导的。他们甚至会说，我们所做的对§Y$building_medical_2$§!背后的技术进行逆向工程压根就不是什么壮举。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:105`

### English

Our former invaders would likely wave it off as a stroke of luck, or argue that they did most of the work. They would claim it's not a big feat, to retro-engineer the technology behind the §Y$building_medical_2$§! as we just did.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:131`

## origin.4301.noagreement.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4301.noagreement.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$origin.4301.base.desc$但不管他们说什么，我们都自主独立地完成了，没有他们的任何协助，这下胜利独属于我们了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:106`

### English

$origin.4301.base.desc$ But we did it anyway, without their assistance, and the triumph is ours alone.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:132`

## origin.4301.agreement.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4301.agreement.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$origin.4301.base.desc$但不管他们说什么，在慷慨的盟友派出的专家的帮助下，我们都完成了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:107`

### English

$origin.4301.base.desc$ But we did it anyway, supported by the expertise of our generous allies.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:133`

## origin.4302.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4302.name&lang=en)

### 中文

修复的正当性

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:111`

### English

Right To Repair

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:138`

## origin.4302.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4302.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

事实证明，要想对使§Y$building_medical_2$§!运转的技术进行逆向工程，这件事比我们料想的更具挑战性。但我们的[root.GetEngineerPlural]仍然充满希望。他们说，自己只是需要更多的时间。然而，随着成本和失败开始堆积，一些人认为应当对诊所进行改造，使其用于另一项事务。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:112`

### English

Reverse engineering the technology powering the §Y$building_medical_2$§! turned out to be more challenging than expected, but our [root.GetEngineerPlural] remain hopeful. They just need more time, they say. But as costs and failures start piling up, some argue the clinics should be retrofitted to suit a different purpose.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:139`

### English

## origin.4303.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4303.name&lang=en)

### 中文

拙劣的修复工作

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:119`

### English

Botched Repair

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:147`

## origin.4303.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4303.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们尝试维修§Y$building_medical_2$§!的努力最终失败了：巨大的爆炸吞没了建筑，现场死伤惨重。\n\n无疑，前入侵者一定正在嗤嗤嘲笑我们的自大、愚蠢和鲁莽。这次失败我们咎由自取，但这堂课我们也同样印象深刻。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:120`

### English

Our attempt to repair the §Y$building_medical_2$§! ended in failure: a massive explosion engulfed the building, and numerous casualties have been reported.\n\nNo doubt our former invaders are laughing, calling us arrogant, foolish, unprepared. Yet this failure belongs to us, and its hard lessons will not be forgotten.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:148`

## origin.4304.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4304.name&lang=en)

### 中文

焕然一新的硬件

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:122`

### English

Refurbished Hardware

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:151`

## origin.4304.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4304.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的§Y$building_medical_2$§!可能不再能够治愈疾病，但是通过将一些关键部件拆卸下来，并将其重新用于纪念馆的展出，至少我们能够开始治愈入侵者留下的疤痕。与过去和解，我们才能逐渐克服这一切。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:123`

### English

Our §Y$building_medical_2$§! may no longer have the power to cure disease, but by stripping them of their components and repurposing them into memorials, we can at least start to heal the scars left by the invaders. By making peace with the past, we can learn to overcome it.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:152`

## origin.4305.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4305.name&lang=en)

### 中文

医保危机

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:125`

### English

Healthcare Crisis

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:155`

## origin.4305.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4305.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于我们失去了§Y$building_medical_2$§!，医疗保障的质量急剧下降。曾经很容易治愈的遗传病和慢性病病例又开始上升，我们的预期寿命中位数返回到了占领前的水平。公众对医疗保障系统的信心已经达到了新的低点，我们中的一些人甚至开始感叹，入侵者走了不如不走。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:126`

### English

The loss of our §Y$building_medical_2$§! has precipitated a sharp decline in the quality of our healthcare. Genetic conditions and chronic illnesses that were once simple to cure are again on the rise, and our median life expectancy has returned to pre-occupation levels. Public faith in the healthcare system has reached a new low, and some of our people are even starting to lament the departure of the invaders.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:156`

### English

## origin.4306.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4306.name&lang=en)

### 中文

自给自足

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:133`

### English

Self-sufficient

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:164`

## origin.4306.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4306.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在掌握了基因工程的奥秘之后，我们的人民现在有能力建造§Y$building_medical_2$§!，其能力水平同入侵者先前留下来的那些一模一样。对我们的人民来说，这是极其光荣的一天：证明我们无需[root.species.GetHandName]的把控，也能触及星辰。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:134`

### English

Having mastered the secrets of genetic engineering, our people are now able to build §Y$building_medical_2$§! like the ones left by the invaders. It's a glorious day for our people: proof that we need no [root.species.GetHandName]-holding to reach the stars.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:165`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:175`

## payback_digsite_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apayback_digsite_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

曾经威胁我们家园的战舰仍然高悬在头顶，严酷地提醒着我们侥幸逃脱的命运。现在该去夺回我们的天空，取回这艘舰船残骸中的高超科技了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:141`

### English

The warship that once threatened our homeworld still looms over us, a stark reminder of the fate we narrowly escaped. It is time to take back our skies and salvage the technological marvels contained within the wreck.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:176`

## origin.4900.name

## origin.4900.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4900.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

与外星侵略者的战斗使我们的母星系充满了残骸、破碎的战舰和背弃的诺言。大量的废弃物料散布其中，使得从[owner.capital_scope.GetName]研发卫星和发射宇宙飞船变得更加困难。得益于清理计划的提出，专门设计的工程船可以用网在近地轨道上进行清理，让我们逐渐打通去往那艘仍漂浮在我们首都上空的$NAME_MSI_country_initials$战舰的路线。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:147`

### English

The battle against the alien invaders left our home system full of debris, shattered warships, and broken promises. The sheer amount of flotsam cluttering the area has made it harder to develop satellites and to launch spaceships from [owner.capital_scope.GetName]. Under the proposed cleanup project, specially-designed construction ships will utilize nets to dredge the space in near-orbit, allowing us to approach the $NAME_MSI_country_initials$ warship that still looms over our capital.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:186`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:148`

### English

清理进行中

### English

Cleanup In Progress

## origin.4930.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4930.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们星系中的清理工程正在顺利进行中。[owner.capital_scope.GetName]上空的碎片数量大幅减少了。随着天空逐渐明晰，我们的未来也变得越来越光明。工作仍在继续，一切都在向好的方向发展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:154`

### English

The cleanup operation in our system is well underway. The amount of debris over [owner.capital_scope.GetName] has been significantly reduced, and with clearing skies, our expectations for the future are also bright. Work continues, but things are looking better.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:197`

## origin.4931.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4931.name&lang=en)

### 中文

晴朗天空

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:156`

### English

Clear Skies

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:201`

## origin.4931.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4931.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

清理工作已经大功告成了。穿过这一片没有碎片的天空，我们的科研船就可以靠近战舰并安全地开始探索残骸。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:157`

### English

The cleanup operation is now complete. With our skies free from debris, our science ships are now able to approach the warship and safely explore the wreck.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:202`

## origin.4905.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4905.name&lang=en)

### 中文

评估损伤

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:160`

### English

Assessing Damage

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:209`

## origin.4905.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4905.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的传感器无法在废弃战舰中探测到任何生命迹象，空空如也的船舱里引擎仍在轻微地嗡嗡作响，各种机械也还在运转。有理由相信，这艘船仍可正常运转，如果强行登船肯定会激活其安保系统。\n\n我们务必谨慎开展工作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:161`

### English

Our sensors cannot detect signs of life within the derelict warship, and yet the engines still faintly buzz and machines are at work inside the empty halls. There is good reason to believe the ship is still operational, and attempts to board it are sure to awaken its security system.\n\nWe must proceed with caution.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:210`

## origin.4910.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4910.name&lang=en)

### 中文

回收资产

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:163`

### English

Reclaiming Assets

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:214`

## origin.4910.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4910.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的施工人员正在清除碎片，修复损伤并确保舱室安全，把这艘曾经威胁我们星球的战舰变成新的行动基地。在残骸中，我们找到了越来越多的船员尸体或者残肢断臂。\n\n如何处理这些遗体引发了公众的热议。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:164`

### English

Our construction crews are clearing debris, repairing damage and securing rooms, turning the warship that once terrorized our world into a base of operations. Amidst the wreckage, we have uncovered the bodies of the ship's crew, or rather, what's left of them.\n\nWhat to do with these remains is a matter of public debate.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:215`

### English

## origin.4920.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4920.name&lang=en)

### 中文

分析竞争

### English

Analyzing The Competition

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:223`

## origin.4920.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4920.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当我们仔细搜索战舰的中央数据库的时候，我们逐渐搞清楚了前任主人的整个计划。他们打着启蒙的幌子接近我们，只是为了将我们训练成为更好的奴隶；他们满怀善意呈上的计划和协议，只不过是精心编织的谎言。我们的星球显然不是他们第一次这么干，而是他们核心“商业战略”的一个典范。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:171`

### English

As we scour the warship's central database, the designs of our former masters have grown clear. The way they approached with enlightenment, only to train us to become better slaves; their well-honed plans and protocols; their calculated lies. Our world was clearly not their first attempt, but rather an example of their core 'business strategy.'

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:224`

### English

### English

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:229`

## origin.4925.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4925.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经完全控制了这艘废弃战舰的系统。我们现在必须决定如何处理它。根据我们的需求，它对于战争和研究都可以成为非常有利用价值的资源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:175`

### English

We have gained complete control of the derelict warship's systems. We now must decide what to do with it. Depending on our needs, it could be a powerful asset for either war or research.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:230`

## RESTORE_WARSHIP_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ARESTORE_WARSHIP_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这艘战舰将成为我们舰队的瑰宝。\n\n这艘战舰将得到修复，变成一艘功能齐全的§H旗舰§!，能够作为一个移动船坞使用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:181`

### English

The warship will become the gem of our fleet.\n\nThe warship shall be repaired and turned into a functioning §HFlagship§!.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:240`

## RESTORE_HABITAT_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ARESTORE_HABITAT_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这艘战舰经过改造之后，现在将用于科学研究。\n\n经过修复之后，这台曾经的死亡机器现在被转变成一个功能齐全的§H居住站§!，致力于揭开弥特集团科技的秘密。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:183`

### English

The warship will now be retrofitted for scientific research.\n\nFollowing repairs, this former instrument of death will be fully converted into a functioning §HHabitat§!, devoted to uncovering the secrets of MSI's technology.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:242`

## origin.4934.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4934.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对前弥特集团战舰进行改造的工作已经完成。现在我们能够指挥一艘具有强大火力和战略意义的舰船了。\n\n接招吧，星辰大海！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:190`

### English

The retrofit of the former MSI Warship is complete. We now have at our disposal a vessel of massive power and significance.\n\nWatch out, galaxy!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:253`

## origin.4934.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4934.tt&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:191`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:254`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4935.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

对前弥特集团战舰进行改造的工作已经完成。现在我们拥有了最先进的科研设施，它将会为我们推进科技知识的开发提供帮助。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:193`

### English

The retrofit of the former MSI Warship is complete. We have now gained a state of the art science facility which will be instrumental to us in advancing our technological knowledge.

## origin.4935.tt

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:194`

## origin.4936.name

## origin.4937.name

### English

Flagship Upgraded!

## origin.4937.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4937.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经升级了旗舰。利用船上现有的技术，我们能够对它的系统进行逆向工程，以获得对飞船内部运作更好的了解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:199`

### English

We have upgraded the flagship. Utilizing the existing technology onboard, we have been able to reverse-engineer its systems and gain a better understanding of the inner workings of the vessel.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:268`

## payback_debt_collectors_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apayback_debt_collectors_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的前主人声称我们亏欠他们战争赔款。他们已经雇佣了一个星际债务回收机构来处理此事。\n\n[root.GetDebtCollectorsDeal]\n\n§L收债人将在§Y[root.payback_debt_collectors_timer]§!访问我们。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:204`

### English

Our former masters claim we owe them war reparations. An intergalactic debt collection agency has been hired to settle the matter.\n\n[root.GetDebtCollectorsDeal]\n\n§LThe debt collectors will visit us on§! §Y[root.payback_debt_collectors_timer]§!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:276`

## payback_make_them_pay_swords_chain_desc_MSI

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apayback_make_them_pay_swords_chain_desc_MSI&lang=en)

### 中文

是时候回敬我们的前宗主$NAME_MSI_country$了。\n\n我们已经不再是当初那个由他们随意剥削的新生文明，现在，是时候让他们血债血偿了。\n\n§L使用§Y$casus_belli_cb_payback$§!宣战理由赢得对于§H$NAME_MSI_country$§!的战争。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:214`

### English

It's time for us to retaliate against our former masters, the $NAME_MSI_country$.\n\nWe are no longer the naive world they so readily exploited. In the end, they will be made to pay.\n\n§LWin a war against the §H$NAME_MSI_country$§! using the §Y$casus_belli_cb_payback$§! Casus Belli.§!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:287`

## payback_make_them_pay_swords_chain_desc_no_MSI

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apayback_make_them_pay_swords_chain_desc_no_MSI&lang=en)

### 中文

我们反击前主人$NAME_MSI_country$的计划被[event_target:payback_stole_revenge_country.GetName]挫败了。\n\n他们要为盗取了我们的复仇而付出代价。\n\n§L对§H[event_target:payback_stole_revenge_country.GetName]§!以§Y$casus_belli_cb_payback$§!作为理由宣战并获胜。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:215`

### English

Our plans to retaliate against our former masters, the $NAME_MSI_country$, have been twarted by the [event_target:payback_stole_revenge_country.GetName].\n\nThey will be made to pay for stealing our revenge.\n\n§LWin a war against the §H[event_target:payback_stole_revenge_country.GetName]§! using the §Y$casus_belli_cb_payback$§! Casus Belli.§!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:288`

## payback_make_them_pay_words_chain_desc_MSI

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apayback_make_them_pay_words_chain_desc_MSI&lang=en)

### 中文

是时候开始向我们的前主人$NAME_MSI_country$发起还击了。\n\n在星海共同体的参议院中，我们会让他们屈膝，揭露他们奴隶贩子和懦夫的事实。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:218`

### English

It's time for us to retaliate against our former masters, the $NAME_MSI_country$.\n\nOn the Senate Floor of the Galactic Community, we will bring them to their knees, exposing them for the slavers and cowards that they are.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:292`

## payback_make_them_pay_words_chain_desc_no_MSI

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apayback_make_them_pay_words_chain_desc_no_MSI&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经挣脱了来自前宗主的锁链，但征途不会到此为止，我们将在星海共同体的参议院中向所有仍想剥削利用前超光速文明的国家开展斗争。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:219`

### English

Our former masters are no more, but our goal doesn't change: on the Senate Floor of the Galactic Community, we will fight all those who wish to exploit pre-FTL civilizations.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:293`

### 中文

### English

The Bill

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:352`

## origin.4010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个小型的自推档案存储器刚刚进入了我们的母星系边缘，用一个信号信标大声宣告了其到来。该档案一经取得，就显示出它所包含的阴森信息：一份长长的列表，里面详细列出了在与侵略者作战期间我们摧毁的所有军事资产，包括船组人员的伤亡，以及每艘被摧毁的船只的估计价值。\n\n我们先前的入侵者正式提出了战争赔款。更讽刺的是，他们还声称，在我们的星系中被摧毁的所有船只仍然是他们的财产，任何试图对残骸进行摧毁、改装或者逆向的行为都将会使我们违反条款。\n\n一个债务收集机构很快将与我们联系，以便解决此事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:266`

### English

A small, self-propelled cache just entered the edges of our home system, loudly announcing its arrival with a signal beacon. Once retrieved, the cache revealed its grim contents: a lengthy list detailing all the military assets we destroyed during the war against our invaders, complete with crew casualties and the estimated value of each destroyed vessel.\n\nOur former invaders are officially demanding war reparations. This despite the fact that they are the ones who invaded us. Even more outrageously, they also claim that all the destroyed ships in our system are still their property, and any attempt to destroy, refit or retro-engineer the wreckage will put us in breach of terms and conditions.\n\nA debt collection agency will soon contact us to settle the matter.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:353`

## origin.4015.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4015.name&lang=en)

### 中文

收债人

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:270`

### English

Debt Collectors

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:358`

## origin.4015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

亲爱的[owner.GetName]，\n\n我们代表§H$NAME_MSI_country$§!就债务未偿还一事与您进行联系。鉴于我们也是新近崛起的太空社会中的一员，我们理解您或许无法承担全部债务。我们的目的是帮助您选择一种适当的还款计划，在不阻碍经济发展的情况下偿还所有未偿还债务。\n\n相应地，我们很高兴为您提供一名咨询顾问，免费！我们热切地希望这个问题能够顺利解决。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:271`

### English

Dear [owner.GetName],\n\nWe are contacting you on behalf of the §H$NAME_MSI_country$§! regarding an outstanding debt. Being a fledging space-faring society, we understand you may not be in a position to repay the full amount. Our aim is to aid you in selecting an appropriate repayment plan, one which will settle any outstanding debts without stifling economic growth.\n\nAccordingly, we are happy to offer you a consultation, free of charge! It is our ardent wish that this matter can be settled amicably.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:359`

## origin.4015.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4015.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们很乐意协商解决问题。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:272`

### English

We are willing to settle.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:360`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4015.b&lang=en)

我们拒绝付钱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:273`

### English

We refuse to pay.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:361`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:362`

## origin.4015.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4015.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们的族人第一次触及星辰之前，我们就知道每个人在这个宇宙都有自己要扮演的角色。有些文化渴望力量。而另一些文化追求知识。我们进化是为了提供人情服务。\n\n如果一个人从未因无私奉献而感到喜悦，那么此人错过了生命的主旨。弯腰鞠躬并不是自降身份，而是提升周围的人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:275`

### English

Even before our people first reached the stars, we understood that everyone has a role to play in this galaxy. Some cultures crave power. Others pursue knowledge. We evolved to favor service.\n\nThe one who has never felt the joy of unselfish service has missed the very theme of life. To bow down is not to be diminished. It is to uplift those around you.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:363`

### English

## origin.4016.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4016.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

根据我们先前提交的估值，你亏欠我们客户的总额等于§Y£energy£ [owner.debt_collectors_amount]能量币§!。我们非常乐意提供给你各种各样的偿还计划。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:278`

### English

According to the estimations we previously submitted, the amount you owe to our client is equal to §Y£energy£ [owner.debt_collectors_amount] Energy Credits§!. We are happy to offer you a variety of repayment plans.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:367`

## origin.4016.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4016.a&lang=en)

### 中文

支付全款。

### English

Pay the full amount.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:368`

## origin.4016.a.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4016.a.response&lang=en)

### 中文

你们想要一次结清债务的积极态度让我们感动不已，但仔细检查之后，你方提供的能量币看起来并不全是真货。想必，这只是单纯的产生了误解！让我们假装无事发生就好了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:280`

### English

We are touched by your eagerness to settle your debt, but upon close examination, the energy credits you offered do not appear to be entirely genuine. Just a simple misunderstanding, we're sure! Let's pretend this never happened.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:369`

## origin.4017.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4017.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

棒极了！很高兴我们能这么快就达成协议。我们将在§Y15年§!后回来收第一笔分期付款，相信你们对这些条款感到很满意。\n\n祝您有美好的一天！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:289`

### English

## origin.4018.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4018.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

如果你无法或不愿意支付能量币，你可以向§H$NAME_MSI_country$§!提供一定比例的劳动力储备来抵扣你的债务。你的工人将被送往指定工作场所，并获得基本食宿，直到他们成功偿还债务。\n\n值得注意的是，上述劳工不需要有完整公民权。任何具有劳动能力的都可以。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:292`

### English

If you are unable or unwilling to cover the energy cost, you may repay your debt by pledging a certain percentage of your labor pool to §H$NAME_MSI_country$§!. Your workers will be transported to their designated job sites and will be provided with basic accommodation until they have successfully repaid the debt.\n\nIt's worth noting that said workers do not need to be full citizens. Any able bodies will do.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:383`

## origin.4018.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4018.a&lang=en)

实际上，我们更愿意用能量币支付。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:293`

### English

Actually, we'd prefer to pay using energy.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:384`

### English

### English

## origin.4018.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4018.c&lang=en)

### 中文

这么说你想要奴隶？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:296`

### English

So you're asking for slaves?

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:387`

## origin.4018.c.response

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4018.c.response&lang=en)

### 中文

正确的术语是“契约仆人”。奴隶制是一种不公平的做法，依靠的是利用现存的权力结构进行剥削。我们的交易完全基于合约条款。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:297`

### English

The correct term is "indentured servants". Slavery is an unfair practice based on exploitation of pre-existing power structures. Our exchanges are based solely on the terms of contract.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:388`

## origin.4018.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4018.d&lang=en)

### 中文

我们拒绝支付。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:298`

### English

We refuse to pay.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:389`

## origin.4019.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4019.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们真的很抱歉，因为我们没能在这个问题上达成一致。你是否有机会再重新考虑一下？根据合约的条款，如果债务国不愿意或无法偿还，我们就必须代表客户收缴资产。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:300`

### English

We are truly sorry that we were unable to reach an agreement in this matter. Is there any chance you would be willing to reconsider? According to the terms of the contract, if a debtor is unwilling or unable to pay, we are required to seize assets on behalf of our client.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:392`

## origin.4019.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4019.a&lang=en)

### English

## origin.4020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

收债人的舰队被迫撤退。他们毫无疑问还会回来的，但至少目前我们安全了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:306`

### English

The debt collector fleet has been forced to retreat. They will doubtlessly return, but for now, we are safe.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:399`

## origin.4020.desc.repeat

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4020.desc.repeat&lang=en)

### 中文

再一次，我们迫使收债人撤离了我们的母星系。这或许不太可能是我们最后一次和他们打交道，但至少我们的子民和财产目前又安全了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:307`

### English

Once again, we forced the debt collectors to retreat from our home system. It's unlikely we've seen the last of them, but for now, our people and our assets are safe.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:400`

## origin.4021.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4021.name&lang=en)

### 中文

收缴资产

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:309`

### English

Asset Seizure

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:403`

## origin.4021.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4021.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对此感到极度失望。\n\n作为一家专精于启发那些欠发展文明的公司，我们因我们的专业才干而自豪。但很明显，我们未能传授一些最基础的知识。你们不能无缘无故地攻击其他人，而应该为你们的失败承担责任。\n\n你们显然需要额外的指导。让我们来教你们如何偿还债务。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:310`

### English

The disappointment is heavy.\n\nAs a company specializing in the enlightenment of less developed civilizations, we pride ourselves in our expertise. Yet it has become abundantly clear that we have failed to impart some basic lessons. Rather than attacking others without cause, you alone must bear the responsibility for your failures.\n\nYou are clearly in need of additional guidance. Let us teach you how to repay a debt.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:404`

## origin.4021.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4021.a&lang=en)

我们绝不会再向你们低头！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:311`

### English

We'll never bow to you again.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:405`

## origin.4025.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4025.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们的舰队被击败后，收债人突袭了[PLANET.GetName]，绑架了我们的许多族人。比起这次战败更加令人心痛的，是他们留下的“账单”，告诉我们这些被扣押的人们将被用来偿还我们的“债务”。收债人说，我们应该心怀感恩，债务清算后我们的族人就能回家。还债时间大约只要29637年左右。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:314`

### English

After our fleet was defeated, the debt collectors raided [PLANET.GetName], kidnapping a number of our people. Even more galling than the defeat was the 'invoice' they left behind, informing us that the seized population will be put to work to repay our 'debt.' We should be grateful, the collectors say, that our people will be returned once the debt is settled. This should only take 29,637 years or so.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:409`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4025.a&lang=en)

### English

Next time we will be ready.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:410`

## origin.4030.desc.defeated

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4030.desc.defeated&lang=en)

### 中文

亲爱的[owner.GetName]，\n\n我们再次代表§H$NAME_MSI_country$§!就债务未偿一事与你联系。我们只希望和平解决此事，同时希望贵方更愿意参与建设性的对话。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:324`

### English

Dear [owner.GetName],\n\nOnce again we're contacting you on behalf of the §H$NAME_MSI_country$§! regarding an outstanding debt. We only wish to resolve this matter peacefully, and hope that you are more willing to participate in a constructive dialogue.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:420`

## origin.4030.desc.furious

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4030.desc.furious&lang=en)

### 中文

亲爱的[owner.GetName]，\n\n这是我们的最后警告：你欠§H$NAME_MSI_country$§!的债务亟需偿还。尽管我们多次试图和平解决此事，你始终没有表达出合作的意愿。如果你再次拒绝执行偿还计划，我们将别无选择，只能采取所有必要的手段收缴你的资产。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:325`

### English

Dear [owner.GetName],\n\nThis is our last warning: the debt you owe to the §H$NAME_MSI_country$§! urgently needs to be repaid. Despite multiple attempts to resolve this matter peacefully, you have consistently failed to cooperate. If you refuse once again to agree on a repayment plan, we will have no choice but to seize your assets by any means necessary.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:421`

## origin.4031.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4031.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

感谢你的及时回应，以及你公平地解决此事的意愿！现在未偿还款项总额为§Y[owner.debt_collectors_amount]§!£energy£§H$energy$§!。我们将在15年后再回来，收下一笔分期款项。祝您愉快！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:337`

### English

Thank you for your prompt response and your willingness to settle the matter equitably! The outstanding sum is now equal to §Y[owner.debt_collectors_amount]§! £energy£ §H$energy$§!. We will be back in 15 years to collect the next installment. Have a nice day!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:434`

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:435`

## origin.4032.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4032.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

正如这个小字条款所标明的，我们执行的是标准利率。如果你继续按现有分期方式支付，你就能在29637年后偿清你的债务。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:340`

### English

As indicated in the small print, standard interest rates are applied. If you continue with your payments at the current rate, you will be able to repay your debt in exactly 29,637 years.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:438`

### English

## origin.4036.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4036.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

我们很遗憾你们没能在商定时间内筹措到足够的款项。我们已经调整了你们的还款计划，减少了下次分期偿还的所需金额。\n\n不幸的是，由于你方无法偿还债务，造成了违约。与之对应的，我们不得不扣押你们的一部分族人以代替资金。我们希望这么做不会造成不合时宜的不便。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:343`

### English

We are saddened to learn you weren't able to collect the agreed amount in time. We have adjusted your repayment plan, halving the required sum for the next installment.\n\nUnfortunately, your inability to pay puts you in breach of our contract. Accordingly, we will now seize a segment of your population in lieu of funds. We hope does not cause undue inconvenience.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:442`

## origin.4036.desc.energy

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4036.desc.energy&lang=en)

### 中文

$origin.4036.desc.base$\n\n考虑当下情况，我们有必要告知您，我们的客户也接受以工作时长为形式的偿付。你们的一些人口将会被运送到指定的工作地点，提供基础食宿，直到他们成功地偿还了债务。\n\n值得注意的是，上述劳工不需要有完整公民权。任何具有劳动能力的都可以。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:344`

### English

$origin.4036.desc.base$\n\nConsidering the situation, we feel compelled to inform you that our client also accepts repayment in the form of dedicated work hours. Some of your population will be transported to their designated job sites and provided with basic accommodation until they have successfully repaid the debt.\n\nIt's worth noting that these workers need not be full citizens of yours. Any able bodies will do.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:443`

## origin.4036.desc.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4036.desc.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不会让你抓走我们的人民。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:345`

### English

We won't let you take our people.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:444`

## origin.4036.desc.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4036.desc.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

收债人会攻击我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:346`

### English

The debt collectors will attack us.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:445`

## origin.4037.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4037.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

我们恳请你重新考虑一下你的决定。如果你拒绝付款，这将会在你的信用分数上留下一个永久的印记。拖欠债务会限制你未来的经济机遇，对于你自己的形象来说没有任何好处。你希望世人将你们当作这样的一个文明吗？通过唾弃那些愿意自降身份提升你们的人？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:348`

### English

We beg you to rethink your decision. Your refusal to pay will leave a permanent mark on your credit score. Defaulting will limit your financial opportunities in the future, to say nothing of your own self-image. Is this how you wish to be known as a society? By spitting on those who kneeled to lift you up?

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:448`

## origin.4037.desc.defeated

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4037.desc.defeated&lang=en)

### 中文

我们意识到你方拒绝偿还债务是一件令人十分担忧的事情。我们的客户，§H$NAME_MSI_country$§!，提供给你的全是帮助和支持，所求的仅仅只是一笔公平的款项作为交换。难道你们学到的东西就是这些？用友善温和换来的竟是阴险欺诈？ 

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:349`

### English

We find the refusal to repay your debt deeply concerning. Our client, the §H$NAME_MSI_country$§!, offered you nothing but help and support, and only asked for a fair repayment in exchange. Is this all you have learned? To reciprocate kindness with deceit? 

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:449`

## origin.4040.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4040.name&lang=en)

### 中文

永远负债

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:351`

### English

Forever Indebted

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:452`

## origin.4040.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4040.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

收债人离开了我们的母星系，但一股深深的恐惧感至今依然在我们心中挥之不去。考虑到他们对于雇主尽心尽力，他们肯定不会停止对我们的骚扰。即使我们以某种方式偿还了全部债务，他们一定也会想出其它办法——利率、法律漏洞、隐藏在文档中的小字条款，藏在我们稀里糊涂签下的文件中。\n\n如果我们无法回购自由，我们别无选择，只能为此而战。唯一的问题是该怎么做。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:352`

### English

The debt collectors departed from our home system, but a deep sense of dread still linger. Given their dedication to their employer, they will never stop harassing us. Even if we somehow manage to repay the full sum, they will come up with something else - interest rates, legal loopholes, small print hidden in documents we don't remember signing.\n\nIf we can't buy back our freedom, we have no choice but to fight for it. The only question is how.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:453`

## origin.4041.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4041.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们击败了他们一次，我们就能再次这么做。但这并非易事——第一次，他们没有料到我们会还手。现在，他们将动用舰队的全部军力来攻击我们。要赢得一线机会，我们必须争取时间，做好准备。这是一场我们输不起的战斗。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:359`

### English

We defeated them once, and we can do it again. But it won't be easy - the first time, they weren't expecting for us to fight back. Now, they will attack us with the full might of their fleet. To have any chance, we must bide our time and prepare ourselves. This is a battle we cannot afford to lose.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:461`

### English

## origin.4042.desc.galcom

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4042.desc.galcom&lang=en)

### 中文

$NAME_MSI_country$对我们的所作所为或许是合法的，但这些事情在道德上站不住脚。为了防止类似的事情再次发生，我们需要强硬的法律来让他们负责。通过谨慎的外交，我们可以尝试破坏维系他们帝国的原则：贪婪、剥削、奴役。如果我们幸运的话，我们可以不费一刀一枪就将$NAME_MSI_country_initials$瓦解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:364`

### English

What the $NAME_MSI_country$ has done to us may have been legal, but it was far from moral. To prevent this from ever happening again, we need strong laws to hold them accountable. Through careful diplomacy, we can attempt to undermine the very principles that hold their empire together: greed, exploitation, slavery. With luck, we can destroy $NAME_MSI_country_initials$ without ever firing a shot.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:467`

## origin.4042.desc.nogalcom

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4042.desc.nogalcom&lang=en)

### 中文

$origin.4042.desc.galcom$\n\n如果存在某个管理主体，我们就可以和想法类似的国家共同商讨这项事务……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:365`

### English

$origin.4042.desc.galcom$\n\nIf only there was some sort of governing body we could join to discuss this matter with like-minded empires...

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:468`

## origin.4042.desc.nogalcommember

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4042.desc.nogalcommember&lang=en)

### 中文

$origin.4042.desc.galcom$\n\n是时候让我们加入星海共同体了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:366`

### English

$origin.4042.desc.galcom$\n\nIt's time for us to join the Galactic Community.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:469`

## origin.4050.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4050.name&lang=en)

### 中文

无械之战

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:370`

### English

A War Without Weapons

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:474`

## origin.4050.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4050.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这是我们的新战场：参议院，一个通过选举投票和辩论来解决冲突的战区。\n\n这是我们的武器：外交支持、大使、我们经济的力量以及我们愿景的纯洁。\n\n我们的目标：禁止启蒙行为，阻止像$NAME_MSI_country_initials$这样的帝国剥削较弱的文明。\n\n这就是我们获胜的地方。不是用暴力，而是用法律那不可动摇的力量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:371`

### English

Here is our new battlefield: the Senate floor, a war zone where conflicts are solved through vote-casting and debate.\n\nHere are our weapons: favors, ambassadors, the strength of our economy and the purity of our vision.\n\nHere is our goal: to ban enlightenment practices, preventing empires like the $NAME_MSI_country_initials$ from exploiting weaker civilizations.\n\nHere is where we win. Not with violence, but through the immutable power of the law.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:475`

### English

## origin.4050.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4050.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

我们将工作重心放在通过§H$resolution_pre_ftl_stances_equal_standing$§!上，阻止帝国对前超光速文明进行剥削。为了确保法律执行，我们还需要支持所有§H4种制裁类型§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:373`

### English

We'll focus our efforts on passing the §H$resolution_pre_ftl_stances_equal_standing$§!, preventing empires from exploiting pre-FTLs. We'll also need to support all §H4 sanction types§! to enforce the law.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:477`

我们将工作重心放在通过§H$resolution_pre_ftl_stances_non_interference$§!上，防止帝国以任何方式影响前超光速社会的发展。为了确保法律执行，我们也需要通过全部§H4类制裁§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:375`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:479`

## origin.4055.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4055.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

通过艰苦奋斗和坚毅决心，我们赢得了参议院的主导权，对前超光速文明的启蒙行为进行了限制，并且对违反这类规定的帝国实施了严厉的制裁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:381`

### English

Through hard work and determination, we have dominated the Senate Floor, restricting enlightenment practices and enforcing harsh sanctions against those empires which breach them.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:487`

## origin.4055.desc.msi

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4055.desc.msi&lang=en)

### 中文

$origin.4055.intro$要想生存下来，$NAME_MSI_country_initials$必须全盘颠覆式地重新思考他们的商业战略——他们帝国内部不安定的消息已经开始传播。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:382`

### English

$origin.4055.intro$ The $NAME_MSI_country_initials$ will have to radically rethink their business strategy to survive - and news of instability within their empire have already starting to spread.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:488`

## origin.4055.desc.nomsi

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4055.desc.nomsi&lang=en)

$origin.4055.intro$$NAME_MSI_country_initials$已经消亡了——多亏了我们的辛勤运作，没有其他帝国会取代它们的地位。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:383`

### English

$origin.4055.intro$ The $NAME_MSI_country_initials$ is already gone - and thanks to our work, no other empire will ever replace them.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:489`

Corporate Newsletter

## origin.4056.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4056.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

致全体前客户的一封重要讯息：\n\n自该公司第一次踏上星空的那一天起，我们就一直致力于改善其他文明，勤勉地帮助他们实现完全的潜能。经过数年的成功，董事会决定简化我们的流程，以更好反映我们公司的价值观。\n\n相应地，我们已经暂时停止了文明启蒙活动，以便更好地将主要精力聚焦在品牌重塑上。\n\n我们将在$NAME_MSI_country$开启勇于进取的全新篇章，感谢您的继续支持！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:386`

### English

An important message to all our former clients:\n\nFrom the day this company first reached the stars, we have dedicated ourselves to the betterment of other civilizations, diligently working to help them ascend to their full potential. After many successful years, the Board of Directors has made the decision to streamline our processes in order to better reflect our company values.\n\nAccordingly, we have temporarily ceased our enlightenment activities to better focus on rebranding.\n\nAs we embark on a bold new chapter at $NAME_MSI_country$, we thank you for your continued support!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:493`

### English

### 中文

消灭债务

### English

Debt Extinguished

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:497`

## origin.4060.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4060.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

甚至在我们踏上星空之前，我们就已经知道自己并不孤单。几个世纪以来，$NAME_MSI_country$一直悬于我们的头顶上——最初作为一个导师，后来又作为一个压迫者，如今到头来成为了一位堕落的暴君。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:390`

### English

Even before we reached the stars, we knew we were not alone. For centuries, the $NAME_MSI_country$ has loomed over us - first as a mentor, then as an oppressor, and now finally as a fallen tyrant.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:498`

## origin.4060.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4060.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$origin.4060.intro$\n\n我们应该庆祝。然后，也许我们应该放下武器，最终开始休养生息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:391`

### English

$origin.4060.intro$\n\nWe should celebrate. And then, perhaps, we should lay down our weapons and finally rest.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:499`

### English

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:394`

### English

### English

## origin.4070.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4070.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$origin.4060.intro$现在$NAME_MSI_country_initials$已经不复存在、被另一个[root.species.GetHandName]摧毁了。\n\n我们的天空可能变得更加晴朗了，但我们的人民仍为永无止境的需求所占据，那便是：\n\n§R复仇。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:397`

### English

$origin.4060.intro$ Now the $NAME_MSI_country_initials$ is gone, crushed by another [root.species.GetHandName].\n\nOur skies may be clear, but our people are still possessed by an insatiable need:\n\n§RRevenge.§!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:506`

我们将继续斗争，直到全宇宙禁止对前超光速文明进行剥削。

### English

They stole our revenge. They'll pay for it.

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4070.d.tooltip&lang=en)

我们现在可以使用§H血债血偿§!宣战理由向§Y[destroyer_country.GetName]§!宣战了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:402`

### English

We can now use the §HPayback§! casus belli to declare war to the §Y[destroyer_country.GetName]§!.

## origin.4071.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4071.name&lang=en)

### 中文

债务转移

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:404`

### English

Debt Offloaded

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:514`

## origin.4071.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4071.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

复仇之后是什么呢？你已经目睹了天敌的陨落，那个应对灭亡你的天敌而负责的国家也因为你而陨落了。现在又该怎么办？我们现在能放下武器吗？我们能和平相处吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:405`

### English

What comes after revenge? What to do after witnessing the fall of your nemesis, and of the empire responsible for killing your nemesis? Can we lay down our weapons now? Can we be at peace?

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:515`

## origin.4071.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4071.a&lang=en)

### 中文

除非所有的剥削者都消亡，否则我们没法安息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:406`

### English

There will be no peace until all exploiters are gone.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:516`

## origin.4110.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4110.name&lang=en)

### 中文

共同的往日

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:409`

### English

A Shared Past

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:521`

## origin.4110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当我们第一次与§Y[bsempire.GetName]§!接触时，我们曾惊叹于他们破译我们语言的速度。我们现在理解了原因。\n\n当我们的前主人试图让我们屈服的时候，他们绑架了我们的人民。其中一些不幸的人被带上了一艘$NAME_MSI_country_initials$飞船。在孤注一掷的下，他们与船上的其他“契约奴隶”发动了兵变。他们在一颗无人居住的行星上进行了迫降，这次勇敢的反抗行为最终导致了§Y[bsempire.GetName]§!的诞生。\n\n我们拥有共同的敌人，以及共同的历史。或许，我们可以携手共建一个没有压迫的未来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:410`

### English

When we first made contact with the §Y[bsempire.GetName]§!, we marveled at the speed with which they deciphered our language. We now understand why.\n\nWhen our former masters tried to bend us, they kidnapped our people. Some of these unfortunates wound up on an $NAME_MSI_country_initials$ ship. To their lasting credit, along with the other 'indentured assets' on board, they mutinied, crashing onto an uninhabited planet. This brave act of rebellion eventually resulted in the birth of the §Y[bsempire.GetName]§!.\n\nWe have a common enemy, and a shared history. And perhaps, together we can forge a future free from oppression.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:522`

Perhaps these xenos are not entirely unlike us.

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4120.name&lang=en)

情报共享

### English

Shared Intel

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:527`

## origin.4120.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4120.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们双方的帝国有一个共同的敌人：§Y$NAME_MSI_country$§!。为了共同防范这个威胁，我们建议让他们的特工访问我们的机密情报。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:415`

### English

Our empires share a common enemy: the §Y$NAME_MSI_country$§!. To protect ourselves against this threat, we suggest giving their operatives access to our classified intel.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:528`

## origin.4120.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4120.a&lang=en)

### 中文

有价值的提议。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:416`

### English

A valuable proposal.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:529`

## origin.4120.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4120.b&lang=en)

### 中文

共同的敌人并不能让我们加深友谊。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:417`

### English

A common enemy doesn't make us friends.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:530`

## origin.4130.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4130.name&lang=en)

### 中文

相互支援

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:419`

### English

Mutual Support

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:533`

## origin.4130.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4130.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[root.GetName]，我们在参议院看到了贵方特使。团结一致，我们可以为共同的事业进行奋斗。通过协调我们双方的利益，我们可以更好地服务于共同的事业，反对奴隶制，终结对前超光速文明的干涉行为。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:420`

### English

We see your envoys on the Senate Floor, [root.GetName]. Together, we we can fight for causes that affect us both. By aligning our interests, we can make common cause against slavery and end the practice of interfering with pre-FTL empires.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:534`

## origin.4130.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4130.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我方会一直支持你方。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:421`

### English

We'll always support you.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:535`

## origin.4130.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4130.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的利益并不总是一致。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:422`

### English

Our interests don't always align.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:536`

## origin.4140.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4140.name&lang=en)

### 中文

坚强不屈

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:424`

### English

Unyielding

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:539`

## origin.4140.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4140.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们听说了你打算对$NAME_MSI_country$开战的计划。尽管我们人民拥有共同的历史，但我们并不认同你对复仇的渴望。不过，如果你对$NAME_MSI_country_initials$宣战，我们也会加入你的事业。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:425`

### English

We have heard about your plans to make war on the $NAME_MSI_country$. Although our people share a common history, we do not share your thirst for revenge. However, should you declare war against $NAME_MSI_country_initials$, we will join your cause.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:540`

## origin.4140.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4140.a&lang=en)

### 中文

那就这样吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:426`

### English

So be it.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:541`

## origin.4150.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4150.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在$NAME_MSI_country_initials$，我们将自己的客户视作家人。今天，我们不由得感到骄傲。击杀了一只星神兽！对于一个新生的太空文明，这是一个重要的里程碑。为了象征我们对你们成就的尊敬，请收下我们送来的这份小礼物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:429`

### English

At $NAME_MSI_country_initials$, we see our clients like family. Today, we can't help but feel proud. Slaying a Guardian! An important milestone for a fledgling space-faring society. As a token of our esteem for your accomplishment, please accept this small congratulatory gift from us.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:546`

## origin.4150.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4150.a&lang=en)

### 中文

谢谢你。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:430`

### English

Thank you.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:547`

## origin.4150.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4150.b&lang=en)

### 中文

留着你的礼物吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:431`

### English

Keep your gifts.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:548`

## origin.4160.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4160.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

多年前，我们给了你们一条通向天空的飞梯。今天，我们目睹了你们对一个低等文明提供了同样的帮助。\n\n我们相信你们能把我们曾经的教导传授给他们。当然，如果你们需要帮助来起草一项有利可图的启蒙策略，你完全可以向我们咨询。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:433`

### English

Years ago, we gave you a ladder to the sky. Today, we have witnessed you offer a lesser civilization the same help.\n\nWe trust you will be able to pass on our lessons to them. Of course, should you need any help in drafting a profitable enlightenment strategy, you have only to ask.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:551`

## origin.4160.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4160.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以运用你的帮助。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:434`

### English

We could use your help.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:552`

## origin.4160.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4160.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们和你们不一样。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:435`

### English

We are not like you.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:553`

## origin.4170.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.4170.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

亲爱的前客户，\n\n我们听说了你最近的经济困境。学习如何管理你的金融是运营一个帝国的必要部分，有些时候，失败也是学习的至关重要的一部分。\n\n我们很乐意为你提供必须的资源，来让你的国家摆脱赤字。当然，出借的资源也将纳入你未偿还债务的一部分。我们希望这点小小的金融教训能对你有所帮助。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:437`

### English

Dear former clients,\n\nWe heard about your recent economic troubles. Learning to manage your finances is a necessary part of running an empire, and sometimes, failure is an essential part of learning.\n\nWe are happy to offer you the necessary resources to put your country out of deficit. Of course, the cost loaned resources will be added to your outstanding debt. We hope this little financial lesson can be of help.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:556`

## origin.4170.a
