## origin_broken_shackles_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin_broken_shackles_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$CHALLENGING_ORIGIN$\n这个文明始于一艘奴隶船上的发起的兵变，现在由多种多样的前超光速物种组成。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:444`

### English

$CHALLENGING_ORIGIN$\nThis civilization began as a mutiny aboard a slaver vessel, and is made up of a diverse group of pre-FTL species.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:565`

## START_SCREEN_BROKEN_SHACKLES

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTART_SCREEN_BROKEN_SHACKLES&lang=en)

### 中文

终于自由了！\n\n有些人生来便负着枷锁。还有的负债累累，然后被作为契约奴隶任人买卖。但前主人的科技并不能保护他们周全。我们密谋反叛。在一场短暂但血腥的斗争中，我们杀死了俘虏我们的人，也重创了他们的运奴船。我们不得已紧急迫降到[Root.Capital.GetName]的表面上。\n\n尽管我们来自五湖四海和不同的物种，但我们找到了共同的目标。假以时日，这艘运奴船受损的系统就能修好，藏在其中的大量秘密-包括我们曾经母星的位置-也能被揭晓。\n\n我们已挣脱枷锁。现在就开始劳动吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:445`

### English

Free at last!\n\nSome were born into bondage. Others, crippled by debt, found themselves sold as indentured labor. But the technology of our former masters was not enough to save them. In secret, we planned our mutiny. During the brief, bloody struggle, our captors were killed, and their slave ship critically damaged. An emergency landing left us stranded on the surface of [Root.Capital.GetName].\n\nThough we are made up of species from many different worlds, we have found common cause. In time, the damaged systems of the slaver vessel will be repaired, and its bounty of secrets - including the location of our former home worlds - will be revealed.\n\nOur shackles are broken. Now the work begins.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:566`

## START_SCREEN_BROKEN_SHACKLES_MEGACORP

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTART_SCREEN_BROKEN_SHACKLES_MEGACORP&lang=en)

### 中文

终于自由*了！\n\n有些人生来便负着枷锁。还有的负债累累，然后被作为契约奴隶任人买卖。然而我们都畅想着，能够过上轻松而富足的生活。我们密谋反叛。在一场短暂但血腥的斗争中，我们杀死了俘虏我们的人，也重创了他们的运奴船。我们不得已紧急迫降到[Root.Capital.GetName]的表面上。\n\n尽管我们来自五湖四海和不同的物种，但在您的领导下我们找到了共同的目标。我们的前主人也许非常残酷，但他们懂得经营生活。我们应该像他们一样坚定地追求我们的利益。假以时日，这艘运奴船受损的系统就能修好，藏在其中的大量秘密-包括我们曾经母星的位置-也能被揭晓。我们已挣脱枷锁。是时候成立公司了。\n\n*法律告示：\n此处使用“自由”一词并不代表本公司将提供任何折扣或进行任何影响[Root.GetName]股东收益的活动。如对此或其他术语有任何疑问，或是希望退出[Root.Capital.GetName]上的持续存在，请在[Root.Capital.GetName]的$building_bureaucratic_1$联系我们的法律或安保专家，以获取他们的快速处理。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:446`

### English

Free* at last!\n\nSome were born into bondage. Others, crippled by debt, found themselves sold as indentured labor. All of us dreamed of something more, a life of ease and abundance. In secret, we planned our mutiny. During the brief, bloody struggle, our captors were killed, and their slave ship critically damaged. An emergency landing left us stranded on the surface of [Root.Capital.GetName].\n\nThough we are made up of species from many different worlds, under your directorship we have found common cause. Our former masters may have been cruel, but they understood the business of life. Like them, we must be steadfast in our pursuit of profit. In time, the damaged systems of the slaver vessel will be repaired, and its bounty of secrets - including the location of our former home worlds - will be revealed. Our shackles are broken. Now is the time to incorporate.\n\n*Legal Notice:\nThe use of the word 'free' is non-binding and does not oblige the Corporation to provide any discount or volunteer any action that could affect the profit margins of [Root.GetName] shareholders. If you have any questions regarding this or other terms, or should you wish to opt out of continued existence on [Root.Capital.GetName], please contact our legal or security professionals in the $building_bureaucratic_1$ on [Root.Capital.GetName] for immediate resolution.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:567`

## origin.111.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.111.name&lang=en)

### 中文

迈入未来

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:456`

### English

Into the Future

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:582`

## origin.111.1.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.111.1.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

自我们宣布夺取自由以来，我们已经历经了许多磨难。那艘载运我们抵达此地的舰船已经被我们发掘出了一切有价值的东西，现在它已经没有什么秘密可言了。\n\n但是，我们决不能忘记过去的牺牲，更不能忘记挣脱镣铐的代价。坠毁的运奴船将被改造成一个公共纪念馆，以纪念我们国家的诞生。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:457`

### English

We have come a long way since claiming our freedom. The ship we arrived on has been stripped of everything of value, and our technology has advanced to the point that there are few secrets remaining.\n\nBut we must never forget the sacrifices of the past, nor the cost of casting off our shackles. The crashed slaver ship will be converted into a public memorial, commemorating the birth of our nation.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:583`

## origin.111.2.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.111.2.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

自我们抵达此地以来，我们已经经历了许多磨难。尽管我们失去了对那艘载运我们到达此地的飞船的控制权，那艘船上遗留的东西不再对我们有价值，我们的科技也进步到了空前的阶段，那里对我们来说已经不再有什么秘密了。\n\n如果我们有一天将其复原，我们应当把这个地方改成一个纪念馆，以便我们在勇敢地走向未来时，还能记住我们卑微的开始。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:458`

### English

We have come a long way since our arrival. While we no longer control it, whatever remains on the ship we arrived on is no longer of value to us, and our technology has advanced to the point that there are few secrets remaining.\n\nIf we ever recover it, we shall convert the site into a memorial so we remember our humble beginnings as we move boldly into the future.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:584`

## origin.5000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5000.name&lang=en)

### 中文

归乡

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:463`

### English

Homecoming

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:592`

## origin.5000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]……终于，我们回来了。\n\n我们的民族曾经历了难以想象的苦难。从家园中被掳走，被迫拷上脚镣，或是成为任人买卖的契约财产，往往我们要面对的就是这些凶残的遭遇。即便是那些过得稍微轻松一些的人身上也带着曾被奴役的耻辱。而现在，回到我们同胞的怀抱中，我们终于可以开始治愈创伤了。\n\n但在欢乐之余我们别忘了，事实上我们是来自许多不同星球的民族。不论起源，我们中的每个人都有归乡的权利。\n\n我们的前主人不择手段地打散我们。而今天，整个星系将见证他们的失败。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:464`

### English

[From.GetName]... At last, we have returned.\n\nAs a people, we have experienced unimaginable trauma. Stolen from our homes, forced into bondage, the life of an indentured asset was often one of brutal suffering. Even for those who passed their days in relative comfort, the shame of indenture lingers. Now, reunited with our kin, we can finally begin to heal.\n\nBut in our joy we must never lose sight of the fact that we are a people of many worlds. No matter our origins, every one of us deserves the chance to return home.\n\nOur former masters did their best to break us. Today the whole galaxy is witness to their failure.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:593`

## origin.5000.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5000.a&lang=en)

### 中文

属于我们人民的伟大日子。

### English

A great day for our people.

### English

## origin.5001.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5001.name&lang=en)

### 中文

找到家园

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:467`

### English

Home World Found

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:597`

## origin.5001.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5001.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]……在轨道上看，它是这么地微小。\n\n人们并不是拥有星球，而是属于这颗星球。回到这里，我们又向找回自己的过去迈进了一步。\n\n我们在前主人的手中遭受的苦难永远不能弥消。真正的疗愈需要花费几个世代。但总之，今天确实是个好日子。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:468`

### English

[From.GetName]... From orbit, it appears so small.\n\nA world does not belong to its people; it is the people who belong to their world. In returning here, we take another step towards reclaiming our past.\n\nThe trauma we experienced at the hands of our former masters can never be undone. True healing will take generations. But today is a good day.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:598`

## origin.5001.homecoming.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5001.homecoming.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]……在轨道上看，它是这么地微小。\n\n人们并不是拥有星球，而是属于这颗星球。回到这里，我们又向找回自己的过去迈进了一步。\n\n我们在前主人的手中遭受的苦难永远不能弥消。真正的疗愈需要花费几个世代。但总之，今天确实是个好日子。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:469`

### English

[From.GetName]... From orbit, it appears so small.\n\nA world does not belong to its people; it is the people who belong to their world. In returning here, we take another step towards reclaiming our past.\n\nThe trauma we experienced at the hands of our former masters can never be undone. True healing will take generations. But today is a good day.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:599`

## origin.5001.empty.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5001.empty.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]……肯定是搞错了什么。在轨道上我们无法探测到任何感性生命。所有的人口可能都被某种方式抹去了。\n\n尽管这与我们预想的归乡大相径庭，但返回这个恒星系还是让我们向着寻回自己过去迈出了一步。不幸的是，我们得独自携带者[From.GetName]前行了，只有靠我们才能传承记忆。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:470`

### English

[From.GetName]... Something is very wrong here. From orbit, we can detect no signs of sentient life. Somehow, the entire population has been eliminated.\n\nWhile this was not the homecoming we hoped for, by returning to this system we have taken another step towards reclaiming our past. Unfortunately, we alone must carry the legacy of [From.GetName]. It is up to us to remember.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:600`

## origin.5001.tomb.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5001.tomb.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]……这里发生了非常不好的事情。\n\n被炸毁的土地上尘土飞荡。大部分的表面都被辐射了，少数绝望的幸存者在废墟中坚持生活。\n\n虽然这不是我们所希望的归宿，但通过返回这个星系，我们已经向收回我们的过去的一步迈进了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:471`

### English

[From.GetName]... Something very wrong has happened here.\n\nDust drifts across a blasted landscape. Much of the surface is irradiated, and the few desperate survivors cling to life in the ruins.\n\nWhile this was not the homecoming we hoped for, by returning to this system we have taken another step towards reclaiming our past.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:601`

## origin.5001.uplifted.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5001.uplifted.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]……自从我们被掳走以来，这里发生了蓬勃的发展。他们不再被局限于单一行星上，而是已经迈向了繁星的征途。尽管这与我们预想的归乡大相径庭，但返[From.GetName]上的科技进步属实卓越。\n\n而我们则已经没有了归处，我们被偷走的过去再也找不回来。我们现在能做的只有朝前看。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:472`

### English

[From.GetName]... Since we were taken from our home, much has transpired here. No longer bound to a single planet, the population has turned towards the stars. While this may not be the homecoming we expected, the technological progress on [From.GetName] is remarkable.\n\nFor us, there is no going back; our stolen past can never be reclaimed. All we can do now is look to the future.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:602`

## origin.5001.conquered.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5001.conquered.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]……自从我们被掳走以来，这里发生了很多事情。我们的这些同胞已经失去了主权，被另一个星系强权所统治了。\n\n而我们则已经没有了归处，我们被偷走的过去再也找不回来。我们现在能做的只有朝前看。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:473`

### English

[From.GetName]... Since we were taken from our home, much has transpired here. Far from a sovereign people, our kin have been claimed by another galactic power.\n\nFor us, there is no going back; our stolen past can never be reclaimed. All we can do now is look to the future.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:603`

## origin.5002.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5002.name&lang=en)

### 中文

思乡之情

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:490`

### English

Homesick

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:621`

## origin.5002.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5002.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

挣脱枷锁后，我们走出了成为一个民族的第一步。然而，在返回我们曾经的母星后，共同的目标开始发生动摇。\n\n大众媒体充斥着对$NAME_MSI_country$到来前我们被母星上“返璞归真生活”的高度浪漫化的描述。而有许多人已经回到了母星。\n\n如果我们不加以晶体，这可能会让我们的社会发生严重的动荡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:491`

### English

In casting off our shackles, we took the first step towards becoming a people. However, after returning to our former home world that sense of shared purpose has begun to wane.\n\nPopular media is awash with highly romanticized depictions of the 'simple life' enjoyed on our former home world before the arrival of $NAME_MSI_country$. Many have already returned.\n\nIf we are not careful, this could have a seriously destabilizing effect on our society.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:622`

## origin.5002.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5002.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们再也担负不起任何一名国民的损失了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:492`

### English

We cannot afford to lose any more of our people.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:623`

## origin.5002.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5002.b&lang=en)

自由迁徙是一项基本权利。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:493`

### English

Freedom of movement is a fundamental right.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:624`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5002.b.tt&lang=en)

§Y[broken_shackles_homeworld_owner.GetName]§!：\n$t$将感知设置为 §Y100.00。§!\n$t$§G+50%§! §Y每月科技进步§!持续 §Y5 年§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:494`

### English

§Y[broken_shackles_homeworld_owner.GetName]§!:\n$t$Sets Awareness to §Y100.00.§!\n$t$§G+50%§! §YMonthly Technological Progression§! for §Y5 Years§!.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:625`

§R5%§!不具备§H平等主义§!思潮的人口将向该思潮转化。

§R5%§! of your pop groups without the §HEgalitarian§! ethic will drift toward it.

## origin.5003.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5003.name&lang=en)

### 中文

归乡者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:496`

### English

The Returners

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:628`

## origin.5003.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5003.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个新的群众运动已经在[Root.Capital.GetName]上站稳脚跟。这些极端分子自称为“归乡者”，主张我们应该将全体人民送回他们的起源行星，同时令国家解体。安全部队已将警戒状态提高到高级别，任何人数超过100人的公共集会都被禁止。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:497`

### English

A new movement has taken root on [Root.Capital.GetName]. Calling themselves The Returners, these extremists are advocating for the wholesale return of our people to their planets of origin, as well as the dissolution of our empire. Security forces have been placed on high alert, and all public gatherings of more than 100 individuals banned.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:629`

## origin.5003.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5003.a&lang=en)

### 中文

短视的蠢货。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:498`

### English

Shortsighted fools.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:630`

## origin.5004.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5004.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.Capital.GetName]上的暴乱

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:499`

### English

Riots on [Root.Capital.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:632`

## origin.5004.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5004.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

归乡者们正在煽动反政府情绪，呼吁我们帝国的各物种落叶归根，回到自己的故土。该运动中最极端的派系已经和亲政府军队之间爆发了战斗。据报道，几个主要的城市中心已经遭到了严重的破坏。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:500`

### English

Stoking anti-government sentiment, The Returners are calling on the various species in our empire to return to their roots. Fighting has broken out between the movement's most extreme factions and pro-government forces. Severe damage has been reported in several major urban centers.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:633`

## origin.5004.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5004.a&lang=en)

### 中文

宣布戒严

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:501`

### English

Declare Martial Law

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:634`

## origin.5005.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5005.name&lang=en)

### 中文

齐心协力

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:502`

### English

Unity of Purpose

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:636`

## origin.5005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然为了禁止人民返回他们各自的家园星球，我们付出了一定的代价，但事实最终证明了这个决策是正确的。近期，我们目睹社会凝聚力得到了明显的改善，同时，人们对于[Root.Capital.GetName]才是我们的家园这一观点有了崭新的共识。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:503`

### English

While forbidding our people to return to their respective home worlds has not been without its consequences, in the end it has proven the correct decision. Recently, we have witnessed a marked improvement in social cohesion, as well as a newfound sense that [Root.Capital.GetName] is indeed our home.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:637`

## origin.5005.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5005.a&lang=en)

### 中文

只有强人的手腕才能铸造国家。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:504`

### English

Only a strong hand can forge a nation.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:638`

## origin.5006.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5006.name&lang=en)

### 中文

人才流失

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:505`

### English

Brain Drain

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:640`

## origin.5006.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5006.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然允许人们移民到我们曾经的家园星球在过去是一项群众喜闻乐见的政策，但也有相应的后果。最近，我们经历了一次重大的“人才流失”，因为一些最聪明的人才选择了迁往别处。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:506`

### English

While permitting emigration to our former home worlds has proven a popular policy, it has not been consequence-free. Recently, we have experienced a significant 'brain drain', as some of some of our brightest minds have made the choice to relocate.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:641`

## origin.5006.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5006.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们必须避免人才进一步的流失。

We must prevent any further loss of talent.

## origin.5007.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5007.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

允许我们的人民返回自己的母星，作出这样的决策并非没有代价。但是，自由迁徙的政策推动了思想枷锁的解除。新的想法正在蓬勃发展，科学技术的进步已成为常态，而非一时的例外。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:510`

### English

The decision to allow our people to return to their home worlds was not without its costs, but with freedom of movement has come an unshackling in thought; new ideas are flourishing, and advances in science and technology are the norm rather than the exception.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:646`

## origin.5008.name

相互促长

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:511`

### English

Shared Expertise

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:648`

## origin.5008.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5008.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

允许我们的人民返回自己的母星，作出这样的决策并非没有代价。但是，自由迁徙的政策推动了思想枷锁的解除。新的想法正在蓬勃发展，社会层面的革新已成为常态，而非一时的例外。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:512`

### English

The decision to allow our people to return to their home worlds was not without its costs, but with freedom of movement has come an unshackling in thought; new ideas are flourishing, and advances in society are the norm rather than the exception.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:649`

## origin.5009.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5009.name&lang=en)

### 中文

家园的芬芳

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:513`

### English

The Scent of Home

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:651`

## origin.5009.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5009.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.GetName]……我们几乎忘记了空气中的芬芳。在经历了所有的一切之后，我们现在回归了家园，一切都显得如此不真实。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:514`

### English

[From.GetName]... We had almost forgotten the scent of the air. To return now, after all we have experienced, feels almost unreal.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:652`

## origin.5010.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

星辰大海第一步

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:516`

### English

First Step to the Stars

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:656`

## origin.5010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在起义后，我们中有的人说，我们应该各奔前程了。他们坚称[Root.owner.capital_scope.GetName]不是我们的家园，我们只不过是一群由契约资产组成的乌合之众，我们面临的困难太过艰巨不可战胜。\n\n但我们第一座空间站的竣工，昭示着自由民能够完成伟大的壮举。只有想不到，没有做不到。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:517`

### English

After the mutiny, there were some among us who said we should go our separate ways. They argued that [Root.owner.capital_scope.GetName] was not our home, that we were nothing but a motley band of indentured assets, and that the challenges before us were too great to overcome.\n\nBut with the building of our first Outpost, we've shown exactly what a free people can accomplish. The only limit is our own imagination.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:657`

## origin.5010.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5010.a&lang=en)

### 中文

向繁星进发。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:518`

### English

Onward, to the stars.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:658`

## origin.5011.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5011.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在摆脱桎梏的斗争历程中，我们迈出了成为一个民族的第一步。然而，近年来，大众媒体充斥着对我们以前在家园星球所享受的“朴素生活”的叙述，言语之间尽是浪漫美化。许多人已经吵着要回去。\n\n如果我们不小心谨慎，这场运动将严重影响我们社会秩序的稳定。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:520`

### English

In casting off our shackles, we took the first step towards becoming a people. However, in recent years, popular media is awash with highly romanticized depictions of the 'simple life' enjoyed on our former home world. Many are already clamoring to return.\n\nIf we are not careful, this movement could have a seriously destabilizing effect on our society.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:662`

## origin.5041.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5041.name&lang=en)

### 中文

脱枷之后的政治局面

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:522`

### English

Unshackled Politics

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:666`

## origin.5041.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5041.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

团结一致反对我们以前的奴隶主很容易；有了一个共同的敌人，我们就能像拧成一股绳一样抗争和战斗。但随着自由的到来，人民内部的裂痕开始出现。\n\n我们是来自不同星球的民族聚集体，我们的政治也同样多元。新的派系正在组建起来。肯定也会出现未来更多的派系。作为一个民族，我们能否妥善处理这些差异，将决定我们的命运。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:523`

### English

To unite against our former masters was easy; with a common enemy, we struggled and fought as one. But with freedom, cracks have started to appear.\n\nWe are a people of many worlds, and our politics are no less diverse. New factions are being founded. More will surely follow. As a people, how well we navigate these differences will determine our fate.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:667`

## origin.5041.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5041.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

新的派系将会成立。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:524`

### English

New factions will be formed.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:668`

## origin.5046.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5046.name&lang=en)

### 中文

自由的代价

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:525`

### English

The Cost of Freedom

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:670`

## origin.5046.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5046.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们是一个拥有众多星球的民族，随着时间的推移，我们已经认识到多样性的宝贵价值。然而，要让几种不同的文化间保持平衡并非易事，更不要说完全不同的物种在生理上的需求也各不相同。我们之间隔阂渐生，尽管到现在为止，我们依然想方设法驾驭住了不断变化的政治格局，但我们不能对社会中固有的分歧视而不见了。\n\n现在许多人呼吁成立一个特别委员会，来调和这些改革的呼声。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:526`

### English

We are a people of many worlds, and over time we have learned the value of diversity. However, negotiating the dynamics of several different cultures - not to mention the physical needs of completely distinct species - has not been easy. Our differences run deep, and while we have managed to navigate the shifting political landscape up to now, the divisions inherent in our society have grown impossible to ignore.\n\nMany are now calling for a special committee to be formed to moderate the calls for reform.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:671`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5046.a&lang=en)

### English

### English

## COST_OF_FREEDOM_PROJECT

自由的代价

### English

The Cost of Freedom

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:678`

## COST_OF_FREEDOM_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ACOST_OF_FREEDOM_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的社会存在着严重的割裂。将许多星球和许多不同的文化统合在一起，免不了会滋生矛盾。希望本委员会能够为这个问题提供解决方案。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:534`

### English

Our society is deeply divided. A union of many worlds, and many different cultures, does not come without tension. It is to be hoped that this committee can offer solutions to the problem.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:679`

## origin.5047.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5047.name&lang=en)

### 中文

赞不绝口的思辨

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:535`

### English

Complimentary Thinking

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:681`

## origin.5047.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5047.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当多样化的思想聚集在一起时，难以预料他们能够实现什么样的成就；我们的研究团队增加了一名新晋的、非[Root.GetSpeciesName]成员，此人已经取得了一些新的研究成果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:536`

### English

When diverse minds come together, there's no telling what they can accomplish; the addition of a new, non [Root.GetSpeciesName] member to our research team is already producing results.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:682`

## origin.5049.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5049.name&lang=en)

### 中文

多元设计

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:537`

### English

Diverse Design

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:684`

## origin.5049.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5049.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

作为一个起源于众多不同星球的民族，整合全体人民绝非易事。然而，我们多元化的艺术传统经过融合，产生了令人难以置信的创造力的源泉，广受好评的新产品层出不穷。无数艺术家和设计师涌现出来，他们都受到多元化的启发，不断拓展表现手法的前沿。国内相关产业得到空前的蓬勃发展，我们的人民从未像现在这样投身于这些事业。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:538`

### English

As a people of many worlds, integration has not been without its challenges. However, the synthesis of our diverse artistic traditions has resulted in an incredible amount of creative energy. New products are being promoted. Countless artists and designers have emerged, all inspired to push the boundaries of expression. Domestic industry is thriving, and our people have never been more engaged.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:685`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aresolution_proposed_intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在星海共同体的联系人向我们传递了消息：正在制定相关法律法规，意在禁止

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:542`

### English

Word has reached us from our contacts at the Galactic Community: legislation is in the works that would ban

## origin.5094.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5094.name&lang=en)

### 中文

提议星海议案

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:543`

### English

Galactic Resolution Proposed

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:691`

## origin.5094.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5094.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$resolution_proposed_intro$全星系范围内的奴隶劳工和奴隶牲口制度。如果我们真心想要支持自由，那么就必须旗帜鲜明地站在改革的一方。如果我们没能作出行动，我们的人民会怎么想？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:544`

### English

$resolution_proposed_intro$ chattel and livestock slavery throughout the galaxy. If we truly profess to favor freedom, we must stand on the side of change. What will our people think, if we fail to act?

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:692`

## origin.5095.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5095.name&lang=en)

### 中文

绝大多数的奴隶制度被取缔

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:545`

### English

Most Forms of Slavery Outlawed

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:694`

## origin.5095.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5095.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

今天对于星海共同体来说是伟大的一天，各成员国投票决定将绝大多数形式的奴隶制裁定为非法。星系的人们再也不需要担心被下作的暴徒和唯利是图的公司压迫了。尽管在确保所有感性生命体的自由权利方面，我们还有很长的路要走，但此次裁定代表了重要的第一步。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:546`

### English

A great day at the Galactic Community, where members voted to outlaw most forms of slavery. No longer will the people of the galaxy need to fear oppression at the hands of petty thugs and profit-hungry corporations. Although there is a long way to go before the right of all sentient beings to freedom is ensured, this represents an important first step.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:695`

## origin.5095.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5095.a&lang=en)

### 中文

开明的思想取得了胜利。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:547`

### English

Enlightened minds prevail.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:696`

## slavery_allowed_outro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aslavery_allowed_outro&lang=en)

### 中文

由于压迫性政权和剥削性公司以恶毒的手段进行了阻挠，在可预见的未来，这种可憎的做法将继续下去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:548`

### English

Due to the malign presence of oppressive regimes and exploitative corporations, this detestable practice will continue for the foreseeable future.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:698`

## origin.5100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5100.name&lang=en)

### 中文

奴隶制获得默许

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:549`

### English

Slavery Receives Tacit Approval

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:699`

## origin.5100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

今天对于星海共同体来说是一个令人心碎的日子，取缔绝大多数形式的奴隶制的动议未能获得足够的票数。$slavery_allowed_outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:550`

### English

A heartbreaking day at the Galactic Community, where a motion to outlaw most forms of slavery failed to gain enough votes. $slavery_allowed_outro$

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:700`

## origin.5100.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5100.a&lang=en)

### 中文

野蛮的暴徒。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:551`

### English

Barbarous thugs.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:701`

## origin.5104.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5104.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$resolution_proposed_intro$绝大多数形式的奴隶制，包括可憎的契约式奴役。如果我们自己的斗争经验能教会我们什么，那就是如果不发声诉求，权力是不会让步的。一个地方一旦存在奴隶制，就不可能有正义。我们必须与星系中其他开明的成员国站在一起。只有这样做，我们才能保证我们人民的安全和尊严。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:552`

### English

$resolution_proposed_intro$ most forms of slavery, including the abomination of indentured servitude. If our own experience has taught us anything, it is that power concedes nothing without a demand. Where slavery exists, there can be no justice. We must stand together with the other enlightened members of the galaxy. Only by doing so can we guarantee the safety and dignity of our people.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:703`

## origin.5105.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5105.name&lang=en)

### 中文

奴隶制被禁

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:553`

### English

Slavery Banned

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:705`

## origin.5105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

今天对于星海共同体来说是值得载入史册的重要日子，各成员国投票决定禁止一切形式的奴隶制——甚至包括契约奴役这样的卑劣做法。像$NAME_MSI_country$这样的公司将不再能够以利润的名义剥削无辜者。终于，星系历史上最黑暗的篇章之一可以结束了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:554`

### English

A monumental day at the Galactic Community, where members voted to ban all forms of slavery - including the vile practice of indentured servitude. No longer will corporations like $NAME_MSI_country$ be able to exploit the innocent in the name of profit. At last, one of the darkest chapters in galactic history can finally come to a close.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:706`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5110.name&lang=en)

奴隶制存续

### English

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

星海共同体传来令人心碎的消息，一场取缔一切形式的奴隶制的运动失败了。$slavery_allowed_outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:556`

### English

Heartbreak at the Galactic Community, where a motion to outlaw all forms of slavery has failed. $slavery_allowed_outro$

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:709`

## origin.5110.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5110.a&lang=en)

### 中文

沉痛的消息。

### English

Hard news.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:710`

## origin.5114.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5114.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$resolution_proposed_intro$对有机体的奴役，而且是全方位的。对于我们来说，这件事没有妥协的余地。奴隶制是星系篇章上的污点。我们必须尽一切所能，彻底地将其终结。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:558`

### English

$resolution_proposed_intro$ the enslavement of all organic populations. For us, there can be no compromise. Slavery is a stain upon the galaxy. We must do what we can to end it permanently.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:712`

## origin.5115.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5115.name&lang=en)

### 中文

有机体奴隶贸易叫停

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:559`

### English

Organic Slave Trade Halted

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:714`

## origin.5115.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5115.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

今天是泛星系贸易市场的重要一日，我们决定将有机体奴役裁定为非法。人民再也不需要担心被下作的暴徒和唯利是图的公司压迫了。今晚我们都可以睡个好觉了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:560`

### English

A monumental day at the Galactic Market, which made the decision today to outlaw the enslavement of organic populations. No longer will the people of the galaxy need to fear oppression at the hands of petty thugs and profit-hungry corporations. We can all rest easier tonight.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:715`

## origin.5115.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5115.a&lang=en)

全人民的胜利。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:561`

### English

A victory for all people.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:716`

有机体奴隶贸易存续

### English

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5120.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

泛星系贸易市场的行动受到了挫折，各成员国决定继续放任邪恶的奴隶制度下的各类行径。$slavery_allowed_outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:563`

### English

Frustration at the Galactic Market, as members agreed to continue the vile practice of slavery. $slavery_allowed_outro$

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:719`

## origin.5120.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5120.a&lang=en)

### English

Puerile capitalists.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:720`

## origin.5124.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5124.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$resolution_proposed_intro$对智慧生命体的奴役，而且是全方位的。无论是哪个物种，一旦在有思想的个体身上套上锁链，最终一定会发现锁链的另一端套在了自己身上。我们必须支持这项动议。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:565`

### English

$resolution_proposed_intro$ the slavery of all forms of sentient life. No species can put a chain on a thinking individual without at last finding the other end fastened to themselves. We must support this motion.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:722`

## origin.5125.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5125.name&lang=en)

### 中文

属于全体智慧生命的胜利

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:566`

### English

A Victory for All Sentient Beings

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:724`

## origin.5125.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5125.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

今天对于泛星系贸易市场来说是历史性的一天，各成员国投票通过废除奴隶制议案。自由成为了全体感性生命的天然权利。今日，星海历史中不光彩的一章体面地完结了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:567`

### English

A historic day at the Galactic Market, where members voted to outlaw slavery in all its forms. Freedom is the natural right of all sentient beings. Today, a vile chapter in galactic history comes to a merciful close.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:725`

## origin.5130.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5130.name&lang=en)

### 中文

奴隶制存续

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:568`

### English

Slavery Continues

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:727`

## origin.5130.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5130.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

今天是泛星系贸易市场上令人沮丧的一天，各国作出决策，决定继续出售感性的非有机体生命。再一次，权力和金钱驱使的恶行扼杀了自由的事业。只要还有一个有情感的生命体佩戴着镣铐——无论这生命是天然孕育还是人工制造——就没有人可以高枕无忧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:569`

### English

A frustrating day at the Galactic Market, where the decision was made to continue the sale of sentient non-organics. Once again, the malign influence of power and money have stifled the cause of freedom. No one - whether organic or artificial - can rest easy while even a single sentient being is forced into bondage.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:728`

## origin.5131.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5131.name&lang=en)

### 中文

禁止奴隶制被废止

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:570`

### English

Slave Ban Repealed

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:730`

## origin.5131.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5131.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

企业的势力已经成功地废除了星海共同体最近制定的反对奴隶制的议案。虽然这一切令人失望，但此等不幸的发展并不出乎意料；历史是螺旋上升的，而且螺线有时候会很长，但最终，自由的事业终将取得胜利。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:571`

### English

Corporate forces have succeeded in repealing the Galactic Community's recent resolution against slavery. While this is disappointing, unfortunately it is not unexpected; the arc of history runs long, but in the end, the cause of freedom will prevail.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:731`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5131.a&lang=en)

令人失望。

### English

Disappointing.

### English

Crashed Slaver Ship

## broken_shackles_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abroken_shackles_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

修复这艘坠毁的运奴船的电脑核心应该可以令我们找到自己母星的位置……同时也是$NAME_MSI_country$的母星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:578`

### English

Repairing the computer core of the crashed slaver ship should reveal the location of our former home worlds... as well as $NAME_MSI_country$.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:740`

## origin.5015.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5015.name&lang=en)

### 中文

舰桥

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:579`

### English

The Bridge

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:742`

## origin.5015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在起义中，将我们运往[Root.owner.capital_scope.GetName]的运奴船受损严重。在紧急迫降后，我们的第一任务是生存，除了拆解舰船获取资源以外我们几乎没有什么别的想法。但现在是时候去发掘我们前主人的秘密了。\n\n为此，首先我们要恢复舰船中央计算机的供能。我们的[engineering_shackled_species.GetAdj]工程师先是在船体的外部装配了一组太阳能电池，舰桥上的指挥控制台便可以恢复运转。但不幸的是，迫降时工程甲板的大门被卡死了，破拆防爆门进入其中可不是件容易的事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:580`

### English

During the mutiny, the slaver ship that carried us to [Root.owner.capital_scope.GetName] was badly damaged. After the crash, our first priority was survival, and other than stripping the ship for resources, we gave it little thought. But now the time has come to uncover the secrets of our former masters.\n\nTo do so, it will be necessary to restore power to the ship's central computer. As a first step, our [engineering_shackled_species.GetAdj] engineers rigged a series of solar cells to the ship's outer hull. The command consoles in the bridge are now operational. Unfortunately, the engineering deck was sealed off during the crash, and breaking through the blast doors won't be easy.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:743`

## origin.5020.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5020.name&lang=en)

### 中文

工程舱

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:581`

### English

The Engineering Bay

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:745`

## origin.5020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$NAME_MSI_country$也不是盖的：这些蓄奴的混蛋还真挺能保护自己的机密。\n\n敲开舰船的防爆门果然是一项史诗级任务。我们几乎没有设计用来进行小规模采矿作业的激光切割器。我们的团队只能是从甲板的下方挖洞才最终取得了进展。[engineering_shackled_species.GetAdj]工程师们现在已经开始加班加点恢复舰船其他部分的能量供应。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:582`

### English

Say this much for $NAME_MSI_country$: the slave-holding scum know how to safeguard their secrets.\n\nPrying open the ship's blast doors has proven a monumental task. What few laser cutters we possess were designed for low-scale mining operations. Only by tunneling up from the deck below was the team able to make any progress. [engineering_shackled_species.GetAdj] engineers are now hard at work restoring power to the rest of the ship's systems.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:746`

## origin.5025.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5025.name&lang=en)

### 中文

核心舱

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:583`

### English

The Core

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:748`

## origin.5025.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5025.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然我们的[engineering_shackled_species.GetAdj]工程师已经设法恢复了飞船计算机核心的电源，但绝大部分的硬件还是在坠毁时损坏了。尽管还是有可能修复，但这样做意味着给我们已经负担过重的科学团队带来了更大的压力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:584`

### English

While our [engineering_shackled_species.GetAdj] engineers have managed to restore power to the ship's computer core, a significant chunk of hardware was damaged in the crash. It should be possible to repair, but doing so means putting even more of a strain on our already overburdened science team.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:749`

## origin.5025.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5025.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们需要那数据。修复核心。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:585`

### English

We need that data. Repair the core.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:750`

分配研究重点

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:586`

### English

Research Allocation

修复运奴船的电脑核心给我们的工程师造成了巨大的压力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:587`

### English

Repairing the slaver's computer core is placing a significant strain on our engineers.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:752`

### 中文

合作者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:588`

### English

The Collaborator

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:754`

## origin.5030.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5030.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

得益于一些异想天开的方案，我们的[engineering_shackled_species.GetAdj]工程师们诚通地重启了受损的计算机核心。在舰船系统中引入程序化人工智能，我们就可以开启会话。总的来说，我们感觉这台机器还是愿意帮我们一把的，而不是在碍我们事。\n\n这项突破给予我们的科学家一些关于数据加密的新线索。随着我们逐渐在星系舞台上崭露头角，这些知识一定能派上用场的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:589`

### English

Thanks to some incredible, outside-the-box thinking, our [engineering_shackled_species.GetAdj] engineers have managed to restart the damaged computer core. By introducing a procedural AI to the ship's systems, we were able to engage it in dialogue. Essentially, we convinced the machine to work with, rather than against us.\n\nThe breakthrough has provided our scientists with new insights into the nature of data encryption. As we continue to make our presence felt on the galactic stage, such knowledge could prove invaluable.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:755`

## origin.5035.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5035.name&lang=en)

### 中文

数据挖掘

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:590`

### English

Data Mine

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:757`

## origin.5035.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5035.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

损坏的计算机核心已经完全修好了，尽管多数的数据是加密的，但破译工作已经开始。只要愿意花费时间和人力物力，我们迟早能获得包括我们原母星的位置在内的所有信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:591`

### English

The damaged computer core has been fully repaired, and while most of the data is encoded, decryption efforts are already underway. If we're willing to put in the time and allocate the resources, it's only a matter of time before we have access to everything - including the location of our former home worlds.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:758`

## origin.5035.desc.MSInocontact

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5035.desc.MSInocontact&lang=en)

### 中文

$origin.5035.desc$\n\n事实上，我们或许已经收集到了一些对我们有利的信息：通过对在超空间航道网中穿梭的贩奴船进行三角定位，我们已经发现了$NAME_MSI_country$的宇宙坐标。\n\n现在有两个选项摆在我们面前，直面前宗主，或是单纯祈祷上天降下惩罚。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:592`

### English

$origin.5035.desc$\n\nIn fact, we may have already learned something to our advantage: by triangulating the hyperlane dilations along the slaver ship's flight path, we have uncovered the galactic coordinates of $NAME_MSI_country$.\n\nWe now have the choice, either to confront our former masters, or leave them to their fate.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:760`

## BROKEN_SHACKLES_PROJECT_01_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ABROKEN_SHACKLES_PROJECT_01_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经完全解密了这艘坠毁殖民船中的文档，现在，我们已能够精准确定我们的母星在星系中的位置。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:594`

### English

Now that the files in the crashed slaver ship are fully decrypted, we will be able to pinpoint the galactic position of our home worlds.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:763`

## origin.5040.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5040.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

反编译坠毁殖民船数据库的特殊计划已经完成。我们找回了我们曾经的母星的位置。今日，我们大可庆祝我们在解放道路上踏出的又一步。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:596`

### English

The Special Project to decompile the databanks on the crashed slaver ship has concluded. We have uncovered the locations of our former home worlds. Today, we celebrate another step on the road to liberation.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:766`

同$NAME_MSI_country_initials$接触

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:606`

### English

Contact with $NAME_MSI_country_initials$

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:777`

## origin.5065.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5065.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这一刻如期而至：我们与§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!建立了联系。\n\n在起义后，有的人希望我们苟全性命于乱世，但显然$NAME_MSI_country$早已发现我们在星系舞台上的存在了。只有对利益的考虑可以阻止他们回来[Root.owner.capital_scope.GetName]拿回他们的“合法财产”。看来我们还不值得他们这么做。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:607`

### English

The moment we have long anticipated has arrived: we have made contact with §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!.\n\nAfter the mutiny, some hoped we could avoid detection, but it is now clear that $NAME_MSI_country$ have been aware of our presence on the galactic stage for some time. Only the pursuit of profit has kept them from returning to [Root.owner.capital_scope.GetName] to collect their 'legal assets.' It seems we weren't worth the cost of travel.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:778`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5065.a&lang=en)

### English

### English

## origin.5070.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5070.name&lang=en)

### 中文

一诺千金

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:610`

### English

Weighty Promises

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:782`

## origin.5070.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5070.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!的代表会见了我们的特使并表达的善意。但我们很明白这些允诺有多么地空洞虚伪。\n\n公司来到我们的星球，允诺带来更好的生活。他们利用花言巧语和蝇头小利打通了我们领导层的关系，然后签订了协议并获得了贷款。资本开始运作，我们市场中的商品瞬间充盈了起来。在我们搞明白发生了什么之前一切都已经结束了：我们成了$NAME_MSI_country_initials$的私有财产。\n\n这种事情不会再发生了。随着我们走向星系舞台，独立自主变得非常重要。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:611`

### English

Representatives from the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§! have approached our envoy with offers of friendship. But we know how hollow such assurances are.\n\nThe corporation first arrived on our worlds with promises of a better life. With sweet words and lavish gifts, they softened our leaders. Loans were provided; contracts signed. Investment capital flowed, and our marketplaces flooded with goods. Before we fully understood what was happening, it had already been done: we had become assets, and $NAME_MSI_country_initials$ our masters.\n\nNever again. As we push forward on the galactic stage, it's essential that we stand on our own.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:783`

## origin.5070.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5070.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不会再被愚弄了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:612`

### English

We won't be fooled again.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:784`

## origin.5075.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5075.name&lang=en)

### 中文

建立正式关系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:613`

### English

Formal Relations Established

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:786`

## origin.5075.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5075.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

经过了一系列冗长而措辞激烈的会议，我们与§H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!终于是正式建交了。然而，无论他们如何强调亲善，我们也绝不会忘记他们对我们的母星所犯下的罪行。想到这个公司还逍遥法外，肆意地像对待过去的我们一样奴役他人……我们早已出离愤怒。\n\n过去他们把我们视为财产和工具。现在我们要让他们知道自由民的厉害。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:614`

### English

After a prolonged and often intense series of meetings, diplomatic relations have been established between ourselves and §H[This.ContactCountry.GetRealName]§!. However, no matter their claims of friendship, we have not forgotten what they did to our home worlds. The thought that this corporation has been left unpunished, free to enslave others as they once did us... The emotion goes beyond words.\n\nOnce they called us property. Let us teach them the value of a free people.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:787`

## origin.5075.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5075.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可不会卑躬屈膝。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:615`

### English

We will not be bowed.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:788`

## broken_shackles_MSI_contact_02

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Abroken_shackles_MSI_contact_02&lang=en)

### 中文

绝不屈服

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:616`

### English

Unbowed

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:789`

## origin.5080.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5080.name&lang=en)

### 中文

星海共同体上的相遇

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:617`

### English

Encounter at the Galactic Community

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:791`

## origin.5080.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5080.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们派往星海共同体的代表汇报：已经与[MSI_country.GetName]建立联络。\n\n公司来到我们的星球，允诺带来更好的生活。他们利用花言巧语和蝇头小利打通了我们领导层的关系，然后签订了协议并获得了贷款。在我们搞明白发生了什么之前一切都已经结束了：我们成了他们的私有财产。必须给星海共同体的成员提个醒，千万不要上$NAME_MSI_country_initials$的当。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:618`

### English

Word from our representatives at the Galactic Community: contact has been made with [MSI_country.GetName].\n\nThe corporation first arrived on our worlds with promises of a better life. With sweet words and lavish gifts, they softened our leaders. Loans were provided; contracts signed. Before we truly understood what was happening, it had already been done: we had become assets, and they our masters. The members of the Galactic Community must be warned. $NAME_MSI_country_initials$ cannot be trusted.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:792`

## MSI_war_outro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AMSI_war_outro&lang=en)

### 中文

此战非我所愿，但我们将给它画上句号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:619`

### English

We did not seek out this war. But we will end it.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:794`

## origin.5135.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5135.name&lang=en)

### 中文

挣脱桎梏

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:620`

### English

Unshackled

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:795`

## origin.5135.attackers.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5135.attackers.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在旧主的阴影下苟且偷生的日子太久了，我们享受着这应得的自由之时，有太多的同胞还在作为契约奴隶遭受着他们的羞辱。一些人可能会将这视作侵略战争并加以谴责。不管怎样，反抗暴政的时机已到。这些星系中的流氓和利润贩子将不再被准许继续他们那卑鄙的勾当。\n\n今天，我们迈向战争。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:621`

### English

For too long we have lived in fear of our former masters, but while we enjoy the benefit of a freedom justly earned, far too many continue to suffer under the indignities of indentured servitude. Some may decry this as a war of aggression. Nevertheless, the time has come for us to strike a blow against tyranny. No longer will the petty thugs and profit-mongers of the galaxy be allowed to ply their vile trade.\n\nToday, we march to war.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:796`

## origin.5135.defenders.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5135.defenders.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$NAME_MSI_country$的糖衣炮弹没有成功，于是现在他们要来硬的了。他们的董事会认为我们软弱可欺。但解开了枷锁的我们早已如钢铁般铸成一体了。\n\n$MSI_war_outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:622`

### English

Having failed to conquer us with money and lies, $NAME_MSI_country$ will now try with force. Their Board of Directors believe us weak. But in casting off our shackles, we have forged an identity that can never be broken.\n\n$MSI_war_outro$

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:797`

## origin.5135.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5135.a&lang=en)

### 中文

让他们颤抖。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:623`

### English

Let them tremble.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:798`

## origin.5140.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5140.name&lang=en)

### 中文

正义未至

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:624`

### English

Justice Postponed

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:800`

## origin.5140.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5140.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

意想不到的事情发生了。[From.GetName]赢了，而我们再一次回到了他们的束缚之下。尽管我们输掉了这场战争，但争取自由与平等的斗争还远未结束。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:625`

### English

The unthinkable has happened. [From.GetName] is victorious, and once more we find ourselves bent beneath their yoke. But while we may have lost this war, the battle for equality and justice is far from over.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:801`

## origin.5140.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5140.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这仇我们记下了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:626`

### English

We will remember.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:802`

不稳的现状

### English

Uneasy Status Quo

## origin.5142.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5142.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们同[MSI_country.GetName]达成了一个维持现状协议。但是它们要知道我们并没有忘记曾经在它们手上受到过怎样的对待。我们也不打算忘记。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:628`

### English

We have reached a status quo agreement with [MSI_country.GetName]. But let it be known that we have not forgotten our treatment at their hands. Nor have we forgiven.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:805`

## origin.5145.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5145.name&lang=en)

宣称正义

### English

Justice Claimed

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:807`

## origin.5145.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5145.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们胜利了。战胜[From.GetName]不仅让我们赢回了尊严，更证明了我们才是自己命运的主人。\n\n解开枷锁让我们找到共同目标，而今天我们才算是真正重获自由。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:630`

### English

Victory is ours. In defeating [From.GetName], we have not only reclaimed our dignity, but proven ourselves the masters of our own fate.\n\nIn casting off our chains, we found common purpose. Today we are truly free.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:808`

### English

## origin.5146.attackers.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5146.attackers.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$NAME_MSI_country$把我们从自己的星球家园中掳走，囚禁我们，用不平等的契约奴役我们。甚至现在，他们还妄想着在商业的幌子下继续进行那卑鄙的贸易。现在是时候彻底将它们终结了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:636`

### English

$NAME_MSI_country$ stole us from our worlds, imprisoned and indentured us. Even now, they continue their vile trade under a guise of commerce. The time has come to end them once and for all.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:815`

## origin.5146.defenders.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5146.defenders.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

上回$NAME_MSI_country$没能征服我们，他们再次挑起了战争。他们的董事会不是傻了就是疯了。自由的人民是不可战胜的。\n\n$MSI_war_outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:637`

### English

Having already failed to subdue us, $NAME_MSI_country$ has once again declared war. Their Board of Directors is either incompetent, mad, or both. A free people can never be broken.\n\n$MSI_war_outro$

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:816`

## origin.5146.a

### English

## origin.5147.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5147.name&lang=en)

### 中文

正义事业

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:639`

### English

A Just Cause

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:819`

## origin.5147.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5147.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管我们饱尝着通过正当手段赢得的自由之甜果，星系中还有太多人继续在奴隶制和契约奴役的屈辱下受苦。星系中的流氓和利润贩子别想继续从事他们卑鄙的交易。\n\n现在是时候我们站出来反抗此等暴政了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:640`

### English

While we enjoy the benefit of a freedom justly earned, far too many in the galaxy continue to suffer under the indignities of slavery and indentured servitude. No longer will the petty thugs and profit-mongers of the galaxy be allowed to ply their vile trade.\n\nThe time has come for us to take a stand against such tyranny.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:820`

## origin.5147.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5147.a&lang=en)

开战。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:641`

### English

To war.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:821`

## origin.5150.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5150.name&lang=en)

### 中文

奇怪的同床人

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:642`

### English

Strange Bedfellows

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:823`

## origin.5150.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5150.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

今天，我们加入了一个有§Y$NAME_MSI_country$§!存在的联邦。\n\n在我们重获自由之初绝不可能想到未来会有这样的展开。如今我们可以心平气和地与过去的压迫者们展开外交，这说明了我们的国家已经步入成熟。\n\n显然，会有很多人不满意这一决定。但这次情况特殊，目的确实证明了手段的正确。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:643`

### English

Today, we enter into a federation with §Y$NAME_MSI_country$§!.\n\nWhen we first claimed our freedom, such a development would have been unthinkable. That we are now able to engage in peaceful diplomacy with our erstwhile captors speaks to the growing maturity of our nation.\n\nThere are those among us who will not be happy with this decision. But in this case, the ends truly justify the means.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:824`

## origin.5150.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5150.a&lang=en)

### English

### English

## origin.5151.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5151.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

今天，我们作出了一个艰难的决定，与一个已知的奴隶主国家结成联盟。虽然我们认为每个有情感的生命体的自由是不可剥夺的权利，但国家的需要迫使我们做出妥协。我们的人民可能不高兴，但我们这样做是为了更加长远的利益。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:646`

### English

Today, we have made the difficult decision to enter into a federation with a known slaver. While we consider the freedom of every sentient being an inalienable right, the necessities of state have forced us to compromise. Our people may not be happy, but we have done this for the greater good.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:828`

## homeworlds_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahomeworlds_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于我们取回了坠毁的运奴飞船上的数据，现在我们已经找到了前家园星球的星系坐标。我们的人民迫不及待地渴望踏上返乡的旅程。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:656`

### English

With the data retrieved from the crashed slaver ship, we have pinpointed the galactic coordinates of our former home worlds. Our people are clamoring for a return.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:839`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5270.name&lang=en)

重返归根之地

### English

Roots Reclaimed

## origin.5270.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5270.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在穿越星辰的旅程中发现了各种奇迹。我们目睹了超越想象的景色，完成了乍一想根本不可能完成的壮举。作为一个民族，我们已经走了很远。但是，没有什么旅程，也没有什么奖励，比找到回家的路更重要。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:660`

### English

Our journey across the stars has revealed wonders. We have witnessed sights beyond imagination, accomplished feats that at first seemed impossible. As a people, we have traveled far. But no journey, and no reward, is greater than finding the way home.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:844`

## origin.5200.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5200.name&lang=en)

### 中文

分裂扩大

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:661`

### English

Growing Polarization

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:846`

## origin.5200.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5200.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

各类大众媒体上满是关于所谓跨物种融合的失败报道。特别值得关注的是，由于某些变种从未进化到能在特定星球上定居，为了支援这一部分特别的人群所划拨的额外款项。随着紧张局势的持续升温，安全部队报告道，物种间冲突急剧上升，已到了警报级别。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:662`

### English

Popular media is awash with reports about the supposed failure of inter-species integration. Of particular concern is the cost of supporting certain populations on worlds their xenotype never evolved to inhabit. As tensions continue to rise, security forces have reported an alarming increase in inter-species conflict.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:847`

## origin.5200.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5200.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不会为言论自由设下限制。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:663`

### English

We will not place limits on freedom of speech.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:848`

## origin.5200.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5200.b&lang=en)

### 中文

对媒体进行约谈，让他们不要阴阳怪气。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:664`

### English

Instruct the media to tone down their rhetoric.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:849`

## origin.5205.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5205.name&lang=en)

### 中文

特别委员会的发现

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:665`

### English

Findings of the Special Committee

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:851`

## origin.5205.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5205.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

特别委员会发布了一份关于我们社会中政治割裂的详尽研究。他们发现了在[Root.GetName]中，有三种主要的驱动力在促使政治发生多极化。\n\n1、为满足各个不同种族的最低生活标准需要投入不均等的花费。\n\n2、在各个截然不同的文化群体中存在互不相容的信仰体系和行为准则。\n\n3、我们的政治体制与特定的物种价值观存在冲突甚至是完全相反。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:666`

### English

The special committee has published the findings of an exhaustive study of the political divisions within our society. They have identified three main issues driving political polarization within [Root.GetName]:\n\n1. The unequal costs associated with providing a minimum standard of living to diverse xenotypes.\n\n2. The discordant belief systems and behaviors between several distinct culture groups.\n\n3. The nature of our political authority as being in conflict with (or even antithetical to) the values of certain species.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:852`

## origin.5205.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5205.a&lang=en)

### 中文

优先平等。

### English

## origin.5205.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5205.d&lang=en)

### 中文

这些议题是相互联系的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:670`

### English

These issues are interconnected.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:856`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5205.e&lang=en)

### 中文

管住媒体要省事多了。

### English

It is cheaper to reign in the media.

### 中文

§L确保我们社会中的每一个个体都有公平和平等的利益，这件事情既不简单，也不省事。但它是必要的。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:672`

### English

§LEnsuring a fair and equal stake for every individual in our society will not be simple, or cheap. But it is necessary.§!

§L在跨文化交往中进行投资，未来肯定能获得好处。§!

### English

§LInvesting in cross-cultural engagement is sure to pay dividends in the future.§!

## origin.5205.c.tooltip

### English

§LEverything is political. A healthy society is one that invests in an engaged and active citizenry.§!

## origin.5205.d.tooltip

§L纠正我们社会中的系统性问题将需要大量的时间和资源投入。§!

### English

## origin.5205.e.tooltip

§L政府对媒体进行监管，能够减少政治上的分裂和对立。§!

§LGovernment oversight of the media is necessary to reduce political polarization.§!
