## origin.5210.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5210.name&lang=en)

### 中文

割裂的文化

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:677`

### English

A Culture of Division

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:864`

## origin.5210.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5210.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

政府的监管可能有助于淡化政治上的不和，但对消除社会中的分歧却起不到什么作用。社群领袖不断地在各物种和文化群体之间挑拨离间。根据信息网络上公布的材料，这个问题只会变得愈发糟糕。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:678`

### English

Governmental oversight may have helped to tone down the political discourse, but it has done little to combat the divisions in society. Community leaders continue to sow discord between various species and culture groups. Based on the kind of material being published on the information net, the problem is certain to get worse before it gets better.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:865`

## origin.5210.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5210.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们需要从根源上解决这个问题。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:679`

### English

We need to address the root causes of this issue.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:866`

## origin.5210.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5210.b&lang=en)

### 中文

限制登录信息网络。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:680`

### English

Restrict access to the information net.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:867`

## origin.5215.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5215.name&lang=en)

### 中文

跨物种暴力

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:681`

### English

Inter-Species Violence

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:869`

## origin.5215.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5215.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

几个月以来，我们限制了公众对信息网络的访问。虽然这能够阻止政治领域的一些偏激分子找到发声的平台，但物种间冲突的根本原因却没有得到遏制。暴力，包括对财产的大规模破坏，在我们的各个恒星系都呈上升趋势。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:682`

### English

For several months now, we have restricted public access to the information net. While this prevented some of the more outlandish voices in the political spectrum from finding a platform, the root causes of inter-species conflict have gone unchecked. Violence, including the mass destruction of property, is on the rise throughout our systems.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:870`

## origin.5215.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5215.a&lang=en)

### 中文

不惜一切代价恢复秩序。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:683`

### English

Restore order at all costs.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:871`

## origin.5220.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5220.name&lang=en)

### 中文

新的星际秩序

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:684`

### English

New Stellar Order

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:873`

## origin.5220.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5220.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的安保部队已经成功地恢复了我们所有恒星系的社会秩序。各类政治激进分子要么被围捕，要么被监禁，他们的领袖现在面临叛国罪的指控。\n\n回顾我们历史上这段令人遗憾的时期，有一点是非常清楚的：像我们这样的社会只能由强硬的手腕来管理。现在是时候由我们提供强硬的手腕了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:685`

### English

Our security forces have succeeded in restoring order throughout our systems. Political radicals of all stripes have been rounded up and imprisoned, and their leaders now face charges of treason against the state.\n\nAs we look back on this regrettable period in our history, one thing is abundantly clear: a society like ours can only be governed by a firm hand. It is high time we provided one.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:874`

## origin.5220.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5220.a&lang=en)

### 中文

只有团结一致，才能繁荣昌盛。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:686`

### English

United, we thrive.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:875`

## origin.5225.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5225.name&lang=en)

### 中文

支离破碎

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:687`

### English

Fractured Mosaic

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:877`

## origin.5225.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5225.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的社会自建立之初，就充斥着冲突。也许事情在变好之前不可避免地会变得更糟；在整个信息网络上，仇恨团体激增。要求将一个或另一个物种从我们的星域中驱逐出去的呼声已经是随处可见了。然而，也有迹象表明，人们对共同斗争的意识也有所提高。\n\n一些团体，除了对变革的渴望之外几乎没有什么共同点，但依然愈发频繁地走到了一起。通过直面我们的问题，在其他文明可能归于黑暗的事物，我们成功将其照亮。也许有一天，我们会把这段冲突的岁月看作是历史大势的转折点。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:688`

### English

From its founding, our society has been shaped by conflict. Perhaps it was inevitable that things would get worse before they got better; across the information net, hate groups proliferate. Calls for the expulsion of one or another species from our systems are all too common. Yet there are also signs of increased awareness of a shared struggle.\n\nGroups with little more in common than a desire for change have been coming together with increasing regularity. By confronting our issues head-on, we have shone a light on what otherwise might have grown in darkness. Perhaps one day we will look back on this time of conflict as the turning of the tide.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:878`

## origin.5230.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5230.name&lang=en)

### 中文

反叛的青年

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:689`

### English

Youthful Rebellion

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:880`

## origin.5230.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5230.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这届政府为提高我们公民的生活质量做出了不懈努力。我们投资于教育、犯罪预防和各种文化项目。然而，我们却不断地注意到自己成为了无情抨击的对象。更过分的是，一个由年轻人组成的跨物种运动已经组织起来，呼吁进行更激进的改革，变革的步子要迈得更大。\n\n这里只有一线希望：通过激励年轻人踊跃行动，我们可能在不知不觉中创造了弥合我们社会分裂的必要条件。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:690`

### English

This government has worked tirelessly to improve the quality of life for our citizenry. We have invested in education, crime prevention, and cultural programs of all kinds. Nevertheless, we continue to find ourselves the target of relentless criticism. What's more, a cross-species movement of young people has organized, calling for more radical reforms and a faster pace of change.\n\nThere is only one silver lining to be found here: in galvanizing the youth to act, we may have unwittingly created the conditions necessary to heal the divisions in our society.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:881`

## origin.5235.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5235.name&lang=en)

### 中文

滑向颓败

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:691`

### English

Decadent Slide

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:883`

## origin.5235.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5235.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管我们一直在努力推动营造清朗良好的政治氛围，然而到目前为止，普通民众几乎没有表现出参与政治的意愿。民意调查一直表明，构成我们社会的各物种和文化之间仍然存在重大分歧。\n\n明显相关的是，我们的经济学家已经注意到消费者支出的明显增加。虽然这在短期内可能对企业有利，但它也导致了通货膨胀的飙升。或许是我们的人民已经厌倦了争斗和不和谐。\n\n讽刺的是，作为政治上的结果，这些人因为在烧钱上志同道合而团结了起来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:692`

### English

Despite ongoing efforts to promote a healthy political climate, thus far, the general population has shown little engagement. Public opinion polls have consistently demonstrated that significant divisions remain between the various species and cultures that comprise our society.\n\nIn an apparent correlation, our economists have noticed a marked increase in consumer spending. While this may be good for business in the short-term, it has also led to a spike in inflation. It may be that our people have simply grown weary of the strife and discord.\n\nIronically, in tuning out politically, they have been united in a shared desire to consume.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:884`

## cost_of_freedom_outro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acost_of_freedom_outro&lang=en)

### 中文

一般而言，一定程度的政治不和总是客观存在的，但是我们之间塑造的连结却绝不会断裂。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:693`

### English

Naturally, a certain amount of political discord remains, but the bonds we've forged together can never be broken.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:886`

## origin.5240.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5240.name&lang=en)

### 中文

公正社会

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:694`

### English

The Just Society

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:888`

## origin.5240.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5240.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

作为一个民族，我们面临着几乎无法克服的困难。我们曾是一群各种各样的奴隶，因为争取自由的斗争被捆在了一起，我们的道路从来都不是一帆风顺的。但是，通过选择将我们的资源集中用于正义事业，我们成功地塑造了作为一个国家的身份。$cost_of_freedom_outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:695`

### English

As a people, we faced nearly insurmountable odds. A diverse group of former slaves, thrown together in a struggle for freedom, our path has never been easy. But in choosing to concentrate our resources towards the cause of justice, we have succeeded in forging an identity as a nation. $cost_of_freedom_outro$

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:889`

## origin.5245.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5245.name&lang=en)

### 中文

文艺复兴

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:696`

### English

Cultural Renaissance

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:891`

## origin.5245.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5245.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

从一开始，我们就面临着几乎无法克服的困难。一群奴隶和契约仆人，联合起来为了摆脱自己的桎梏……还有什么样的基础能比这更强大更牢固呢？这并不容易，但通过选择促进跨文化的对话，我们已经创造了一个强健和繁荣的社会。$cost_of_freedom_outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:697`

### English

From the beginning, we faced nearly insurmountable odds. A group of slaves and indentured servants, banding together to cast off their shackles... What stronger foundation could there be? It has not been easy, but in choosing to foster cross-cultural dialogue, we have created a robust and thriving society. $cost_of_freedom_outro$

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:892`

## origin.5250.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5250.name&lang=en)

### 中文

热烈的多元主义

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:698`

### English

Impassioned Pluralism

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:894`

## origin.5250.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5250.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们中间很少有人想到，当我们揭竿而起诉求自由时，争取平等的斗争才刚刚开始。作为一个由来自多个星球的物种组成的民族，对我们来说，建立共识或找到共同点从来都不容易，但通过鼓励参与政治生活，我们成功地创造了一个健全和繁荣的社会。$cost_of_freedom_outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:699`

### English

Little did it occur to us that the struggle for equality had only just begun when we rose up to claim our freedom. As a people of many worlds, it has never been easy for us to build consensus or to find common ground, but by investing in political engagement, we have succeeded in creating a robust and flourishing society. $cost_of_freedom_outro$

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:895`

## origin.5255.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5255.name&lang=en)

### 中文

成熟社会

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:700`

### English

Mature Society

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:897`

## origin.5255.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5255.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

因为渴望自由而联合到一起，我们奋起反抗曾经的主人。但是，社会不仅仅由暴力组成。从政治分裂到经济上的不安全，我们面临着巨大的挑战，但现在，当我们开始看到为建立共识所付出的不懈努力终于开花结果时，我们有理由为自己的瞩目成就感到骄傲。\n\n同一个国家，多样的民族；同一个民族，多元的文化。团结一致，我们无所不克。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:701`

### English

United by a desire for freedom, we rose up against our former masters. But society is not made by violence alone. From political division to economic insecurity, we have faced massive challenges, but now, as we begin to see the results of a concerted effort to build consensus, we can rightly take pride in our accomplishments.\n\nWe are a nation of many people, and a people of many cultures. Together, there is no challenge we cannot overcome.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:898`

## origin.5255.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5255.a&lang=en)

### 中文

多元之中孕育着我们的力量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:702`

### English

In diversity, our strength.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:899`

## cost_of_freedom_03

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acost_of_freedom_03&lang=en)

### 中文

国家特批媒体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:703`

### English

State Sanctioned Media

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:901`

## cost_of_freedom_03_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acost_of_freedom_03_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

为了维持社会秩序，我们对新闻界实行了一定的限制。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:704`

### English

In order to maintain social order, we have imposed certain limits on the media.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:902`

## cost_of_freedom_04

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acost_of_freedom_04&lang=en)

### 中文

亡羊补牢，为时不晚

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:705`

### English

Better Late Than Never

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:903`

## cost_of_freedom_04_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acost_of_freedom_04_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们没有选择，必须解决造成政治割裂局面的根本原因。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:706`

### English

We have no choice but to deal with the root causes of our political divisions.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:904`

### 中文

和解

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:736`

### English

Reconciliation

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:936`

## origin.5276.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5276.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们中的许多人曾以为这一天永远不会到来。今天，我们与$NAME_MSI_country$的董事会签订了正式的外交协议。\n\n毫无疑问肯定有些人会反对这一决定。过去的伤痛绝不能被抹消，但也不能让我们在未来面前故步自封。\n\n今天我们踏出了和解的第一步。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:737`

### English

Many among us thought this moment would never come. Today, we have formally entered in a diplomatic agreement with the Board of $NAME_MSI_country$.\n\nNo doubt there are some who will oppose this decision. The past can never be erased. But neither should it define the course of the future.\n\nToday we take the first steps towards reconciliation.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:937`

## origin.5276.federation.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5276.federation.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们决定与$NAME_MSI_country$构成联邦时，质疑声四起。许多人认为与曾经压迫我们的人和解绝不会带来任何好处。在过去这种担忧不无道理。\n\n如今，我们与$NAME_MSI_country_initials$的理事会签订了第一项外交协议，我们应当消除一律。我们要放下成见，平等地一起向前看。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:738`

### English

When we agreed to form a Federation with $NAME_MSI_country$, there was widespread skepticism. Many among us said no good could ever come from reconciling with our former masters. At the time, such concerns might have been warranted.\n\nToday, as we sign our first diplomatic agreement with the $NAME_MSI_country_initials$ Board of Directors, all doubts should be dispelled. Together, we look to a shared future. Not as adversaries, but as equals.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:938`

## origin.5500.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5500.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们发动兵变之后，运奴船的工程舱被迅速重新布置成为一个基础的综合实验室。在适应了[Root.Capital.GetName]上的生活后，我们的工程师对其进行了改进和升级，以更好地满足他们的需求。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:741`

### English

After the mutiny, the engineering bay of the slaver vessel was rapidly repurposed to serve as a rudimentary lab complex. Having adapted to life on [Root.Capital.GetName] our engineers have made improvements and upgrades to better suit their needs.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:942`

## origin.5500.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5500.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[Root.Capital.GetName]§!上的§H£building£$building_low_tech_research_lab$§!将升级为§H£building£$building_research_lab_1$§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:742`

### English

The §H£building£ $building_low_tech_research_lab$§! on §Y[Root.Capital.GetName]§! will be upgraded to §H£building£ $building_research_lab_1$§!.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:943`

### English

## origin.5505.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5505.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在进行最初的几次行动的时候，我们新生的政府就匆忙地将运奴船的几层甲板进行了重新改造，成为一个基本的行政管理中心。现在我们已经在[Root.Capital.GetName]上站稳了脚跟，而这些设施也已经得到了升级。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:744`

### English

In one of their first acts, our fledgling government hastily repurposed several decks of the slaver vessel to serve as a rudimentary administrative hub. Now that we have firmly established ourselves on [Root.Capital.GetName], these facilities have been upgraded.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:946`

### English

## origin.5515.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5515.name&lang=en)

### 中文

照料，还是提升

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:749`

### English

To Hold, or to Raise

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:952`

## origin.5515.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5515.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有一座环绕[From.GetName]的观测站，我们将更好地照看我们曾经的母星。这座观测站不仅会促进物质和文化交流，还能帮助我们改善这颗星球上人民的生活。\n\n现在我们必须做出决定：在知道结果的前提下，我们是否愿意帮助我们的同胞走向群星？\n\n毫无疑问，他们配得上在宇宙的舞台上加入我们，但他们真的准备好迈出这一步了吗？正如我们曾经经历过的，急速的社会变革的后果很可能是极端的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:750`

### English

With an observation post built around [From.GetName], we will be able to keep a much better watch on our former home world. Not only will this station facilitate material and cultural exchange, but it could also help us to improve the life of the people on the planet.\n\nA decision must be made: are we willing to help our kin reach the stars, knowing what the results could be?\n\nNo doubt they deserve to join us on the galactic stage, but are they truly ready to make the leap? As we have seen firsthand, the consequences of rapid social change can be extreme.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:953`

## origin.5515.a

## origin.5520.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5520.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的观测站现在正处于环绕[From.GetName]的轨道上。从这个制高点俯瞰下去，我们可以为同胞们的点点成功感到喜悦，如果有必要的话，还能帮助他们应对挑战。\n\n随着我们的发展，一个新的问题摆在了我们面前：我们是该让他们宁静地繁衍生息，还是让他们走向宇宙？两个选择都会有各自的风险，以及一定的回报。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:753`

### English

Our observation post is now in orbit around [From.GetName]. From this vantage point, we can celebrate the successes of our kin, and if necessary, help them to mitigate their challenges.\n\nAs we move forward, a new question presents itself: should we leave them in peace, or help them reach the stars? Both choices carry their own risks, and their own rewards.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:957`

## origin.5525.name

繁星的门槛

### English

Galactic Threshold

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:959`

## origin.5525.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.5525.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们提升[From.Capital.GetName]使其登上星海舞台的工作取得了巨大的成功。在一个新近统一的政府的领导下，我们的亲族将被称为“[From.GetName]”。\n\n在接下来的几个月和几年里，他们肯定要经历很多挑战。为了帮助它们在一个飘渺不定的星系中正确地前行，我们同意接纳他们作为附庸，至少目前是如此。与之相应地，我们已经交出了对该恒星系的完全控制权，并拆除了我们的观测站。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:755`

### English

Our work to uplift [From.Capital.GetName] to the galactic stage has been a resounding success. Under a newly unified government, our kin will be known as the "[From.GetName]."\n\nIn the months and years ahead, they are sure to experience many challenges. In order to help them navigate an uncertain galaxy, we have agreed to accept them as vassals, at least for the time being. Accordingly, we have ceded full control of the system and dismantled our Observation Post.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:960`

## job_low_tech_researcher_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_low_tech_researcher_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的旧主人毫不重视我们的天赋，对我们的智慧大加贬低。现在，我们中间最活跃的头脑已经可以随心所欲地追求自己的雄心。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:760`

### English

Our former masters ignored our gifts and downplayed our intelligence. Now, the keenest minds among us are free to follow their ambition.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:967`

## job_low_tech_laborer_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_low_tech_laborer_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

作为契约奴隶，他们将工作刻到了自己的骨子里。现在，随着自由的到来，他们有了新的奋斗目标，这是任何奴隶都无法企及的干劲。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:765`

### English

As assets they were worked to the bone. With freedom has come renewed purpose, and a vigor that no slave could ever hope to match.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:973`

## job_low_tech_technician_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ajob_low_tech_technician_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在旧主人的舰船上，这些作为契约奴隶的工程师经常被指派去做最卑贱、最危险的工作。正是这样的经历磨砺了他们的技能。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:771`

### English

Aboard their masters' vessels, indentured engineers were often assigned the lowliest and most dangerous jobs. This hardship only sharpened their skills.

### 中文

我们前奴隶主管制下的农业星球满是残酷压迫的场景。现在，我们的自耕农们可以自由地享受他们的劳动成果。

### English

The agri-worlds of our former masters were scenes of brutal oppression. Now, our homesteaders are free to enjoy the fruits of their labor.

## d_crashed_slaver_ship_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ad_crashed_slaver_ship_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然坠毁产生的碎片七零八落，散落得到处都是，但这艘船还是有相当一大部分保留了下来，搜刮它的零件或许能发现什么有价值得东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:821`

### English

While debris from the crash is scattered across a massive area, enough of the ship has survived that scavenging it for parts may be of value.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1044`

## origin_fear_of_the_dark_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin_fear_of_the_dark_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一个世纪以前，在帝国的母星系，其中的一颗行星被一颗古怪的小行星摧毁了。一些人认为这是某个外星物种的恶意攻击。其他人则把这些担忧当作纯粹的妄想症。两类人之间的关系变得愈发紧张，最后无可奈何之下，帝国将一个新近完成地形改造的星球给予这些恐宇派。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:847`

### English

A century ago, one of the planets in this empire's home system was destroyed by a freak asteroid. Some believed this was a malicious attack by an alien species. Others brushed off these concerns as mere paranoia. The tension between the two groups grew so great that a newly-terraformed planet was granted to the fearmongers.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1078`

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1079`

## START_SCREEN_FEAR_OF_THE_DARK_START

### 中文

一个世纪以前，被我们称为“大分裂者”的行星发生了爆炸。这便是我们 

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:850`

### English

A century ago, the planet we now call "The Divider" exploded. This was the beginning of our 

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1081`

## START_SCREEN_FEAR_OF_THE_DARK_REGULAR

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTART_SCREEN_FEAR_OF_THE_DARK_REGULAR&lang=en)

### 中文

$START_SCREEN_FEAR_OF_THE_DARK_START$分裂的开端。\n\n起先我们对这颗星球的毁灭不太在意，充其量只是一件怪事罢了，但[GetHomeWorldName]的一些人认为这其实是一场针对我们物种的有预谋的袭击。这些人认定是我们的无线电信号被远处的某些外星人发现了，他们便以这种足以毁灭行星的武器回应我们。这些反对者敦促我们做好应对这些外星人再次来袭甚至摧毁我们的准备。更多的人则认为他们的忧虑是杞人忧天。\n\n两个派别之间的政治割裂日益加深。蔓延全球的内乱最终促成了一项协议，为这些反对者们寻找到一个环境改造候选星球，这样他们就可以在其中隐居，不受我们的干涉。$START_SCREEN_FEAR_OF_THE_DARK_END$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:851`

### English

$START_SCREEN_FEAR_OF_THE_DARK_START$dissociation.\n\nAt first we brushed off the destruction as a freak occurrence, but there were some on [GetHomeWorldName] who believed it was a premeditated attack on our species. This faction hypothesized that our radio transmissions had been detected by some distant alien race, a race that replied with a planet-cracking projectile. These dissenters urged us to prepare for the day when the aliens would return to finish us off. Many dismissed their concerns as paranoia.\n\nThe political division between the two camps continued to grow. Planet-wide civil unrest culminated in the creation of a treaty that granted the dissidents a terraforming candidate, a planet upon which they would be free to reside in seclusion without any interference from us.$START_SCREEN_FEAR_OF_THE_DARK_END$

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1082`

## START_SCREEN_FEAR_OF_THE_DARK_MEGACORP

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTART_SCREEN_FEAR_OF_THE_DARK_MEGACORP&lang=en)

### 中文

$START_SCREEN_FEAR_OF_THE_DARK_START$互相疏远的开端。\n\n在那场事故以后，忧心忡忡的经理们向董事会发来请求，希望能关停所有现存的行星间无线电通讯设施，取消太空探索计划。他们相信大分裂者一定是某种外来的力量在尝试摧毁[GetHomeWorldName]。这项请求当然被否决了。\n\n这些经理们将董事会的决议称为“史诗级误判”，并激活了公民协议中的一项副条款，他们有权公开斥责董事会。为了安抚股东和增强消费者信心，决定向持异议的经理提供一份新合同。作为放弃与 [GetName] 有关的任何权利的交换，他们将获得一颗环境改造候选星球，并且保证他们的财政自主。$START_SCREEN_FEAR_OF_THE_DARK_END$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:852`

### English

$START_SCREEN_FEAR_OF_THE_DARK_START$estrangement.\n\nAfter the incident, worried managers sent requests to our Board of Directors to shut down existing extra-planetary radio infrastructure and cancel our space exploration programs. They believed the creation of The Divider was caused by an external agent attempting to destroy [GetHomeWorldName]. This request was denied.\n\nCalling the Board's decision a "monumental lapse of judgment", these managers activated a sub-clause in their citizenship contracts that granted them the right to publicly rebuke the Board. In an effort to placate share-holders and to bolster consumer confidence, the decision was made to offer the dissenting managers a new contract. In exchange for giving up any rights pertaining to [GetName], they would be provided with a terraforming candidate, and a guarantee of fiscal independence.$START_SCREEN_FEAR_OF_THE_DARK_END$

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1083`

## START_SCREEN_FEAR_OF_THE_DARK_END

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASTART_SCREEN_FEAR_OF_THE_DARK_END&lang=en)

### 中文

\n\n随着超光速引擎的出现，一个新的紧张局势出现了：我们渴望拥抱宇宙，但我们的邻居却旗帜鲜明地反对。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:853`

### English

\n\nWith the advent of the Hyperdrive, a new source of tension has arisen: while we are eager to greet the galaxy, our neighbors stand resolutely in opposition.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1084`

## origin.6005.name

要求转移

### English

Transfer Request

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1123`

## origin.6005.desc.base1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6005.desc.base1&lang=en)

### 中文

一名来自[fotd_habitants.capital_scope.GetName]的科学家要求加入我们[Owner.GetHomeWorldName]。\n\n[fotd_Leader.GetName]是[fotd_Leader.GetSpecialization]专家，和[Root.GetHomeWorldName]上的任何[Root.GetSpeciesName]享受同等权利。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:891`

### English

A scientist from [fotd_habitants.capital_scope.GetName] has requested to join us on [Owner.GetHomeWorldName].\n\n[fotd_Leader.GetName] is [fotd_Leader.GetSpecialization] specialist, equal to any [Root.GetSpeciesName] on [Root.GetHomeWorldName].

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1124`

## origin.6005.desc.base2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6005.desc.base2&lang=en)

### 中文

一名来自[fotd_habitants.capital_scope.GetName]的科学家要求加入我们[Owner.GetHomeWorldName]。\n\n我们的首席科学家同[fotd_Leader.GetName]保持了多年的联系，对[fotd_Leader.GetHerHis]所作的工作赞不绝口。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:892`

### English

A scientist from [fotd_habitants.capital_scope.GetName] has requested to join us on [Owner.GetHomeWorldName].\n\nOur lead scientists have been in communication with [fotd_Leader.GetName] for many years and are extremely impressed by [fotd_Leader.GetHerHis] work.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1125`

## origin.6005.desc.base3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6005.desc.base3&lang=en)

### 中文

一名来自[fotd_habitants.capital_scope.GetName]的科学家要求加入我们[Owner.GetHomeWorldName]。\n\n尽管[fotd_Leader.GetSheHe]被[fotd_Leader.GetHerHis]在[owner.GetHomeWorldName]上的同事称为[fotd_habitants.capital_scope.GetName]上最后一位偏执狂科学家，但这样的论述并不够全面。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:893`

### English

A scientist from [fotd_habitants.capital_scope.GetName] has requested to join us on [Owner.GetHomeWorldName].\n\nAlthough [fotd_Leader.GetSheHe] has been described as the least paranoid scientist on [fotd_habitants.capital_scope.GetName] by [fotd_Leader.GetHerHis] colleagues on [owner.GetHomeWorldName], this isn't really saying much.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1126`

## origin.6005.desc.base4

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6005.desc.base4&lang=en)

### 中文

一名来自[fotd_habitants.capital_scope.GetName]的科学家要求加入我们[Owner.GetHomeWorldName]。\n\n[fotd_Leader.GetName]视自己为一名殉道者。[fotd_Leader.GetSheHe]前来我们“厄运笼罩的星球”，希望拯救我们的科技免受外部势力的摧毁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:894`

### English

A scientist from [fotd_habitants.capital_scope.GetName] has requested to join us on [Owner.GetHomeWorldName].\n\n[fotd_Leader.GetName] styles themselves something of a martyr. In traveling to our "doomed world" [fotd_Leader.GetSheHe] hope to preserve our technology from destruction by outside forces.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1127`

## origin.6005.desc.end1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6005.desc.end1&lang=en)

### 中文

[fotd_Leader.GetSheHeCap]向我们求助，因为[fotd_habitants.capital_scope.GetName]上条件受限的设施阻碍了[fotd_Leader.GetHerHis]的研究。我们的科学家已经做了担保，保证这位潜在访客的一切行为都是良好的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:895`

### English

[fotd_Leader.GetSheHeCap] reached out to us because the limited facilities on [fotd_habitants.capital_scope.GetName] are stunting [fotd_Leader.GetHerHis] research. Our scientists have vouched for our potential visitor's good behavior.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1129`

## origin.6005.desc.end2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6005.desc.end2&lang=en)

### 中文

[fotd_Leader.GetHerHisCap]的研究已经被[fotd_Leader.GetHerHis]的政府定性为危险。他们的损失，是我们的收获。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:896`

### English

[fotd_Leader.GetHerHisCap] research has been classified as dangerous by [fotd_Leader.GetHerHis] government. Their loss is our gain.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1130`

## origin.6005.desc.end3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6005.desc.end3&lang=en)

### 中文

出于在学术上的杰出表现，[fotd_Leader.GetSheHeCap]将获得[Owner.GetHomeWorldName]上的一个著名奖项。[fotd_Leader.GetSheHeCap]也同意在我们这里待上一段时间，就自己的研究举行一系列讲座。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:897`

### English

[fotd_Leader.GetSheHeCap] will be the recipient of a prestigious award on [Owner.GetHomeWorldName] for academic excellence. [fotd_Leader.GetSheHeCap] have also agreed to stay with us for a while, holding a series of lectures about their research.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1131`

## origin.6005.desc.end4

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6005.desc.end4&lang=en)

### 中文

[fotd_Leader.GetSheHeCap]会研究当地的[GetStudyTopic]，并试图利用这些来改善[fotd_habitants.capital_scope.GetName]上的生活。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:898`

### English

[fotd_Leader.GetSheHeCap] will be studying local [GetStudyTopic] in an attempt to improve life on [fotd_habitants.capital_scope.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1132`

## origin.6005.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6005.a&lang=en)

### 中文

只要你遵守这个社会的规范 ，那么你就会受到欢迎。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:899`

### English

You are welcome as long as you abide by the norms of this society.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1133`

## origin.6005.a.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6005.a.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

将§Y[fotd_Leader.GetName]§!作为§H科学官§!添加到我们的§H$RECRUITABLE_LEADERS$§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:900`

### English

Add §Y[fotd_Leader.GetName]§! as a §HScientist§! to our §H$RECRUITABLE_LEADERS$§!.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1134`

## origin.6005.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6005.b&lang=en)

### 中文

管好你自己。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:901`

### English

Stay on your rock.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1135`

## origin.6006.desc.base1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6006.desc.base1&lang=en)

### 中文

一位来自[fotd_habitants.Capital.GetName]的指挥官提出要加入我们的海军，担任临时指挥官。[fotd_Leader.GetSheHeCap]的动机尚不清楚，但我们的线人告诉我们，这可能牵涉到一起丑闻。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:902`

### English

A Commander from [fotd_habitants.Capital.GetName] is offering to join our navy as a temporary commander. [fotd_Leader.GetSheHeCap] motivations are unclear, but our informants tell us that there might be a scandal involved.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1137`

## origin.6006.desc.base2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6006.desc.base2&lang=en)

### 中文

[fotd_Leader.GetName]非常急迫地想和我们签署临时合同。[fotd_habitants.Capital.GetName]一直是滋生狡猾和非常规战术的温床。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:903`

### English

[fotd_Leader.GetName] is eager to sign on with us temporarily. [fotd_habitants.Capital.GetName] has always been a breeding ground for crafty and unconventional tactics.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1138`

## origin.6006.desc.base3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6006.desc.base3&lang=en)

### 中文

由于指挥官[fotd_Leader.GetName]在最近一次联合舰队演习上的出色表现，我们决定扩大此人的权限，让其指挥一支真正的海军舰队。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:904`

### English

After excelling in a recent joint fleet exercise, we have extended Commander [fotd_Leader.GetName] the opportunity to take command in a real navy.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1139`

## origin.6006.desc.base4

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6006.desc.base4&lang=en)

### 中文

因涉嫌将[fotd_Leader.GetHerHis]指挥的舰船置于过度危险之中而被暂时调离岗位，不服气的指挥官[fotd_Leader.GetName]请求加入我们的海军。尽管[fotd_Leader.GetName]名声在外，那些曾与[fotd_Leader.GetHerHim]一起服役的人仍然尊重[fotd_Leader.GetHerHis]的才能。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:905`

### English

Placed on temporary leave for allegedly putting the vessels under [fotd_Leader.GetHerHis] command in excessive danger, Commander [fotd_Leader.GetName] has requested to join our navy. Despite [fotd_Leader.GetName]'s reputation, those who have served with [fotd_Leader.GetHerHim] still respect [fotd_Leader.GetHerHis] talent.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1140`

## origin.6006.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6006.a&lang=en)

### 中文

只要你不叛逃。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:906`

### English

As long as you don't go rogue.

## origin.6006.a.tt

将§Y[fotd_Leader.GetName]§!作为§H指挥官§!添加到我们的§H$RECRUITABLE_LEADERS$§!。

### English

Add §Y[fotd_Leader.GetName]§! as an §HCommander§! to our §H$RECRUITABLE_LEADERS$§!.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1142`

## origin.6006.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6006.b&lang=en)

### 中文

对于一根松动的鱼雷管来说，你也太胖了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:908`

### English

You are too much of a loose torpedo tube!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1143`

## origin.6010.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

渴望[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:909`

### English

Yearning for [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1145`

## origin.6010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在同我们一起生活了许多年之后，[fotd_Leader.GetName]决定返回家乡。一些政府官员反对这一变动，因为[fotd_Leader.GetName]已经成为我们高级领导层不可或缺的一部分。\n\n[GetfotdCoercion]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:910`

### English

After long years living among us, [fotd_Leader.GetName] has decided to return home. Some in government are opposed to the move, as [fotd_Leader.GetName] has become an integral part of our senior leadership.\n\n[GetfotdCoercion]

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1146`

## origin.6010.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6010.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会信守承诺。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:911`

### English

We will keep our word.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1147`

## origin.6010.a.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6010.a.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

[fotd_Leader.GetName]会返回[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:912`

### English

[fotd_Leader.GetName] will return to [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1148`

## origin.6010.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6010.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们必须不惜一切代价留住他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:913`

### English

We need to keep them at any cost.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1149`

## origin.6011.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6011.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最近，[fotd_Leader.GetName]的性格变得越来越孤僻。[fotd_Leader.GetSheHe]再次请求允许他回家。[fotd_Leader.GetHerHisCap]的同事指出，[fotd_Leader.GetHerHis]由于患上了抑郁症一直对他们的工作产生了负面影响，并建议我们批准[fotd_Leader.GetHerHis]离开。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:914`

### English

Recently, [fotd_Leader.GetName] has grown increasingly withdrawn. Once again, [fotd_Leader.GetSheHe] is requesting permission to return home. [fotd_Leader.GetHerHisCap] coworkers note that [fotd_Leader.GetHerHis] depression has been affecting their work, and suggest we let [fotd_Leader.GetHerHis] go.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1150`

## origin.6012.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6012.name&lang=en)

### 中文

回家

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:915`

### English

Gone Home

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1152`

## origin.6012.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6012.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在过去的几个周期里，人们看到[fotd_Leader.GetName]在喃喃自语，有时在大厅里踱步。今天，[fotd_Leader.GetSheHe]没有来上班。我们的一架逃生飞船也报告失踪。这些事件不太可能是一个巧合。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:916`

### English

Over the last several cycles, [fotd_Leader.GetName] has been seen muttering to themselves and pacing the halls. Today, [fotd_Leader.GetSheHe] failed to arrive at work. One of our escape shuttles has also been reported missing. It's unlikely these events are a coincidence.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1153`

## origin.6012.a.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6012.a.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

[fotd_Leader.GetName]回到了[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:917`

### English

[fotd_Leader.GetName] returned to [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1154`

## origin.6015.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6015.name&lang=en)

### 中文

跨星系洞见

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:918`

### English

Inter-system Insight

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1155`

## origin.6015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

似乎我们兄弟星球上的科学家已经在[root.fotdGetTech]领域取得了数个全新的、公认的、有见地的突破。尽管他们可以说是目光狭隘，但在这种情况下有一说一，他们的技术已经超过了我们自己取得的成就。\n\n他们愿意与我们分享他们的见解，以换取我们[root.fotdGetTechPrice]——可以肯定的是，这个价格不菲。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:919`

### English

It seems that the scientists on our partner planet have made several new, and admittedly insightful, breakthroughs into the field of [root.fotdGetTech]. Despite their parochial tendencies, it's fair to say that in this case their technology has outstripped our own achievements.\n\nThey are willing to share their insights with us in exchange for us [root.fotdGetTechPrice] - a steep price, to be sure.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1156`

## origin.6015.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6015.a&lang=en)

### 中文

好，我们会遵从。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:920`

### English

Fine, we will comply.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1157`

## origin.6015.a.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6015.a.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

这会让我们§R10年§!内无法进入§Y虚境§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:921`

### English

This will prevent us from accessing §YThe Shroud§! for §R10 years§!.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1158`

## origin.6015.b

### English

[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] [root.GetSeperatistAid] Request

## origin.6020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

无论是出于工作疏忽、工作人员的无能，还是纯粹的不幸，§Y[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]§!现在深陷[root.GetSeperatistAid]供应危机。如果没有帮助，很可能数百万人将受灾。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:925`

### English

Whether due to negligence, incompetence, or sheer misfortune §Y[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]§! now struggle with their [root.GetSeperatistAid] supply. Without help it is very likely that millions will suffer.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1163`

## origin.6020.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6020.a&lang=en)

我们需要帮助[fotd_seperatist_planet.owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]同胞。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:926`

### English

We must help our fellow [fotd_seperatist_planet.owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural].

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1164`

## origin.6020.a.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6020.a.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]会铭记这件事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:927`

### English

[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] will remember this.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1165`

## origin.6020.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6020.b&lang=en)

那我们又能得到什么回报呢？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:928`

### English

What do we get in return?

如果他们需要帮助，他们应该重新加入我们。

### English

If they want aid they should rejoin us.

## origin.6025.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6025.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName][root.GetFotDAid]供应

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:930`

### English

[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] [root.GetFotDAid] Offer

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1168`

## origin.6025.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6025.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]似乎从周边某个地方收到了经济援助。现在他们已经准备好防范[root.GetFotDAid]危机中可能出现的最坏的影响了。 

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:931`

### English

Economic aid seems to have come from a nearby source. [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] is ready to stem the worst effects of our current [root.GetFotDAid] crisis. 

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1169`

## origin.6025.desc.helped

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6025.desc.helped&lang=en)

### 中文

$origin.6025.desc.base$\n\n由于我们先前的支援，他们决定偿还我们的人情，不求回报地向我们提供帮助。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:932`

### English

$origin.6025.desc.base$\n\nDue to our previous support, they have decided to repay the favor, offering us help free of charge.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1170`

## origin.6025.desc.didnthelp

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6025.desc.didnthelp&lang=en)

### 中文

$origin.6025.desc.base$\n\n一位沾沾自喜的信使告知我们他们提供帮助的报价是[root.fotdGetTechPrice]。我们有得选吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:933`

### English

$origin.6025.desc.base$\n\nA smug message informed us that the price for their help would be [root.fotdGetTechPrice]. Do we have a choice?

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1171`

给这帮子[fotd_seperatist_planet.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePluralInsult]他们想要的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:935`

### English

Give the [fotd_seperatist_planet.Owner.GetSpeciesNamePluralInsult] what they want.

## origin.6025.c

我们应该独立挺过难关。

### English

We shall weather this alone.

## origin.6030.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6030.name&lang=en)

### 中文

第一次清理

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:937`

### English

The First Clearing

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1176`

## origin.6030.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6030.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然确认外星人存在的消息对[Root.GetHomeWorldName]来说并非意料之外，但在[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]上情况就要糟糕得多。他们本来抱着微不足道的希望生活，祈愿大分裂者的毁灭只是一个古怪的事故，但现在他们确信，宇宙是要来对付他们。\n\n“我们需要在这些外星人来这里完成工作之前消灭他们！”这正是[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]媒体上正在播放的那种疯狂的呼喊。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:938`

### English

While the news confirming existence of aliens was not entirely unexpected for [Root.GetHomeWorldName], it was felt worse on [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]. They were living with the minuscule hope that perhaps the Divider's destruction was just a freak accident, but now they are convinced that the universe is out to get them.\n\n"We need to destroy these Aliens before they can come here and finish the job!" is the type of frantic cry being broadcast on [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] media.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1177`

## origin.6030.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6030.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们生活的星系就是如此。他们很快就会适应的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:939`

### English

That's the galaxy we live in. They will adapt soon enough.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1178`

## origin.6030.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6030.b&lang=en)

### 中文

这是无稽之谈，屏蔽他们的传输信号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:940`

### English

This is nonsense. Block their transmissions.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1179`

## origin.6040.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6040.name&lang=en)

### 中文

发现追踪者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:941`

### English

Stalker Sighted

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1181`

## origin.6040.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6040.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

发现外星智能生命的消息已经传到[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]。然而，出乎我们的预期，[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]并没有传来恐惧和愤怒的情感，当地反而陷入了沉默。多艘恒星际飞船重新回到了自己的船坞，还有数个旧的超光速驱动器从仓库中挪出。看来他们正准备进行一次远行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:942`

### English

News of the discovery of intelligent alien life has reached [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]. However, instead of the fear and outrage that we expected to from [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName], they have instead gone silent. Multiple inter-system ships have returned to their dockyards, and several old FTL drives are missing from storage. It appears they are preparing for an excursion.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1182`

## origin.6040.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6040.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们需要知道正在发生的情况。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:943`

### English

We need to know what's going on.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1183`

## origin.6040.a.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6040.a.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

进行安管巡查，以应对任何可能的情报泄露。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:944`

### English

Secure our investigation against any possible leaks.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1184`

## origin.6040.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6040.b&lang=en)
