让他们自个玩去吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:945`

### English

Let them play their games.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1185`

## origin.6045.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6045.name&lang=en)

### 中文

灾难性的初见

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:946`

### English

Disastrous Introduction

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1187`

## origin.6045.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6045.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们与[From.GetName]初步展开沟通后，我们收到了一些令人不安的消息：一支由过时的[Root.GetAdj]飞船组成的攻击舰队已经进入[From.GetSpeciesAdj]的宇域，现在正向他们的恒星基地加速前进。这些战舰以这样的加速度前进，想必舰船内部任何[owner_species.GetBiologicalLithoidLower]现在已经变成了[owner_species.GetLiquidState]状态。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:947`

### English

After our initial communication with the [From.GetName], we received some troubling news: an attack fleet of outdated [Root.GetAdj] ships has entered [From.GetSpeciesAdj] space, and is now speeding towards their starbase. The accelerating ships are moving at such a rate that anything [owner_species.GetBiologicalLithoidLower] inside them must have been reduced to a [owner_species.GetLiquidState] state by now.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1188`

## origin.6045.desc.secured

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6045.desc.secured&lang=en)

### 中文

$origin.6045.desc.base$\n\n幸运的是，我们做好了充分的准备。在观察到[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]的异常行为后，我们随时都可以在太空中将他们打下来，不让他们击中空间站。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:948`

### English

$origin.6045.desc.base$\n\nFortunately, we are prepared. Having observed the odd behavior of [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] we stand ready to shoot them out of space before they can hit the station.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1189`

## origin.6045.desc.notsecured

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6045.desc.notsecured&lang=en)

### 中文

$origin.6045.desc.base$\n\n我们没有办法阻止这场灾难。这些超规格的导弹将彻底摧毁[target_system.GetName]的恒星基地。我们只能惊恐地看着。除非……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:949`

### English

$origin.6045.desc.base$\n\nThere is nothing we can do to stop this disaster. These oversized missiles will completely demolish the starbase in [target_system.GetName]. We can only look on in horror. Unless...

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1190`

## origin.6045.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6045.a&lang=en)

### 中文

立刻阻止他们！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:950`

### English

Stop them now!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1191`

## origin.6045.starbase_destroyed.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6045.starbase_destroyed.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[target_system.GetName]§!的恒星基地会被§R摧毁§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:951`

### English

The starbase in §Y[target_system.GetName]§! will be §RDestroyed§!.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1192`

## origin.6045.b.secured

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6045.b.secured&lang=en)

### 中文

让他们削弱这些外星人，然后把锅扣在[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:952`

### English

Let them weaken these aliens, then reprimand [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1193`

## origin.6045.b.notsecured

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6045.b.notsecured&lang=en)

### 中文

发表道歉声明，并责备[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:953`

### English

Issue an apology and reprimand [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1194`

## origin.6045.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6045.c&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的邻国很虚弱。趁着这个机会结果他们！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:954`

### English

Our neighbors are weak. Use this opportunity to end them!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1195`

## origin.6045.c.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6045.c.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

$origin.6045.starbase_destroyed.tt$\n[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]将会失去§Y5§!艘位于§Y[target_system.GetName]§!星系的护卫舰的控制权。\n将会同§Y[From.GetName]§!进入§R全面战争§!状态。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:955`

### English

$origin.6045.starbase_destroyed.tt$\n[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] will cede control of §Y5§! corvettes in §Y[target_system.GetName]§!.\nWill enter a §RTotal War§! with §Y[From.GetName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1196`

## origin.6046.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6046.name&lang=en)

### 中文

损失恒星基地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:956`

### English

Starbase Lost

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1197`

## origin.6046.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6046.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们位于§Y[target_system.GetName]§!的恒星基地被一帮子所谓的[From.GetName]“叛逃分子”给摧毁了。我们已经收到了道歉，对方承诺不会再发生这种情况。但谁知道呢？今天是[target_system.GetName]，也许明天就是[capital_star.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:957`

### English

Our starbase in §Y[target_system.GetName]§! has been destroyed by so-called "rogue agents", the [From.GetName]! We have already received an apology and a promise that this will not happen again. But who knows? First it was [target_system.GetName], and perhaps [capital_star.GetName] is next.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1198`

## origin.6046.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6046.a&lang=en)

### 中文

密切关注他们的行动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:958`

### English

Keep an eye on them.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1199`

## origin.6046.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6046.b&lang=en)

### 中文

他们威胁到了我们的存续。不是他们死就是我们亡！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:959`

### English

They threaten our very existence. It's them or us!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1200`

## origin.6047.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6047.name&lang=en)

### 中文

猎手与猎物

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:960`

### English

Hunter and Prey

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1201`

## origin.6047.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6047.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在与[From.GetName]第一次接触后不久，我们在§Y[target_system.GetName]§!的恒星基地就被爆炸摧毁。在系统掉线之前，我们接收到了图像，那是一支采用[From.GetAdj]设计的敌方舰队。\n\n我们现在发现自己卷入了一场事关生死存亡的战争之中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:961`

### English

Shortly after first contact with the [From.GetName], our starbase in §Y[target_system.GetName]§! exploded. Before the system went dark, we received images of a hostile armada of [From.GetAdj] design.\n\nWe now find ourselves engaged in a war for our very survival.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1202`

## origin.6055.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6055.name&lang=en)

### 中文

非此即彼！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:962`

### English

It's Us or Them!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1204`

## origin.6055.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6055.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然我们尽了全力设法将他们蒙在鼓里，但没过多久，[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]就听说我们成为了[owner.federation.GetName]的成员国一事。这种亲外主义的证据引起了我们伙伴世星球的一些极端反响。一夜之间，许多对我们联邦其他成员国的贬义词和标签涌现出来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:963`

### English

While we did our best to keep them in the dark, it did not take long for [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] to hear about our membership in the [owner.federation.GetName]. This evidence of xenophilia has elicited some extreme reactions from our partner world. Derogatory terms and labels for our federation members have sprung up overnight.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1205`

## origin.6055.desc.noprotests

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6055.desc.noprotests&lang=en)

### 中文

$origin.6055.desc.base$\n\n公众的愤怒甚至影响到了我们兄弟星球的学术界，他们现在已经结束了我们两颗星球之间的所有合作项目。只能希望我们的新联邦盟友能够理解这一切。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:964`

### English

$origin.6055.desc.base$\n\nThe public outrage has even reached the academies of our partner world which have now ended all cooperative projects between our worlds. It is to be hoped that our new federation allies will understand.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1206`

## origin.6055.desc.protests

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6055.desc.protests&lang=en)

### 中文

$origin.6055.desc.base$\n\n[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]已经因为我们先前的外交行动被激怒，而这一次则让他们彻底翻了脸。在我们切断他们的联系之前，他们设法向我们的盟友发送了几条五颜六色的信息，损害了我们之间的关系，其影响也许是永久性的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:965`

### English

$origin.6055.desc.base$\n\n[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] was already agitated after our previous diplomatic actions, and this seems to have tipped them over the edge. Before we could cut them off, they managed to send several colorful messages to our allies, damaging our relations, perhaps permanently.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1207`

## origin.6055.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6055.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的盟友会把这个视作无意义的闹剧。肯定的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:966`

### English

Our allies will see this for the nonsense it is. Surely.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1208`

## origin.6055.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6055.b&lang=en)

### 中文

派出特使！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:967`

### English

Send for the envoys!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1209`

## origin.6060.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6060.name&lang=en)

### 中文

顶级掠食者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:968`

### English

Apex Predator

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1211`

## origin.6060.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6060.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

自[FromFrom.solar_system.GetName]恒星系传来的消息深深震撼了我们的母星。如果像这样的怪物都能够存在，那么在太空深处还可能潜伏着什么样的威胁？在我们的人民中，要求移民到[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]的申请已经飙升。然而此时此刻，我们很难责怪他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:969`

### English

The news from the [FromFrom.solar_system.GetName] system has shaken our homeworld deeply. What other threats may be lurking in the depths of space, if monsters like this can exist? Already, applications for immigration to [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] have soared amongst our people. At the moment, it is difficult to blame them.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1212`

## origin.6060.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6060.a&lang=en)

### 中文

难道我们错了？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:970`

### English

We were wrong?

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1213`

## origin.6060.a.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6060.a.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

§R200§!£pop£§H人口§!会移民前往[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:971`

### English

§R200§! £pop£ §HPops§! will emigrate to [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1214`

## origin.6060.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6060.b&lang=en)

### 中文

不！[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]才是错误的！我们必须一起面对这个。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:972`

### English

No! [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] is wrong! We will face this together.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1215`

## origin.6065.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6065.name&lang=en)

### 中文

所作所为的后果

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:973`

### English

Consequences of Our Own Actions

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1217`

## origin.6065.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6065.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当我们向[overlord.GetName]臣服后，[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]立马就来讥讽我们。\n\n“任何外星生命本质上都是有敌意的。如果你们当初就留在[home_planet.GetStarName]上，这一切都不会发生！”\n\n不用说，我们的内部人民现在还不是特别团结。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:974`

### English

After our submission to the [overlord.GetName], [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] did not waste any time ridiculing us.\n\n"Alien life is inherently hostile. If you all had simply stayed in [home_planet.GetStarName], none of this would have happened!"\n\nNeedless to say, our people are not particularly unified right now.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1218`

## origin.6065.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6065.a&lang=en)

### 中文

通讯终止。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:975`

### English

End transmission.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1219`

## origin.6075.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6075.name&lang=en)

### 中文

绵羊的外衣

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:976`

### English

Sheep's Clothing

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1221`

## origin.6075.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6075.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在收取了一个附庸之后，我们的帝国朝着一个宇宙强权又迈进了一步。然而，[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]却不以为然。根据他们的说法，我们只是成功地将外星人的威胁拉近了。为了表示抗议，他们已经停止了与我们的所有研究合作。\n\n假以时日，我们一定会证明他们是错的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:977`

### English

In taking a vassal, our empire made a major leap forward as a galactic power. However, [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] is not impressed. According to them, we have only succeeded in bringing the alien threat closer. In protest, they have ceased all research cooperation with us.\n\nIn time, we will certainly prove them wrong.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1222`

## origin.6075.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6075.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的属民肯定没有在密谋针对我们！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:978`

### English

Our subject surely isn't plotting against us!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1223`

## origin.6085.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6085.name&lang=en)

### 中文

先发制人

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:979`

### English

First Strike

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1225`

## origin.6085.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6085.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[FromFrom.GetName]的黎明，我们获得了一个意想不到的盟友：来自[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]的同志们终于在一个外交问题上与我们达成一致，恳请我们将外星人扫除掉。令人遗憾的是，一旦战争结束，这样的合作就要告一段落了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:980`

### English

At the dawn of the [FromFrom.GetName] we have gained an unexpected ally: our comrades from [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] have finally aligned themselves with us on a diplomatic issue, imploring us to wipe the xenos out. It's a shame that once the war is over their co-operation will end.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1226`

## origin.6085.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6085.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的船坞需要协助！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:981`

### English

Our shipyards need assistance!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1227`

## origin.6085.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6085.b&lang=en)

### 中文

指派人手前往我们的舰船。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:982`

### English

Assign personnel to our ships.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1228`

## origin.6085.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6085.c&lang=en)

### 中文

他们观测到什么和敌人有关的吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:983`

### English

Have they observed anything about the enemy?

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1229`

## origin.6085.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6085.d&lang=en)

### 中文

我们能独自应对。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:984`

### English

We can stand alone.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1230`

## origin.6090.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6090.name&lang=en)

### 中文

早就说过

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:985`

### English

Told You So

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1231`

## origin.6090.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6090.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]的住民曾经预言的那一天如今已经到来：随着敌国对我们宣战，星系已经成为一处荒凉、充满敌意的境地。然而，这个故事尚未到结局。我们将挺过这一关。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:986`

### English

The day the inhabitants of [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] predicted has arrived: with war declared against us, the galaxy has become an inhospitable and hostile realm. However, this story's ending is not yet written. We will survive this.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1232`

## origin.6095.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6095.name&lang=en)

### 中文

多线程思维

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:988`

### English

Multithreaded Thoughts

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1235`

## origin.6095.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6095.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的人民对于这种通过人为手段提升思想水平的经历还很陌生，因此当听说[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]的人们对我们的新升级持有如此激烈的反对意见之后，普遍感到惊讶。这些人警告说，如果我们再“玷污”自己的身体，所有先前的合作努力都将视作无效。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:989`

### English

Still new to this artificially elevated level of thought, our people were surprised to receive such vehement opposition to our new upgrades from [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]. They warn that if we "defile" our bodies any further, all collaborative efforts will be canceled.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1236`

## origin.6095.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6095.a&lang=en)

### 中文

对于在这个星系中存活需要付出怎样的代价，他们的理解太肤浅太单纯了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:990`

### English

They are too simple to understand what it takes to survive in this galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1237`

## origin.6096.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6096.name&lang=en)

### 中文

增强的权利

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:991`

### English

Right to Enhancement

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1239`

## origin.6096.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6096.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们决定彻底地转换到合成体的生命形式，作为结果，[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]的人们充分履行了当初的承诺：每条通信途径、每个合作研究站和所有形式的媒体现在都被封锁了。\n\n考虑到我们现在已经发展到了如此地步，问题的关键在于，我们是否还需要他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:992`

### English

As a result of our decision to transition to a fully cybernetic form of life, the people of [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] have made good on their promise: every avenue of communication, every collaborative research station, and all forms of media are now blocked.\n\nThe question is whether or not we still need them, now that we are so much more.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1240`

## origin.6096.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6096.a&lang=en)

### 中文

让他们在黑暗中颤抖吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:993`

### English

Let them cower in the dark.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1241`

## origin.6099.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6099.name&lang=en)

### 中文

金属复制体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:995`

### English

Metal Facsimiles

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1244`

## origin.6099.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6099.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

第一批合成躯体正从工厂车间滚滚落下，并由其未来的寄居者进行视察。然而，并非每个[Species.GetName]都对这一非凡的发展感到兴奋。[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]的居民已经警告我们，如果我们继续在这条歧路上行进，所有先前的合作努力都将视作无效。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:996`

### English

The first synthetic bodies are rolling off the factory floor and are being inspected by their future inhabitants. However, not every [Species.GetName] is excited about this remarkable development. The inhabitants of [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] have warned us that they will cancel all collaborative efforts if we continue down this route.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1245`

## origin.6099.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6099.a&lang=en)

### 中文

迈向我们的新形态！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:997`

### English

Behold our new forms!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1246`

## origin.6100.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6100.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不完备的完美

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:998`

### English

Partial Perfection

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1247`

## origin.6100.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6100.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetName]的每一名成员都接受了他们新的、机械合成体的形态。然而，[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]的人们则选择留在他们的[fotd_seperatist_planet.Owner.Species.GetFleshy]凡胎肉体之中。此外，他们已经兑现了先前的威胁，结束了与我们的所有合作关系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:999`

### English

Every member of the [Root.GetName] has embraced their new, synthetic forms. However, the population of [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] has chosen to stay in their [fotd_seperatist_planet.Owner.Species.GetFleshy] vessels. Furthermore, they have made good on their threat to end all collaborative efforts with us.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1248`

## origin.6100.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6100.a&lang=en)

### 中文

没能看透他们的[fotd_seperatist_planet.Owner.Species.GetFleshy]囚牢。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1000`

### English

Unable to see past their [fotd_seperatist_planet.Owner.Species.GetFleshy] Prisons.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1249`

## origin.6105.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6105.name&lang=en)

### 中文

迷途的思想

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1002`

### English

Errant Thoughts

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1252`

## origin.6105.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6105.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

没有必要等待不可避免的[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]评判了。我们已经充分意识到了他们愤怒的想法。在所有的不和谐之音中，想要停止一切合作关系的声音尤其突出。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1003`

### English

There is no need to wait for the inevitable judgment of [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] We are fully aware of their agitated thoughts. Amidst the cacophony, their intention to cease all collaboration is crystal clear.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1253`

## origin.6105.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6105.a&lang=en)

### 中文

真是老顽固。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1004`

### English

So close-minded.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1254`

## origin.6110.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6110.name&lang=en)

### 中文

抛弃守旧派

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1005`

### English

Out With The Old

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1255`

## origin.6110.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6110.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们终于同§H虚境§!内的某个大型实体建立了联系。再一次，[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName][GetLamentations]侵扰到了我们；他们正在从我们的网络中断开自己的连接，取消了所有的联合研究行动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1006`

### English

At last we have secured access to one of the grand entities of the §HShroud§!. Once again, the [GetLamentations] of [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] intrude on us; they are disconnecting themselves from our networks, cancelling every joint research operation.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1256`

## origin.6110.desc_2.default

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6110.desc_2.default&lang=en)

### 中文

只有我们才明白这样的行为实际上有多么无足轻重。有一种更开明的伙伴关系即将诞生。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1007`

### English

Only we understand how inconsequential such actions truly are. The birth of a more enlightened partnership is at hand.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1257`

## origin.6110.desc_2.robot

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6110.desc_2.robot&lang=en)

### 中文

这种没有逻辑的行为无足轻重。我们自己的处理能力已经远超他们原始的理解力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1008`

### English

Such illogical actions are inconsequential. Our own processing power has ascended beyond their primitive comprehension.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1258`

## origin.6110.desc_2.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6110.desc_2.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

这种个人主义反常不值得我们关注，无法理解我们开明思想的人在我们中也没有立足之地。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1009`

### English

Such individualist deviancy is beneath our concern, those who cannot comprehend our enlightened thoughts have no place among us.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1259`

## origin.6110.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6110.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的飞升是大势所趋。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1010`

### English

Our ascension is inevitable.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1260`

## origin.6115.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6115.name&lang=en)

### 中文

摒除不完美

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1012`

### English

Abolishing Imperfections

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1263`

## origin.6115.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6115.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

自从我们成功地改造了自己的给养来源，[Root.GetSpeciesName]就幻想着基因强化的无限可能性。现在这一天已经到来。缺陷、弱点、不完美——这些都将成为被抛弃的历史遗留物。\n\n唯一持反对意见的是[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]的居民。他们称这种改造是一次堕落，将不可避免地导致一个由基因完善者统治的阶级主义社会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1013`

### English

Ever since we successfully modified our source of sustenance, [Root.GetSpeciesName] have fantasized about the limitless possibilities of genetic enhancement. Now the day has come. Defects, weaknesses, imperfections - these are all remnants of a discarded past.\n\nThe only naysayers are the inhabitants of [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]. They have called such modification a slippery slope, one that will inevitably lead to a classist society ruled by the genetically perfected.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1264`

## origin.6115.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6115.a&lang=en)

### 中文

允许这些瑕疵继续存在是不负责任的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1014`

### English

Allowing these flaws to persist would be irresponsible.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1265`

## origin.6120.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6120.name&lang=en)

### 中文

发育过度

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1015`

### English

Hypergenesis

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1266`

## origin.6120.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6120.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]拒绝一切形式的通讯。当我们破解了如何将外星基因拼接到我们身体构成中这一秘密以后，他们留给我们的只有一条信息：“你已经放弃了作为[fotd_seperatist_planet.owner.GetSpecesName]的意义”。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1016`

### English

[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] refuses all communication. Once we cracked the secret of splicing alien genetics into our physical makeup, they left us with a single message: "You have abandoned what it means to be [fotd_seperatist_planet.owner.GetSpecesName]"

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1267`

## origin.6120.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6120.a&lang=en)

### 中文

将思维狭隘的人抛到身后。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1017`

### English

Leave the narrow-minded to themselves.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1268`

## origin.6125.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6125.name&lang=en)

### 中文

林外猎手

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1018`

### English

Hunters from Beyond the Forest

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1270`

## origin.6125.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6125.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当整个宇宙面临威胁时，即使是我们隐居的邻国也必须站出来采取行动。只要我们能够抵抗即将到来的风暴，我们将会再次成为同一个民族。\n\n我们已被授予[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]舰队的指挥权。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1019`

### English

When the galaxy is threatened even our reclusive neighbors must act. As long as we can resist the coming storm we will be one people again.\n\nWe have been granted command of the [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] fleet.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1271`

## origin.6125.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6125.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们拒绝被扼杀。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1020`

### English

We refuse to be snuffed out.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1272`

## origin.6145.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6145.name&lang=en)

### 中文

需要确认

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1021`

### English

Confirmation Requested

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1274`

## origin.6145.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6145.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的兄弟行星要求我们对他们的一些发现进行确认。他们再次声称有证据表明，§Y大分裂者§!实际上是被某个满怀恶意的外星实体给摧毁的。这已经变得令人厌烦了；每隔几年，他们似乎都会发现一些新的“证据”。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1022`

### English

Our partner planet has requested that we confirm some of their findings. Once again, they claim to have evidence that §YThe Divider§! was destroyed by a malicious foreign entity. This has become tiresome; every few years it seems they discover some new "proof".

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1275`

## origin.6145.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6145.a&lang=en)

### 中文

让我们逗他们乐乐。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1023`

### English

Let us humor them.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1276`

## origin.6150.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6150.name&lang=en)

### 中文

恐怖的证据

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1024`

### English

Horrific Proof

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1277`

## origin.6150.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6150.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]是对的！\n\n我们多年前所遭受的攻击确实是一个能发射大型钨制弹丸的邪恶实体所为。由于母星系独特的重力场我们才得以幸存。通过分析那个关键夜晚的数据并与这些新的发现进行交叉研究，我们可以定位出这颗小行星大小的钨弹的来源位置。\n\n它来自一个我们在数十年前就已经登记过的格外富矿（但除此之外也没有别的特点）的星系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1025`

### English

[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] were right!\n\nThe attack we suffered all those years ago was perpetrated by a malicious entity capable of launching massive tungsten projectiles. Only the unique gravitational features of our home system allowed us to survive. By analyzing the data from that fateful night and cross-referencing it with these new findings, it was possible for us to triangulate the source of the asteroid-sized tungsten round.\n\nIt originated from an incredibly mineral-rich (yet otherwise unremarkable) system we have had in our logs for decades.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1278`

## origin.6150.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6150.a&lang=en)

### 中文

给我们的超空间驱动器充能。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1026`

### English

Charge up our hyperdrives.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1279`

## origin.6155.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6155.name&lang=en)

### 中文

荒废之地

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1027`

### English

Desolate

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1281`

## origin.6155.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6155.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

曾经侵略过我们的势力所在的恒星系现在是一片不毛之地。虽然一个小行星带缺乏矿物并不罕见，但这个小行星带可以说是已经被剥光了。不仅如此，在应该有富含稀有金属的小行星的地方，我们只发现了灰尘。不管居住在这个恒星系的物种是如何获取他们用于制造子弹的钨的，他们现在已经没有这样的资源了。他们似乎甚至没有能力离开他们的行星。\n\n虽然他们已经尽力保持隐蔽，阻断了所有的无线电信号，甚至限制使用人工照明，但我们还是在该恒星系唯一的宜居行星上探测到了生命。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1028`

### English

The system of our old aggressors is barren. While it isn't unusual for an asteroid belt to be mineral-poor, this one has been stripped bare. Not only that, but where there should be minor planetoids rich in rare metals, we have found only dust. However the species inhabiting this system managed to gain the tungsten necessary to construct their bullets, they no longer have the resources to do so now. They don't even seem to be capable of leaving their planet.\n\nWhile they have done their best to remain hidden, preventing all radio signals and even limiting the use of artificial lights, we have detected life on the system's lone habitable planet.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1282`

## origin.6155.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6155.a&lang=en)

### 中文

让我们好好拜访一下旧日的梦魇。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1029`

### English

Let's pay our old nightmares a visit.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1283`

## origin.6160.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6160.name&lang=en)

### 中文

猎手

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1030`

### English

The Hunters

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1285`

## origin.6160.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6160.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

观察§Y菲沃尔人§!的经历对于我们来说相当有启发性。事实上，他们的意识形态与导致我们自己物种分裂的意识形态非常相似。然而，菲沃尔人没有选择自我隔离，而是选择了对他们能够探测到的任何生命迹象进行先制打击的策略。一旦他们耗尽了重金属，这些外星人就只能回到他们的星球，从他们现在贫瘠的家园中拾掇一切来勉强维持生计。虽然我们伙伴星球的领导层正在敦促我们采取与菲沃尔人相同的策略，但如何最好地对付我们的潜在灭绝者，这事并没有我们原以为的那么简单。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1031`

### English

Observing the §YFevorians§! has been a revelatory experience. In fact, their ideology is remarkably similar to that which produced the split in our own species. However, rather than choosing isolation, the Fevorians opted for a strategy of pre-emptive strikes against any life signs they could detect. Once they ran out of heavy metals, the aliens returned to their world, scraping whatever bare subsistence they could from their now barren home. While the leadership of our partner planet is urging us to adopt the same tactics as the Fevorians, how best to deal with our would-be exterminators is no easy question.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1286`

## origin.6160.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6160.a&lang=en)

### 中文

宇宙中只剩下我们才是安全的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1032`

### English

We will only be safe when we are alone.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1287`

## origin.6160.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6160.a.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会加入我们的兄弟行星，为确保星系的安全将其他生命全部抹除。就从这块石头开始。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1033`

### English

We will join our partner planet in securing this galaxy by expunging all other life. Starting with this rock here.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1288`

## origin.6160.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6160.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们会向他们表示同情。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1034`

### English

We will show them compassion.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1289`

## origin.6160.c.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6160.c.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

衷心希望，他们能够从我们身上学会，不是所有的外星人都要杀个干净。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1035`

### English

Hopefully, they can learn from us that not all alien life is out to kill them.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1290`

## origin.6165.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6165.name&lang=en)

### 中文

拥抱黑暗

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1036`

### English

Embracing the Dark

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1292`

## origin.6165.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6165.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

毁灭菲沃尔人竟是……这么容易？又少了一个威胁到我们生存的猎人。我们已经很久没有感觉到与我们的伙伴星球如此同步了。也许他们是对的。这里只有两种类型的生命：静默的和死亡的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1037`

### English

Destroying the Fevorians was... easy? One fewer hunter, threatening our existence. We have not felt this in sync with our partner world for a long time. Perhaps they were right. There are only two types of life out here: the silent and the dead.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1293`

## origin.6165.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6165.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们应当将他们全部静默。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1038`

### English

We shall silence them all.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1294`

## origin.6165.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6165.b&lang=en)

### 中文

不，我们不会成为侵略者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1039`

### English

No, we will not be the aggressors.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1295`

## origin.6170.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6170.name&lang=en)

### 中文

最后的赠礼

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1040`

### English

The Final Gift

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1297`

## origin.6170.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6170.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y菲沃尔卫星§!比我们最初预期的要更重要。整个星系里仅存的重金属原来全部都在这颗卫星内，进一步研究发现这颗卫星其实是一座巨型的质量投射器。错综复杂的投射机构往返盘绕着整颗小行星。粗略计算表明，这个武器可以将在和发射至高亚光速。\n\n最后次发射的数据仍停留在其火控计算机中，目标就是[Root.GetHomeWorldName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1041`

### English

The §YFevorian Moon§! was more important than we first anticipated. The only heavy metals left in the system are found within the moon, which upon closer examination was revealed to be a titanic mass driver. The sprawling launch mechanism encircles the planetoid multiple times. Rough calculations indicate that the weapon could launch payloads approaching the speed of light.\n\nThe data for the final shot is still in its targeting computer, aimed at [Root.GetHomeWorldName].

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1298`

## origin.6170.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6170.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们必须返回家园！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1042`

### English

We must return home!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1299`

## origin.6175.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6175.name&lang=en)

### 中文

遭到猎杀

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1043`

### English

Hunted

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1301`

## origin.6175.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6175.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y菲沃尔人§!赢了。我们的人口九死一生，家园支离破碎。\n\n直到这颗行星的最后一刻，跨大气层运输船还在将[Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural]不断运往轨道。而来不及离开的人则在高亚光速炮弹击中的瞬间被产生的高温瞬间化为灰烬。[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]愿意接纳我们的政府并安置难民。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1044`

### English

The §YFevorians§! have won. Our people are decimated, and our home world desecrated.\n\nInter-atmosphere ships were ferrying [Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural] into orbit until the planet's final moments. Those who remained on the surface were incinerated the instant the near-lightspeed projectile ignited the atmosphere. [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] has offered to host our government and house the evacuees.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1302`

## origin.6180.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6180.name&lang=en)

### 中文

航线净空

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1045`

### English

Trajectories Clear

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1304`

## origin.6180.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6180.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Target.GetName]已经恢复安全了。我们已经成功将钨弹偏转到了足够远的地方，以确保我们自己能够存活下来。尽管经过其漫长的旅途之后，它很可能成为另一个行星的问题。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1046`

### English

[Target.GetName] is safe. We have managed to divert the tungsten projectile far enough to ensure our own survival, though over the course of its long journey, it may very well become another planet's problem.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1305`

## origin.6180.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6180.a&lang=en)

### 中文

现在，该看看卫星了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1047`

### English

Now, about that moon.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1306`

## origin.6185.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6185.name&lang=en)

### 中文

反制措施

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1048`

### English

Countermeasures

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1308`

## origin.6185.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6185.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的科学家孜孜不倦地针对“§Y菲沃尔问题§!”进行攻关，目前已经提出了四种截然不同的解决方案。如果我们迅速应对，他们相信我们有相当的机会对这个恐怖事物进行偏转。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1049`

### English

Our scientists have been working tirelessly on the "§YFevorian Problem§!" and have come up with four distinct solutions. If we act quickly, they believe we have a fair chance to deflect this terror.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1309`

## origin.6185.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6185.a&lang=en)

### 中文

看着它完成！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1050`

### English

See that it's done!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1310`

## origin.6205.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6205.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[home_planet.GetName]被污染

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1051`

### English

[home_planet.GetName] Tainted

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1312`

## origin.6205.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6205.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]组织的抗议活动在[From.GetAdj]大使馆的正门前发生了骚乱，本应是对多样性和友谊进行庆祝的庆典活动，最后却在酸楚中结束。抗议者并不羞于表达自己的观点，他们喊出了“[From.GetSpeciesNamePluralInsult]滚回垃圾站！”这样丰富多彩的口号。\n\n毫不意外的是，这次事件俨然成为了两国之间外交关系紧张的导火索。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1052`

### English

What should have been a celebration of diversity and friendship ended on a sour note as a protest organized by [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] broke out in front of the [From.GetAdj] embassy. The protestors were not shy about their views, shouting such colorful slogans as "[From.GetSpeciesNamePluralInsult] belong in the recycling plant!"\n\nUnsurprisingly, this has increased diplomatic tensions between our empires.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1313`

## origin.6205.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6205.a&lang=en)

### 中文

立刻谴责[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]并进行道歉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1053`

### English

Immediately reprimand [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] and apologize.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1314`

## origin.6205.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6205.b&lang=en)

### 中文

让他们叫唤。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1054`

### English

Let them chant.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1315`

## origin.6220.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6220.name&lang=en)

### 中文

统一阵线

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1055`

### English

Unified Front

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1317`

## origin.6220.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6220.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[FromFrom.GetName]战争将会被永远铭记，在这一刻我们与[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]并肩作战，携手抗击外来威胁。\n\n面对这一最新的威胁，我们本以为会从[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]的人们那收到嘲笑的信息。但他们的回复却是这样的：\n\n“有哪里需要帮助吗？”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1056`

### English

[FromFrom.GetName] war will forever be remembered as the moment that we and [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] stood together, unified against an existential threat.\n\nConfronted by this latest terror, we expected another dismissive message from the people of [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]. Instead, they responded with five words: \n\n"Where do you need us?"

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1318`

## origin.6220.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6220.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们需要建造更多的舰船！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1057`

### English

We need to build more ships!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1319`

## origin.6220.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6220.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的舰队需要更多的军官！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1058`

### English

We need more officers in our fleets!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1320`

## origin.6220.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6220.c&lang=en)

### 中文

把我们敌人的所有一切都告诉我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1059`

### English

Tell us everything about our enemy.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1321`

## origin.6220.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6220.d&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以独自应付。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1060`

### English

We stand alone.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1322`

## origin.6230.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6230.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]环境改造行动

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1061`

### English

[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] Terraforming Initiative

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1324`

## origin.6230.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6230.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]并不适应[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]上的条件。但得益于细致的环境改造，严苛的环境终将成为历史。\n\n尽管[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]还残留着一些更加困难的环境，但这颗星球的居住者们已经让[From.Target.GetClassAdj]环境彻底成了历史。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1062`

### English

Conditions on [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] have always been inhospitable to [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]. But thanks to diligent terraforming, extreme hardship will become a thing of the past.\n\nWhile [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] remains a somewhat challenging environment, the inhabitants of the world have left their [From.Target.GetClassAdj] past behind.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1325`

## origin.6230.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6230.a&lang=en)

### 中文

希望他们喜欢这些改变。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1063`

### English

May they enjoy the change.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1326`

## origin.6235.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6235.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]环境改造成效卓绝

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1064`

### English

[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] Terraforming Excellence

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1328`

## origin.6235.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6235.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

他们完成了。\n\n我们有些人之前说，[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]的居民简直是疯了才会在那种鬼地方定居。而如今，他们已经将那里改造成了第二个[home_planet.GetName]，吸引着每一个[Root.GetSpeciesName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1065`

### English

They have done it.\n\nSome of our people called the inhabitants of [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] mad for settling a world with such horrendous conditions. Yet, now they have fashioned a more than adequate facsimile of [home_planet.GetName], attractive to any [Root.GetSpeciesName].

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1329`

## origin.6235.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6235.a&lang=en)

### 中文

似乎我们的星球已经不是唯一在[from.Target.GetStarName]有吸引力的地方了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1066`

### English

It seems ours is not the only attractive world in [from.Target.GetStarName] anymore.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1330`

## origin.6240.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6240.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]环境改造臻于完美

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1067`

### English

[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] Terraforming Perfection

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1332`

## origin.6240.desc.changedhome

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6240.desc.changedhome&lang=en)

### 中文

也许是受我们自己在环境改造方面所取得成功的启发，[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]的居民已经将他们的星球塑造成了一件真正的美丽之物。之前就很丰饶的[From.Target.GetClassName]现在已经成为一个盖亚星球。居民们可以继续在天堂里躲避宇宙的威胁。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1068`

### English

Perhaps inspired by our own successes in terraforming, the inhabitants of [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] have sculpted their world into a thing of true beauty. The previously bountiful [From.Target.GetClassName] has become a Gaia World. The inhabitants can continue to hide from the galaxy in paradise.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1333`

## origin.6240.desc.notchangedhome

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6240.desc.notchangedhome&lang=en)

### 中文

来自[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]的消息令人惭愧：当地居民已经突破了[home_planet.GetName]的发展界限。他们摒弃了[From.GetClassName]的形态，成功打造出一颗盖亚星球。面对近在咫尺的理想家园，我们的民众开始质疑：为何还要继续留在这里生活？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1069`

### English

Humiliating news from [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]: the inhabitants have managed to transcend the limits of [home_planet.GetName]. By discarding the form of a [From.GetClassName], they have created a Gaia World. Many of our own people question why they should continue to live here, when a paradise exists on their doorstep.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1334`

## origin.6240.a.changedhome

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6240.a.changedhome&lang=en)

### 中文

想要改善自己的家园，这是非常自然的想法。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1070`

### English

It's only natural to want to improve one's home.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1335`

## origin.6240.a.notchangedhome

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6240.a.notchangedhome&lang=en)

### 中文

[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]让我们的天空失色。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1071`

### English

[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] tarnishes our sky.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1336`

## origin.6260.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6260.name&lang=en)

### 中文

守夜

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1072`

### English

Vigil

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1338`

## origin.6260.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6260.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最近的报告称[fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName]已经部署了一支巡逻舰队，用以保卫[capital_star.GetName]。他们好像已经发展出了隐形科技。好在他们已经同意把频率分享给我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1073`

### English

Recent reports indicate that [fotd_seperatist_planet.GetName] has now deployed a fleet to patrol [capital_star.GetName]. They appear to have developed cloaking technology. Thankfully, they have agreed to share their frequencies with us.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1339`

## origin.6275.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6275.name&lang=en)

### 中文

老猎人

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1074`

### English

Old Hunters

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1341`

## origin.6275.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6275.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

观察者们，你们好。据说我们的祖先曾试图消灭你们。\n\n为此，我们表示歉意。坦率地说，我们很惊讶你们没有进行报复。\n\n从那时起已经过去了许多个世纪，我们愿意相信，我们现在是一个不一样的民族了，毋庸置疑这也要感谢你们。\n\n我们想再给宇宙一次机会。能允许我们和你们一起探索吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1075`

### English

Greetings Observers. It is said that our ancestors tried to end you.\n\nFor that, we apologize. Frankly, we are surprised that you didn't return the favor.\n\nMany centuries have past since then, and we would like to believe that we are a different people now, no doubt thanks to you as well.\n\nWe wish to give the universe a second chance. Would you allow us to explore it with you?

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1342`

## origin.6275.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6275.a&lang=en)

### 中文

可以。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1076`

### English

You may.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1343`

## origin.6275.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aorigin.6275.b&lang=en)

### 中文

你们可以按自己的意愿行事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1077`

### English

You are free to do so on your own.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1344`

## situation_last_gift_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asituation_last_gift_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

菲沃尔人给我们留下了一份可怕的赠别礼物：初步遥测显示，他们的巨型质量驱动器最后一次发射瞄准的目标是[Target.GetName]。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1084`

### English

The Fevorians have left us with a dreadful parting gift: initial telemetry has revealed that the final shot of their titanic mass driver was aimed at [Target.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1353`

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1377`

## fotd_settled_differences_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_settled_differences_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在解决了意识形态上的分歧之后，人们团结一致。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1107`

### English

Having settled their ideological differences, these people stand as one.
