## fotd_alien_shock

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_alien_shock&lang=en)

### 中文

外星冲击

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1108`

### English

Alien Shock

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1384`

## fotd_alien_shock_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_alien_shock_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的兄弟行星在面对外星生命的新闻时显得手足无措、无从应对，他们的这股情感也借助新闻媒体和通讯手段传达到了我们的人民中间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1109`

### English

Our partner planet has reacted poorly to the news of alien life, and their feelings have spread to our people through shared media and communications.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1385`

## fotd_alien_terrified

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_alien_terrified&lang=en)

### 中文

可怕的发现

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1110`

### English

Terrifying Revelation

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1387`

## fotd_alien_terrified_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_alien_terrified_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

或许我们的兄弟行星是对的：这些巨型猛兽的可怕新闻已经让我们两个星球都变得不太稳定。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1111`

### English

Perhaps our partner planet was right: the horrifying news of these titanic beasts has destabilized both our worlds.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1388`

## fotd_refusal_to_work

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_refusal_to_work&lang=en)

### 中文

拒绝工作

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1112`

### English

Refusal to Work

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1390`

## fotd_refusal_to_work_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_refusal_to_work_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

作为对我们近期外交行动的天真般地抗议，我们的邻居中止了一切科学上的合作。当然，这件事肯定很快就平息了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1113`

### English

In a childish protest over recent diplomatic actions, our neighbors have ceased all scientific cooperation. Surely, this will all blow over soon.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1391`

## fotd_forced_to_stay

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_forced_to_stay&lang=en)

### 中文

强制驻留

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1114`

### English

Forced to Stay

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1393`

## fotd_forced_to_stay_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_forced_to_stay_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星上的人们被剥夺了离开的机会。自不必说，在要求进行迁移之后，这样的结果肯定无法令人满意。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1115`

### English

The people on this planet were denied the opportunity to leave. Needless to say, after requesting relocation, they are not satisfied with this outcome.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1394`

## fotd_disunity

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_disunity&lang=en)

### 中文

不团结

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1116`

### English

Disunity

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1396`

## fotd_disunity_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_disunity_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的伙伴行星相信，当前的政治局势是无法避免的。他们不遗余力地提醒我们这个事实。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1117`

### English

Our neighboring planet believes the current political situation was an inevitability. They have wasted no time in reminding us of this fact.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1397`

## fotd_unified_stance

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_unified_stance&lang=en)

### 中文

团结一致

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1118`

### English

Unified Stance

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1399`

## fotd_unified_stance_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_unified_stance_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这代表着一件两个星球都要面临的威胁。安内之前，必须先攘外。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1119`

### English

This represents a threat to both our worlds. It must be dealt with before returning to our internal discourse.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1400`

## fotd_unified_purpose

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_unified_purpose&lang=en)

### 中文

众志成城

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1120`

### English

Unified Purpose

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1402`

## fotd_unified_purpose_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_unified_purpose_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

有某个存在正在威胁我们的星系。我们必须对抗它。作为一个国家。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1121`

### English

Something threatens our galaxy. We will stand against it. As one.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1403`

## slowed_science_ships

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aslowed_science_ships&lang=en)

### 中文

探索延期

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1122`

### English

Exploration Put on Hold

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1405`

## slowed_science_ships_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aslowed_science_ships_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于与我们的兄弟行星所签订的科研协议，我们要暂停探索新星球了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1123`

### English

As a result of the research pact with our partner planet, for the time being our exploration of new worlds will be put on hold.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1406`

## slowed_first_contact

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aslowed_first_contact&lang=en)

### 中文

第一次接触停滞

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1124`

### English

First Contact Stalled

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1408`

## slowed_first_contact_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aslowed_first_contact_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

作为与我们兄弟行星交换新科技的代价，我们愿意暂停目前的第一次接触项目。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1125`

### English

In exchange for new technologies from our partner planet, we have agreed to cease our current First Contact projects.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1409`

## supporting_haven

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asupporting_haven&lang=en)

### 中文

公平交易

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1126`

### English

Fair Exchange

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1411`

## supporting_haven_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asupporting_haven_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们得到了新科技，他们则有了更多的资源。这笔交易很公平。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1127`

### English

We get new technologies, they get more resources. A fair enough trade.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1412`

## promised_no_shroud

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apromised_no_shroud&lang=en)

### 中文

承诺不再深入研究

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1128`

### English

Promised not to Probe Deeper

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1414`

## promised_no_shroud_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apromised_no_shroud_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经向我们的兄弟行星承诺，不对神秘的灵能力量或科技进行进一步的深入探索。他们担心我们会迷失自我。真是杞人忧天。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1129`

### English

We have promised our partner planet not to delve any deeper into the mysteries of psionic power or technology. They are worried we might lose ourselves. Such childlike anxiety.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1415`

## have_no_voice

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahave_no_voice&lang=en)

### 中文

噤声

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1130`

### English

Voiceless

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1417`

## have_no_voice_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ahave_no_voice_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的外交影响力被严重削弱了。目前，我们必须在星系舞台上噤声。毕竟我们换到的科技还是更有所值的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1131`

### English

Our diplomatic clout has been severely curtailed. For the time being, we must remain voiceless on the galactic stage. The technology we received in exchange had better be worth it.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1418`

## ruined_mass_driver

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aruined_mass_driver&lang=en)

### 中文

损毁的物质投射炮

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1132`

### English

Ruined Mass Driver

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1420`

## ruined_mass_driver_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aruined_mass_driver_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

巨大的$NAME_fotd_Hunter_species$星系级物质投射炮的遗址。尽管已经无法运作，但对其进行研究仍能增进我们对弹道学的理解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1133`

### English

The remains of the great $NAME_fotd_Hunter_species$ galactic mass driver. While no longer functional, studying it should enhance our understanding of ballistics.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1421`

## fotd_secure_contact

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_secure_contact&lang=en)

### 中文

稳妥的第一次接触

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1134`

### English

Secure First Contact

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1423`

## fotd_secure_contact_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_secure_contact_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们有理由相信我们的第一次接触工作受到了负面影响。从现在起我们必须更加小心翼翼。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1135`

### English

We have reason to believe that our first contact efforts have been compromised. We must be more careful from now on.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1424`

## fotd_assited_shipyards

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_assited_shipyards&lang=en)

### 中文

船坞援助

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1136`

### English

Shipyard Assistance

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1426`

## fotd_assited_shipyards_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_assited_shipyards_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

得益于我们兄弟行星的帮助，船坞目前以110%的效率运行中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1137`

### English

Shipyards are running at 110% efficiency thanks to assistance from our partner planet.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1427`

## fotd_assisted_fleet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_assisted_fleet&lang=en)

### 中文

满员舰队

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1138`

### English

Fully Crewed Fleets

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1429`

## fotd_assisted_fleet_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_assisted_fleet_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过与我们的兄弟行星共事，我们的舰船以前所未有的效率运作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1139`

### English

By working together with our partner planet, our ships are moving like never before.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1430`

## fotd_unified_fleet

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_unified_fleet&lang=en)

### 中文

联合舰队

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1140`

### English

Unified Fleets

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1432`

## fotd_unified_fleet_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_unified_fleet_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的舰队运作效率无与伦比，感谢我们兄弟行星的军官团。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1141`

### English

Our ships are maneuvering better than ever, thanks to our partner planet's officer corps.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1433`

## fotd_terrified_outraged

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_terrified_outraged&lang=en)

### 中文

恐惧与愤怒

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1142`

### English

Terrified & Outraged

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1435`

## fotd_terrified_outraged_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_terrified_outraged_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的举措使得一些我们物种的成员嘲讽我们不再是真的[Root.Species.GetName]了，但他们的观点不值一提。我们无所谓指指点点。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1143`

### English

Our actions have led some members of our species to deride us as no longer truly [Root.Species.GetName], but their opinion is inconsequential. We stand above judgment now.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1436`

## fotd_extorted_seperatists

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_extorted_seperatists&lang=en)

### 中文

分离主义者受勒索

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1144`

### English

Extorted Separatists

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1438`

## fotd_extorted_seperatists_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_extorted_seperatists_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然我们是以强硬的方式获得了这些资源，但这又有什么不好呢？有些人只是比别人需要更多的说服力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1145`

### English

We may have strong-armed our way into these resources, but what's the harm? Some people just take more convincing than others.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1439`

## fotd_rationed_living

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_rationed_living&lang=en)

### 中文

配给生活

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1146`

### English

Rationed Living

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1441`

## fotd_rationed_living_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_rationed_living_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在经历了一场灾难性的赤字后，我们的兄弟行星现在需要削减非必要的机构。所以我们的文化交流项目黄了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1147`

### English

After experiencing a devastating deficit, our partner planet must now make cuts to non-essential institutions. As a result, our cultural exchange projects have deteriorated.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1442`

## fotd_triumviri

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_triumviri&lang=en)

### 中文

三执政之一

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1148`

### English

Triumviri

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1444`

## fotd_triumviri_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_triumviri_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然我们的伙伴星球为我们提供了技术和战术方面的见解，但菲沃尔人独特的历史视角让我们能以新的角度来看待自己的社会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1149`

### English

While our partner planet provides us with insights into technology and tactics, the Fevorians' unique historical perspective has provided a new lens through which to view our own society.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1445`

## fotd_chain_title

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_chain_title&lang=en)

### 中文

黑暗之怖

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1151`

### English

Fear of the Dark

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1449`

## fotd_chain_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_chain_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

作为技术领先的敌人发动无端侵略的受害者，我们学到了疏忽大意会付出的代价。这种事情不会再发生了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1152`

### English

As the victim of an unprovoked act of aggression by a technologically superior foe, we understand the cost of negligence. Never again.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1450`

## fotd_hunter_poi

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_hunter_poi&lang=en)

### 中文

猎手星系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1153`

### English

Hunter System

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1451`

## fotd_hunter_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afotd_hunter_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这里就是粉碎了§Y大分裂者§!的那次攻击发起的星系。扫描显示这个恒星系异常地富有矿物。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1154`

### English

The system from which the attack that shattered §YThe Divider§! originated. Scans indicate that the system is abnormally rich in minerals.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1452`

## FOTD_CONFIRM_FINDINGS

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AFOTD_CONFIRM_FINDINGS&lang=en)

### 中文

确认发现

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1155`

### English

Confirm Findings

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1454`

## FOTD_CONFIRM_FINDINGS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AFOTD_CONFIRM_FINDINGS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

击中大分裂者的那颗小行星异常地富含钨元素，许多科学家声称他们已经“证实”这是一起对我们恒星系的攻击。遗憾的是，我们与之的协议意味着我们必须迎合这些胡思乱想。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1156`

### English

The asteroid that hit the Divider contained an abnormal amount of tungsten, and several scientists now claim to have "proof" that this was an attack on our solar system. Sadly, our agreements with their world mean we will have to humor these fancies.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1455`

## FOTD_DEFLECTING_FIELDS

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AFOTD_DEFLECTING_FIELDS&lang=en)

### 中文

偏转力场

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1157`

### English

Deflecting Fields

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1457`

## FOTD_DEFLECTING_FIELDS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AFOTD_DEFLECTING_FIELDS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

假使有足够时间的话，单一艘科研船就可以构建一个涨落力场来改变钨导弹的矢量。但光是这个可能还不够。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1158`

### English

A lone science ship could, given enough time, establish a fluctuating force field to change the vector of the tungsten missile. But that in itself may not be enough.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1458`

## FOTD_REVERSED_THRUSTERS

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AFOTD_REVERSED_THRUSTERS&lang=en)

### 中文

反向推进器

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1159`

### English

Reversed Thrusters

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1460`

## FOTD_REVERSED_THRUSTERS_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AFOTD_REVERSED_THRUSTERS_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

特别改造的鱼雷可以通过编程将自身附着在这些炮弹上，通过反向推进器把这份“惊喜”的速度降下来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1160`

### English

Specially modified torpedoes could be programmed to attach themselves to the projectile; by reversing their thrust, they might just slow this 'gift' down.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1461`

## FOTD_COUNTER_SHOT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AFOTD_COUNTER_SHOT&lang=en)

### 中文

反制射击

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1161`

### English

Counter Shot

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1463`

## FOTD_COUNTER_SHOT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AFOTD_COUNTER_SHOT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管在我们的恒星系里没有什么比这个钨质小行星的密度更大，但太空石块还是有不少的。即使只能以亚光速发射，但用另一颗小行星砸上去应该能缓解这场危机。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1162`

### English

While we don't have anything as dense as a tungsten asteroid in our system, we have no shortage of space rocks. Even at less than lightspeed, firing a counter asteroid at the current threat might help.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1464`

## FOTD_CONTROLLED_SHATTERING

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AFOTD_CONTROLLED_SHATTERING&lang=en)

### 中文

受控碎裂

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1163`

### English

Controlled Shattering

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1466`

## FOTD_CONTROLLED_SHATTERING_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AFOTD_CONTROLLED_SHATTERING_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

把这颗钨质炮弹直接炸毁好像是个不错的主意，但产生的碎片仍然会对我们的行星造成毁灭性打击。我们的科学家已经计算出了一个受控破拆流程，也许可以将这颗炮弹拆分成更好处理的形式。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1164`

### English

Blowing up the tungsten bullet might seem appealing, but the resulting shrapnel would still have a catastrophic impact on our world. Our scientists have calculated a controlled detonation sequence that might help fracture the projectile into a slightly more manageable form.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1467`

## fotdCoercion1

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AfotdCoercion1&lang=en)

### 中文

为了诱使他们多留几年，我们得给予一些激励。不要小看蝇头小利的力量。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1166`

### English

To entice them to stay on a few more years, we might offer an incentive. Never underestimate the power of a well-placed bribe.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1472`

## fotdCoercion2

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AfotdCoercion2&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以提醒他们这里乐趣多多，或许可以提供一些具体的例子来鼓励他们留下来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1167`

### English

We could remind them of the many pleasures here, and perhaps provide a few discrete examples as an incentive to stay.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1473`

## fotdCoercion3

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AfotdCoercion3&lang=en)

### 中文

好吧，如果他们坚持要回家的话我们也别无选择，只能告知他们的政府他们参与了违法的异星关系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1168`

### English

Well, if they insist on returning home, we have no choice but to inform their government about the illicit xeno-relations they were engaging in.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1474`

## fotdCoercion4

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AfotdCoercion4&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以预留政府津贴来资助这个项目。这应该能让他们分心一段时间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1169`

### English

We could set aside a government stipend to fund this project. That should keep them distracted for a while longer.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1475`

## a_voidcraft

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aa_voidcraft&lang=en)

### 中文

宇航技术

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1170`

### English

a voidcraft

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1477`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aa_computing&lang=en)

### 中文

计算技术

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1171`

### English

a computing

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1478`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aa_field_manipulation&lang=en)

力场操控

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1172`

a field manipulation

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aa_particle_physics&lang=en)

### 中文

粒子物理

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1173`

### English

a particle physics

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1480`

## a_biology

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aa_biology&lang=en)

### 中文

生物学

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1174`

### English

a biology

## first_contact.3000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.3000.name&lang=en)

### 中文

来客

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1255`

### English

The Guest

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1582`

## first_contact.3000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.3000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

如楼房大小、长着许多触手的怪物突然出现在§Y[This.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!让首都陷入了一片混乱。这只生物似乎和我们一样困惑于它自己的突然出现，毫无抵抗地就被我们捕获拘束了起来。\n\n许多[owner.GetScientistPlural]宣称，代号§H来客§!的这个生物是来自外太空，而其他人则认为这是一种此前尚未发现的物种直至今日才得以为人所知。无论如何，轨道上确实没有发现任何外星舰船。这个生物的凭空出现，似乎也不是借助的科技手段。\n\n令人困惑（同时它自己也充满困惑）的客人目前被收容在一座研究设施中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1256`

### English

The appearance of a building-sized, tentacled monstrosity in the midst of §Y[This.Owner.Capital.GetName]§! has thrown the capital into chaos. The creature seems as confused as we are by its sudden appearance, and has allowed itself to be restrained without resistance.\n\nWhile many [owner.GetScientistPlural] already claim the creature, codenamed §HThe Guest§!, to be of alien nature, others have suggested it could be a previously unknown species which had until now gone undiscovered. After all, no alien ship has been detected in orbit. The creature materialized out of nowhere, seemingly unassisted by technology.\n\nOur confusing (and confused) visitor is currently being held in one of our research facilities.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1583`

## first_contact.3000.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.3000.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

如楼房大小、长着许多触手的怪物突然出现在§Y[This.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!让首都失去了秩序。这个生物对于它自己的突然出现似乎和我们一样困惑，毫无抵抗地就被我们捕获拘束了起来。\n\n我们怀疑该生物应该来自外太空，但无法排除是一种本就在我们星球上的未知物种的可能性。无论如何，轨道上确实没有发现任何外星舰船。这个代号§H来客§!的生物的凭空出现，似乎也不是借助的科技手段。\n\n令人困惑（同时它自己也充满困惑）的客人目前被收容在一座研究设施中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1257`

### English

The appearance of a building-sized, tentacled monstrosity in the midst of §Y[This.Owner.Capital.GetName]§! has thrown the capital into disarray. The creature seems as perplexed as we are by its sudden appearance, and has allowed itself to be restrained without resistance.\n\nWe suspect the creature to be of alien nature, but we can't discount the possibility of it being an unknown species native to our world. After all, no alien ship has been detected in orbit. The creature - codenamed §HThe Guest§! - materialized out of nowhere, seemingly unassisted by technology.\n\nOur confusing (and confused) visitor is currently being held in one of our research facilities.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1584`

## fircon.1220.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1220.name&lang=en)

### 中文

食物中毒

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1270`

### English

Food Poisoning

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1599`

## fircon.1220.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1220.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们没能成功地给这位神秘访客提供合适的营养：该生物很快就生病了，它拒绝餐食，还拒绝作出任何应答。\n\n在我们能够进一步了解它的新陈代谢机制之前，通讯行为暂时停止。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1271`

### English

Our attempts to provide our mysterious visitor with suitable nourishment have failed: the creature quickly sickened, expelled the meal, and is currently unresponsive.\n\nCommunication efforts are on hold until we can gain a better understanding of its metabolism.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1600`

## fircon.1221.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1221.name&lang=en)

### 中文

外交事故

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1272`

### English

Diplomatic Accident

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1601`

## fircon.1221.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1221.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

该生物对我们的尝试性联络反应激烈，猛烈地袭击了一位我们的科学家，并拒绝进一步的合作。也许我们无意见冒犯了它。\n\n尽管没有人严重受伤，但是这次事故依然对迄今为止我们同这个生物建立的脆弱的信任造成了损伤。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1273`

### English

The creature has reacted violently to our communication attempts, lashing out at one of our scientists and refusing further collaboration. We believe we have unintentionally offended it.\n\nWhile no one was seriously injured, the accident has damaged the fragile trust we have so far built up with the creature.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1602`

## fircon.1210.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1210.name&lang=en)

### 中文

建立联络

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1275`

### English

Communication Established

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1605`

## fircon.1210.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1210.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[owner.GetScientistPlural]研究后得知，这位客人扭曲触手的动作其实是一种复杂的语言形式。\n\n于是乎我们沮丧地了解到这个生物正因为衣不蔽体而感到尴尬，正在反复地请求合适的衣物以遮蔽其赤裸的躯体。\n\n在得体地遮蔽躯体后，我们的研究者建造了一台多肢机器以便与这个生物交流。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1276`

### English

Our [owner.GetScientistPlural] has determined the wriggling tentacle motions of our guest are a form of sophisticated sign language.\n\nTo our dismay, we thus learned that the creature is embarrassed about its lack of clothing and has been repeatedly asking for appropriate garments to cover its naked appendages.\n\nAfter it's suitably clad, our researchers have proposed the construction of a many-tentacled machine to better aid communication with the creature.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1606`

## fircon.1211.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1211.name&lang=en)

### 中文

万爪之母

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1277`

### English

The Many-Tentacled One

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1607`

## fircon.1211.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1211.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的这位访客十分健谈，但文化差异让我们难以相互理解。现在我们知道了该生物为雌性，她称自己为§H“万爪之母”§!，似乎隶属于某个它称之为§Y薄幕行者§!的唯心主义教团。但我们仍然不明白这个她一直唠唠叨叨不停的§Y“山现”§!是什么，或者它和她的突然出现之间有什么因果关系。\n\n我们继续尝试同其进行沟通。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1278`

### English

Our guest is very talkative, but cultural differences are making it hard for us to understand each other. We now know that the creature is female, refers to herself as §H"The Many-Tentacled One"§! and seems to be a member of a spiritual order it calls the §YVeilfarers§!. But we still don't understand what this §Y'Jaunt'§! She keeps mentioning is, or how it can be responsible for her sudden appearance.\n\nOur communication efforts continue.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1608`

## fircon.1212.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1212.name&lang=en)

### 中文

迷失旅行者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1279`

### English

Lost Traveler

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1609`

## fircon.1212.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1212.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的研究人员终于拼凑出了“万爪之母”的故事：我们的宾客是一位专注于研究§Y闪现§!的唯心主义教团的领袖：这是一种允许在宇宙中任何地方瞬间传送的灵能技术。纵观她的物种的历史，从来没有人成功地进行过闪现……直到现在。\n\n万爪之母不确定她是否有能力复制这一壮举，更何况她的能量储备目前已经耗尽，无法再做尝试。\n\n当前，她愿意继续接受我们的照顾，并将通过帮助我们的研究人员来回报我们的热情款待。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1280`

### English

Our researchers have finally pieced together the Many-Tentacled One's story: our guest is the head of a spiritual order focused on studying the §YJaunt§!: a psionic technique that allows for instantaneous teleportation anywhere in the universe. Throughout the history of her species, no one has ever managed to perform a successful Jaunt... Until now.\n\nThe Many-Tentacled One is unsure about her ability to replicate the feat, and her energy reserves are currently too depleted to make another attempt.\n\nFor now, she would like to remain in our care, and will repay our hospitality by aiding our researchers.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1610`

## fircon.1230.desc.outro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1230.desc.outro&lang=en)

### 中文

学习§H闪现§!，即§H万爪之母§!用来抵达我们母星的传送技术。\n\n这位笼罩着神秘主义色彩的太空生命愿意分享她的知识，尽管她也不确定我们是否能够复制这项技术。我们首都的一座合适的建筑已经准备好被改造成一座研究用的神殿。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1290`

### English

learning the §HJaunt§!, the teleportation technique used by the §HMany-Tentacled One§! to reach our homeworld.\n\nThe space-faring mystic is willing to share her knowledge, though she doubts we will be able to replicate the technique. A suitable building in our capital is ready to be converted into a temple facility for research purposes.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1620`

## fircon.1231.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1231.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的万爪之母最近表达了回归家乡的渴望，但我们难以定位她的母星系：她所描述的天体特征不符合我们所知星系的任何一个区域。\n\n我们的访客凝视着夜空，久久不曾挪开视线。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1297`

### English

Our Many-Tentacled One has recently expressed a desire to return to her people, but our efforts to locate her home system have proven fruitless: the celestial features she described don't correspond to any known part of the galaxy.\n\nOur guest's gaze remains fixed on the night sky.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1627`

## fircon.1231.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1231.a&lang=en)

### 中文

再加把劲。我们会找到她的家乡的！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1298`

### English

Redouble our efforts. We'll find her home!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1628`

## fircon.1231.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1231.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们这不就是她的家吗。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1299`

### English

This is her home now.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1629`

再会

### English

Goodbye

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1631`

## fircon.1232.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1232.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

万爪之母无处可寻。这个生物突然地出现，现在却又突然地消失了，仅仅在研究设施的地板上留下了一条匆忙划下的告别信息。\n\n“§H我很害怕，但我必须要闪现离开了。我心中的思乡之情无处排解，苦痛难耐。感谢你们所做的一切。我祈祷薄幕会善待我。§!”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1302`

### English

The Many-Tentacled One is nowhere to be found. The creature disappeared as abruptly as she appeared, leaving a hastily scratched goodbye message on the floor of the research facility:\n\n'§HI'm scared, but I have to Jaunt. My hearts ache with thoughts of home. Thank you for everything. I pray the Veil will be kind to me.§!'

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1632`

### English

迟来的道谢

### English

A Belated Thank You

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1635`

## fircon.1233.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1233.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的一艘舰船截获了一个突然凭空出现在我们母星系的储藏箱。在我们的物种一接触到它的瞬间，上面复杂的锁就自动解开了，展露出了其中令人瞠目咋舌的大量工艺品……还有一张来自多肢旅行者的感谢纸条，其中声称她在几十年前曾经造访过我们的母星。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1306`

### English

One of our ships has intercepted a cache that suddenly materialized in our home system. The complex lock gave way as soon as a member of our species touched it, revealing a dizzying collection of artifacts... And a thank-you note from a Many-Tentacled traveler who claims to have visited our home planet decades ago.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1636`

## THE_JAUNT_PROJECT

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATHE_JAUNT_PROJECT&lang=en)

学习闪现

Learning to Jaunt

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1639`

## THE_JAUNT_PROJECT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ATHE_JAUNT_PROJECT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

§H万爪之母§!在我们首都的凭空出现证明了“灵能传送”这一曾经只存在于理论层面的概念是实操上可行的。通过让我们最能干的[owner.GetScientistPlural]投入研究工作，也许就可以学会这种瞬间旅行方式的秘密。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1309`

### English

The sudden appearance of the §HMany-Tentacled One§! on our capital has proven that the once theoretical concept of 'psionic teleportation' is workable in practice. By putting our most effective [owner.GetScientistPlural] on the job, it might be possible for us to learn the secrets of instantaneous interstellar travel.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1640`

## fircon.1240.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1240.name&lang=en)

学习闪现

### English

## fircon.1240.desc.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1240.desc.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的[owner.GetScientistPlural]花费了数不尽的时间试图掌握灵能传送的秘密，终于，其中的一位成功地进行了§H闪现§!！\n\n这位研究人员传送了一米，但因为传送至地板内导致失去了

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1312`

### English

After spending many years trying to master the secrets of psionic teleportation, one of our [owner.GetScientistPlural] finally managed to §HJaunt§! successfully.\n\nThe researcher teleported a short distance but lost

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1643`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1313`

### English

$fircon.1240.desc.intro$ their lower appendages as a result of partly teleporting inside a floor. Much clapping and some screaming ensued.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1644`

## fircon.1241.desc.intro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1241.desc.intro&lang=en)

### 中文

又一次成功§H闪现§!了！根据我们的传感器数据，我们的一位[owner.GetScientistPlural]成功地传送到了离我们最近的恒星系，在几分之一秒内跨越了不可估量的距离。只是这个倒霉蛋在一颗恒星内实体化，并瞬间化为灰烬。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1317`

### English

Another successful §HJaunt§!. According to our sensor data, one of our [owner.GetScientistPlural] managed to teleport to our closest neighboring solar system, crossing incalculable distances in a fraction of a second... Only to materialize inside a star and become instantly incinerated.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1648`

## fircon.1241.desc.outro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1241.desc.outro&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然建造灵能的推进的引擎存在可能性，但这种技术应用起来可能会难以置信地危险，甚至会给宇宙带来混乱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1318`

### English

While the construction of a psionic propulsion drive might be possible, such technology could be incredibly dangerous to use and may bring chaos to the galaxy.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1649`

## fircon.1241.desc.non_gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1241.desc.non_gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$fircon.1241.desc.intro$\n\n使用这种技术来推进舰船讨论遭到了深刻的质疑。$fircon.1241.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1319`

### English

$fircon.1241.desc.intro$\n\nTalks of using this technique to propel ships have been met with profound skepticism. $fircon.1241.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1650`

## fircon.1241.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1241.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$fircon.1241.desc.intro$\n\n目前，使用这种技术来推进舰船的前景还停留在理论阶段。$fircon.1241.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1320`

### English

$fircon.1241.desc.intro$\n\nThe prospect of using this technique to propel ships remains purely theoretical for now. $fircon.1241.desc.outro$

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1651`

## fircon.1242.desc.base.outro

### 中文

它实在太不精确，根本带不来任何可行的好处。我们越是尝试，就越清楚地意识到，§H万爪之母§!的到来完全是随缘。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1322`

### English

on the first Jaunt when they materialize inside a rock, a star, or even a black hole. It is too imprecise to provide any workable benefits. The more we try to Jaunt, the clearer it becomes that the arrival of the §HMany-Tentacled One§! was entirely random.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1653`

## fircon.1242.desc.base.intro.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1242.desc.base.intro.gestalt&lang=en)

在对§H闪现§!进行了深入的研究后，我们得出的结论是其毫无实用价值。我们的[GetResearchers]们可能要花费数十年来学会这项技术，结果也只会是因传送入石头、恒星甚至是一颗黑洞的首次闪现

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1323`

### English

After extensive study of the §HJaunt§!, we have concluded that its use value is zero. Our [GetResearchers] could spend decades learning to master the technique, only to

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1654`

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1324`

## fircon.1243.nodrive.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1243.nodrive.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

然而，通过通过将闪现的原理应用到飞船工程而不是单个躯体之中，我们的[GetResearchers]距离造出§H$tech_psi_jump_drives$§!仅有一步之遥，将灵能传送变成了一个更可靠、更安全的手段。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1336`

### English

However, by applying the principles of the Jaunt to ship engineering rather than individual bodies, our [GetResearchers] are one step closer to building a §H$tech_psi_jump_drives$§! and turning psionic teleportation into a more reliable, marginally safer endeavor.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1667`

## fircon.1243.drive.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1243.drive.desc&lang=en)

尽管如此，我们获得的知识也会有一些工程应用。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1337`

### English

Nonetheless, the knowledge we gained will have some engineering applications.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1668`

## awareness.910.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aawareness.910.name&lang=en)

### 中文

暴露！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1365`

### English

Exposed!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1703`

## awareness.910.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aawareness.910.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们一直明目张胆地在§Y[contact_empire_system.GetName]§!星系进行行动，对于隐藏自己的行动丝毫没有任何注意。尽管他们的技术水平不高，但是即使是§Y[contact_empire.GetName]§!也注意到了我们的行动。现在第一次接触不可避免了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1366`

### English

We have been careless, conducting operations in the §Y[contact_empire_system.GetName]§! system without bothering to hide our activities. Despite their modest technology level, even the §Y[contact_empire.GetName]§! have taken notice of our activity. First Contact is now unavoidable.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1704`

## awareness.920.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aawareness.920.name&lang=en)

### 中文

谁在盯着观察者？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1367`

### English

Who Watches the Watchers?

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1706`

## awareness.920.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aawareness.920.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们围绕§Y[fromfrom.GetName]§!建造了一座观测站来研究上面的居民，但却意识到§Y[contact_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§!反而是在研究我们：他们完全清楚其他外星物种的存在，并很快注意到了我们[root.GetScientistPlural]的到来。\n\n我们先前在这个恒星系的活动一定已经让[fromfrom.owner.GetName]对我们的存在有所警觉。当前情况下，保持我们的存在隐形化已经没有什么意义。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1368`

### English

We built an observation post around §Y[fromfrom.GetName]§! to study its inhabitants, only to realize the §Y[contact_empire.GetSpeciesNamePlural]§! are the ones studying us: they are fully aware of the existence of other alien species, and soon noticed the arrival of our [root.GetScientistPlural].\n\nOur previous activity in this solar system must have alerted the [fromfrom.owner.GetName] to our presence. Efforts to keep our existence hidden are futile at this point.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1707`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3AAWARENESS_MONTHLY_VALUE_DELAYED&lang=en)

### 中文

§E这个文明的觉醒度在上一个月会改变这么多量。\n\n觉醒度会受到多个方面的影响，包括恒星系内空间活动，以及我们同某个假定文明的互动。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1371`

### English

§EThis civilization's Awareness changed by this amount in the previous month.\n\nAwareness is affected by various factors including in-system spatial activity, and our own interactions with a given civilization.§!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1711`
