## fircon.1256.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1256.name&lang=en)

### 中文

温顺好生养

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1410`

### English

Tractable and Fertile

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1760`

## fircon.1256.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1256.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!上的居民通过试用富营养肥料提高了他们的农业水平。他们能够收获到更高质量的农作物产品，社会的整体健康也得到改善。\n\n考虑到他们简陋的技术，这种粮食生产的进步是非常显著的。获得化肥样品后，我们就可以在更大范围内复刻这一成就。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1411`

### English

The inhabitants of §Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§! have enhanced their agricultural skills through experimentation with nutrient-rich fertilizers. Their harvests are yielding higher quality produce and the health of their society is improving.\n\nGiven their rudimentary technology, this advancement in food production is remarkable. Obtaining a sample of the fertilizer should allow us to replicate the achievement on a wider scale.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1761`

## fircon.1256.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1256.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们将继续从远处监视他们的努力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1412`

### English

We will continue to monitor their efforts from a distance.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1762`

## fircon.1256.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1256.b&lang=en)

### 中文

派一支队伍去获取样本。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1413`

### English

Send a team to obtain a sample.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1763`

## fircon.1257.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1257.name&lang=en)

### 中文

温顺好生养：已研究样品

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1414`

### English

Tractable and Fertile: Samples Studied

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1764`

## fircon.1257.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1257.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对从§Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!上收集到的肥料样本的分析获得了对前超光速文明农业行为的重要见解，其中的一部分甚至新到可以增进我们自己的技术。大家纷纷表示，这次努力物有所值，任务大获成功。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1415`

### English

Our analysis of collected fertilizer samples from §Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§! has provided valuable insights into pre-FTL agricultural practices, some so novel that they could actually bolster our own techniques. By all accounts, this was a worthwhile endeavor, and a successful mission.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1765`

## fircon.1261.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1261.name&lang=en)

### 中文

饥荒！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1416`

### English

Famine!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1766`

## fircon.1261.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1261.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!上的定居点一直在经历着农业困境。最近的农作物歉收可能会导致争夺食物的冲突。对于一个年轻的、发展中的社会，这是一个关键时刻。\n\n无论他们是选择在需要的时候团结在一起，还是反目成仇，他们现在的决定对于未来朝着哪个方向发展都会产生很大的影响。\n\n不幸的是，无论采取哪种方式，他们都将进入一个极端困难的时期。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1417`

### English

The settlements on §Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§! have been experiencing agricultural difficulties. Recent crop failures could lead to conflicts over food. This is a critical moment in the development of a young society.\n\nWhether they choose to come together in their time of need, or to turn against each other, their decisions now will have a strong bearing on who they ultimately become.\n\nUnfortunately, no matter which course is taken, they are entering a period of extreme hardship.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1767`

## fircon.1261.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1261.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不会打扰他们的自然发展历程。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1418`

### English

We will not interfere with the natural course of their development.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1768`

## fircon.1261.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1261.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在道义上应该帮助。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1419`

### English

We have a moral obligation to help.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1769`

## fircon.1261.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1261.c&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以降低他们对食物的需求……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1420`

### English

We will reduce their need for food...

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1770`

## fircon.1262.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1262.name&lang=en)

### 中文

饥荒结束了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1421`

### English

The Famine is Over!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1771`

## fircon.1262.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1262.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们最近对§Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!的干预产生了正面的结果。利用我们自己的农业技术，我们暗中确保了他们不会遭受饥荒。\n\n现在，他们有了盈余的食物，所以我们必须约束自己的行径，限制干预的程度。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1422`

### English

Our recent intervention on §Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§! yielded positive results. Utilizing our own agricultural technologies, we covertly ensured they would not starve.\n\nNow, they have a surplus of food, so it is only right that we limit the extent of our interference by taking some for ourselves.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1772`

## fircon.1271.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1271.name&lang=en)

### 中文

洞穴壁画！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1423`

### English

Cave Paintings!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1773`

## fircon.1271.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1271.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!上的居民持续地在他们的公共生活空间中创造出原始的、艺术化的代表作品，我们注意到其中有些图像开始显露出我们的轮廓。\n\n现在看来，我们在这颗星球上的所作所为很可能已经引起了注意。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1424`

### English

As the inhabitants of §Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§! continue to create crude, artistic representations in their communal living spaces, we have noticed some images have begun to look like us.\n\nIt now seems likely that our efforts on the planet have attracted notice.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1774`

## fircon.1271.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1271.a&lang=en)

### 中文

他们描绘了我们的样子。不可思议！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1425`

### English

They have captured our likeness. Fascinating!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1775`

## fircon.1271.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1271.b&lang=en)

### 中文

这画的什么东西。给我抹了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1426`

### English

Their scribbles insult us. Remove them!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1776`

## fircon.1271.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1271.c&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经污染了他们的文化。以后得多加小心。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1427`

### English

We have contaminated their culture. Take more care in future.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1777`

## fircon.1285.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1285.name&lang=en)

### 中文

写在星间！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1428`

### English

Written in the Stars!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1778`

## fircon.1285.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1285.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在§Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!上，居民们正在使用原始的雕刻图画来表达和分享想法。我们的研究人员记录的样本表明，居民对他们自己星球以外的天体有认识，甚至已经开始记录星座。\n\n对于这样一个年轻的文明来说，他们早期绘制的星图是相当准确的，但也不至于到了无法改进的地步，只要我们想留下一些暗示……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1429`

### English

On §Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!, the inhabitants are using crudely carved imagery to express and share ideas. Samples documented by our researchers indicate that the inhabitants have an awareness of celestial bodies beyond their own planet, and have even begun to document constellations.\n\nTheir early attempts at charting the stars are fairly accurate for such a young civilization, but not so much that they couldn't be improved should we wish to drop some hints...

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1779`

## fircon.1285.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1285.a&lang=en)

### 中文

继续被动观察。他们必须自食其力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1430`

### English

Continue passive observations. They must learn on their own.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1780`

## fircon.1285.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1285.b&lang=en)

### 中文

也许我们可以默默地把他们推向正确的方向……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1431`

### English

Perhaps we could subtly point them in the right direction...

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1781`

## fircon.1290.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1290.name&lang=en)

### 中文

写在星间：图表已递交

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1432`

### English

Written in the Stars: Charts Delivered

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1782`

## fircon.1290.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1290.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由§Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!的居民绘制的早期星图被我们暗中修订了。\n\n描绘附近恒星系图样的雕刻是在附近的一个山洞里制作的。一旦它们被发现，它们应该能够让当地居民对已知宇宙的规模有一个更好的认识。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1433`

### English

The early star charts of the inhabitants on §Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§! have been covertly corrected.\n\nCarvings depicting nearby system diagrams were made in a nearby cave. Once they are discovered, they should provide the local population with a better sense of the scale of the known universe.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1783`

## fircon.1290.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1290.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们今天做了件大好事。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1434`

### English

We did a good thing today.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1784`

## fircon.1291.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1291.name&lang=en)

### 中文

写在星间：图表已收获

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1435`

### English

Written in the Stars: Charts Received

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1785`

## fircon.1291.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1291.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

不幸的是，由于§Y[FromFromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!的居民不知道是谁负责制作了他们的新星图，他们把我们的所作所为视作单纯的破坏行为。山洞周围的安保措施得到了加强，统治精英们对新形式的自我表达越来越敌视……\n\n从积极的方面看，我们的干预并不是完全浪费时间。他们对这一事件的反应在文化研究方面很有价值。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1436`

### English

Unfortunately, since the inhabitants of §Y[FromFromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§! did not know who was responsible for their new star charts, they passed off our efforts as simple vandalism. Security around the caves has increased, and the ruling elite has grown hostile to new forms of self-expression...\n\nOn a positive note, our interference was not a complete waste of time. their reaction to this event has proven fascinating in terms of cultural research.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1786`

## fircon.1292.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1292.name&lang=en)

### 中文

写在星间：拦截

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1437`

### English

Written in the Stars: Intercepted

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1787`

## fircon.1292.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1292.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们没能顺利地在暗中纠正§Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!的星图。对于一个如此年幼的社会而言他们的安保措施令人意外地复杂。对目前的任务来说，暴露的风险太高了。至少目前当地居民需要依靠自己的力量来探索星空了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1438`

### English

Our efforts to covertly correct the star charts on §Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§! have been unsuccessful. Their security protocols are surprisingly sophisticated for such a young society. Given our current mission, the risks of exposure were too high. For now at least, the inhabitants will have to face their astronomical aspirations alone.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1788`

## fircon.1300.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1300.name&lang=en)

### 中文

图画血统

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1439`

### English

Pictorial Descent!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1789`

## fircon.1300.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1300.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在§Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!上独立出现了多种非语言的沟通体系。由于在图像和象形文字集之间存在冲突，居民们距离形成一种主导语言还有几个世纪。\n\n有趣的是，其中一个书写体系的语法结构与我们的很相似。如果§Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!的社会采用这个系统，他们在未来可能会在文化上相当具有可塑性……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1440`

### English

Multiple non-verbal communication systems have emerged independently on §Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!. With conflicting sets of imagery and hieroglyphs, the inhabitants are still centuries away from forming a dominant language.\n\nInterestingly, one of the writing systems has a similar grammatical structure to our own. If §Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§! society were to adopt this system, they could become culturally malleable in the future...

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1790`

## fircon.1300.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1300.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们应该按照我们的形象塑造他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1441`

### English

We should mold them in our image.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1791`

## align_pre_FTL_country_ethics_tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aalign_pre_FTL_country_ethics_tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

他们将会变得与我们相似。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1442`

### English

They will become like us.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1792`

## fircon.1300.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1300.b&lang=en)

### 中文

他们要有自己的语言。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1443`

### English

Their language is their own.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1793`

## fircon.1310.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1310.name&lang=en)

### 中文

我们真伟大！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1444`

### English

All Hail Us!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1794`

## fircon.1310.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1310.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在§Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!上的存在引起了旁人的注意。占主要地位的文化团体现在将我们视为睿智而强大的存在，认为我们控制着元素，并最终掌握着他们的宿命。\n\n许多人在精心设计的仪式中以自己的性命向我们宣誓效忠，担心招致我们的不悦会带来十分严重的后果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1445`

### English

Our presence on §Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§! has not gone unnoticed. The dominant cultural group now regards us as wise and mighty beings who control the elements and ultimately their fate.\n\nMany have pledged their lives to us in elaborate ceremonies, fearing that incurring our displeasure could come with drastic consequences.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1795`

## fircon.1310.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1310.a&lang=en)

### 中文

就该这样！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1446`

### English

As it should be!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1796`

## fircon.1310.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1310.b&lang=en)

### 中文

他们不欠我们什么。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1447`

### English

They owe us nothing.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1797`

## fircon.1315.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1315.name&lang=en)

### 中文

他们崇拜我们！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1448`

### English

They Worship Us!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1798`

## fircon.1315.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1315.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在对§Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!的社会进行了大量的历史观察之后，他们占主流地位的社会群体已经把我们当作神灵来敬仰。他们希望继续用恩惠的行为得到我们的青睐……因此，为了纪念我们，血祭仍在继续。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1449`

### English

After actively observing their society for a substantial chunk of their history, the dominant society on §Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§! have come to revere us as deities. They wish to keep us in their good graces... And so the blood sacrifices continue in our honor.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1799`

## fircon.1320.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1320.name&lang=en)

### 中文

诸神已逝

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1450`

### English

The Gods are Dead

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1800`

## fircon.1320.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1320.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

根据§Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!当地居民的说法，根本没有神。\n\n尽管我们试图将自己打造成他们神圣的主人，却被他们一眼看穿了本质。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1451`

### English

According to the inhabitants of §Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!, there are no gods.\n\nAlthough, we attempted to assert ourselves as their divine overlords, they saw through us easily.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1801`

## fircon.1320.desc.regular

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1320.desc.regular&lang=en)

### 中文

$fircon.1320.desc.base$人们完全拒绝神灵的概念，接受了一种明显倾向于唯物主义的宇宙观。在没有确凿证据的情况下，他们相信在宇宙中只有自己。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1452`

### English

$fircon.1320.desc.base$ Rejecting the concept of deities altogether, the population has embraced a decidedly materialist view of the cosmos. Without hard evidence, they believe themselves alone in the universe.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1802`

## fircon.1320.desc.materialist

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1320.desc.materialist&lang=en)

### 中文

$fircon.1320.desc.base$如果没有基于科学论证的、经同行评议的反面证据，他们已经根据经验确定，在这个现实的位面上，哪怕是遥远的神明亦不存在。他们仍有希望。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1453`

### English

$fircon.1320.desc.base$ Without science based, peer reviewed evidence to the contrary, they have determined empirically that nothing even remotely godly exists on this plane of reality. There is hope for them yet.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1803`

## fircon.1320.desc.authoritarian

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1320.desc.authoritarian&lang=en)

### 中文

$fircon.1320.desc.base$这些桀骜不驯的人们拒绝向他人称臣，选择遵从自己最基础的冲动，进入无政府状态。加入机会恰当，他们应该接受我们作为他们的主人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1454`

### English

$fircon.1320.desc.base$ Refusing to be subjugated, the wayward population has relented to their base urges and made the choice to become ungovernable. They should have accepted us as their masters when they had the chance.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1804`

## fircon.1330.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1330.name&lang=en)

### 中文

艺术寻道！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1455`

### English

Art Finds a Way!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1805`

## fircon.1330.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1330.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!上的社会正不断孕育出丰富的文化和对艺术进行深刻鉴赏的风潮。居民们放下了武器，转而使用书写工具。随着这种全新的学习和探索氛围已超脱于冲突之上，我们可能正在见证这个行星上一个黄金时代的到来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1456`

### English

Society on §Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§! is blossoming with rich culture and a deep appreciation for art. The inhabitants are putting down their weapons in favor of writing instruments. With this newfound willingness to learn and explore beyond conflict, we could be witnessing the dawn of a golden age on the planet.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1806`

## fircon.1330.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1330.b&lang=en)

### 中文

能见证如此美丽的诞生，实属荣幸。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1457`

### English

It is a privilege to witness the birth of such beauty.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1807`

## fircon.1330.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1330.c&lang=en)

### 中文

无用的追求。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1458`

### English

Useless pursuits.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1808`

## fircon.1340.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1340.name&lang=en)

### 中文

旅程开始

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1459`

### English

The Journey Begins

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1809`

## fircon.1340.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1340.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们正在见证§Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!的居民迈出他们走向启蒙的第一步。他们向上抬头，对宇宙的奥秘始终抱持开放的态度。随着艺术表达在他们文化中的发展，艺术力量捕捉社会的精神奋斗的一面也在发展。他们渴望有某种高于自我的信仰。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1460`

### English

We are witnessing the inhabitants of §Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§! taking their first steps towards enlightenment. They look upwards, opening themselves to the mysteries of the universe. As artistic expression develops within their culture, so does art's power to capture the spiritual striving of their society. They yearn for something beyond themselves to believe in.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1810`

## fircon.1340.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1340.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们应当回应他们的祈祷！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1461`

### English

We shall answer their prayers!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1811`

## fircon.1340.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1340.c&lang=en)

### 中文

一切都将化为尘土。剩下的只不过是这件事的序曲。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1462`

### English

Everything turns to dust. The rest is merely prelude.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1812`

## fircon.1345.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1345.name&lang=en)

### 中文

未来会如何？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1463`

### English

What Lies Ahead?

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1813`

## fircon.1345.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1345.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过向存在的神圣真理开放自己，§Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!的居民正在接近开悟。作为精神领袖，我们有责任引导他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1464`

### English

By opening themselves to the divine truth of existence, the inhabitants of §Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§! are approaching enlightenment. As spiritual leaders, it is our duty to guide them.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1814`

## fircon.1350.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1350.name&lang=en)

### 中文

一切皆往终结

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1465`

### English

All Paths Must End

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1815`

## fircon.1350.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1350.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

当§Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!的居民仰望天空时，他们希望得到圣灵的赐福。然而，他们实际得到的是沉默。他们的祈祷没有得到回应。现在他们必须接受精神飘忽不定的本质。只有科学拥有他们寻求的答案。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1466`

### English

When the inhabitants of §Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§! looked up to the skies they hoped to receive blessings from the divine. What they actually received was silence. Their prayers have gone unanswered. Now they must accept the frivolous nature of spirituality. Only science holds the answers they seek.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1816`

## fircon.1360.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1360.name&lang=en)

### 中文

无知会传染

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1467`

### English

Ignorance is Contagious

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1817`

## fircon.1360.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1360.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!的居民已经开始提出一个理论，认为他们的所在的星球是由一个不可言喻的存在赠送给他们的，而生命是对神圣造物主不屈不挠的奉献所进行的回报。或许当他们遇到更广泛的星海共同体时，他们会有一个惊喜。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1468`

### English

The inhabitants of §Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§! have begun to theorize that their world was gifted to them by an ineffable being, and that life is a reward for unyielding devotion to a divine creator. They may be in for a surprise when the time comes for them to meet the wider galactic community.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1818`

## fircon.1360.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1360.a&lang=en)

### 中文

让我们来教教他们科学吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1469`

### English

Let's educate them in the sciences.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1819`

## fircon.1360.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1360.b&lang=en)

### 中文

假以时日，他们的信仰会发展。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1470`

### English

Their faith will develop, given time.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1820`

## fircon.1360.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1360.c&lang=en)

### 中文

他们应该知道真相！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1471`

### English

They should know the truth!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1821`

## fircon.1365.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1365.name&lang=en)

### 中文

无知会传染：事与愿违

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1472`

### English

Ignorance is Contagious: Backfired

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1822`

## fircon.1365.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1365.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过向§Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!上的唯心领袖们现身，我们告诉他们所谓的“神圣造物主”并不存在。他们想要的答案只有通过科学才能找到。\n\n不幸的是，我们的这堂课没有起到效果。这些人们将我们视为神明，整日向我们三叩九拜。我们的任务起到了反效果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1473`

### English

Revealing ourselves to the would-be spiritual leaders on §Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!, we assured them that their so-called 'divine creator' does not exist. The answers they seek can be found only through science.\n\nUnfortunately, our lessons have been ignored. The people now look to us as as gods, vowing to worship us for all time. Our mission has backfired.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1823`

## fircon.1365.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1365.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这件事本来能够做得更好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1474`

### English

This could have gone better.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1824`

## fircon.1370.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1370.name&lang=en)

### 中文

无知会传染：羞辱

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1475`

### English

Ignorance is Contagious: Humiliation

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1825`

## fircon.1370.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1370.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

他们生气了！我们的行动彻底地惹恼了§Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!的居民。数个世纪以来，他们社会中的成员经常在神秘的情况下失踪，然后又在数日或数月后出现，口述着难以置信的外星科技的故事，这一切在他们眼里无异于魔法。\n\n可以理解，发现魔法不存在，这一切仅仅是外星人们为了研究你们的所作所为这件事极大地撼动了他们的世界观。人们在一夜之间倒向孤立主义，礼貌地要求我们不要再把他们当成实验对象。也许我们是该考虑他们的请求。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1476`

### English

They are furious! Our actions have completely embarrassed the inhabitants of §Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!. For centuries, members of their society have disappeared under mysterious circumstances, only to reappear days or months later with the most incredible stories of alien technology, which from their perspective is nothing short of magical.\n\nUnderstandably, learning that magic isn't real - but aliens who probe you for research purposes are - has somewhat shaken their worldview. The population has become an insular society overnight, and are politely requesting us to stop treating them as lab specimens. Perhaps we should consider their request.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1826`

## fircon.1370.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1370.a&lang=en)

### 中文

可能不该这么做。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1477`

### English

Perhaps not.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1827`

## fircon.1380.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1380.name&lang=en)

### 中文

工业进步

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1478`

### English

Industrial Advancement

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1828`

## fircon.1380.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1380.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!上快速的科技进展催生了工业化的时代。交通方式的跃进式发展将此前星球上相对孤立的区域连成一片，创造了环球贸易和思想交流的机遇。\n\n然而，资源的危机正在悄悄酝酿。如果其他星球上的历史也会重演的话，这里的人们很快就会陷入战争。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1479`

### English

Rapid technological advances on §Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§! are ushering in an age of industrialization. Breakthroughs in transportation have linked previously disconnected areas of the world, creating opportunity for global trade and the sharing of ideas.\n\nHowever, tensions over resources are already brewing. If the history of other worlds is any guide, the populace could soon find themselves at war.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1829`

## fircon.1380.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1380.a&lang=en)

### 中文

或许我们可以伸出援手，帮助他们赈济灾民……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1480`

### English

Perhaps we could help them avert a catastrophe...

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1830`

## fircon.1380.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1380.b&lang=en)

### 中文

无论是哪种方法，他们总要学会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1481`

### English

They will learn, one way or another.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1831`

## fircon.1385.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1385.name&lang=en)

### 中文

灾难悬而未决

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1482`

### English

Catastrophe Pending

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1832`

## fircon.1385.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1385.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们冒着计算过的风险，向§Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!的星球领袖透露了自己的身份。我们毫不含糊地建议当地政府结束他们的冲突，警告说战争的后果很可能是灾难性的。许多人试探性地听着。其他人则认为任何关于“外星使者”的谈话都是一个骗局。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1483`

### English

Taking a calculated risk, we revealed ourselves to world leaders of §Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!. In no uncertain terms, we advised the local governments to end their conflict, warning that the consequences of war could well be catastrophic. Many listened tentatively. Others dismissed any talk of 'alien emissaries' as a hoax.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1833`

## fircon.1385.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1385.a&lang=en)

### 中文

时间将证明他们是否听从了我们的警告。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1484`

### English

Time will tell if they will heed our warning.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1834`

## fircon.1390.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1390.name&lang=en)

### 中文

不文明的战争

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1485`

### English

Uncivil War

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1835`

## fircon.1390.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1390.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们决定任由§Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!的星球领袖自行发展，现在他们是自己命运的第一责任人了。不幸的是，好像他们正在走向战争。\n\n据信是不可调和的差异导致了星球上不同的文化群体之间兵戎相见。紧随而来的冲突开启了他们的发展史上一段黑暗的篇章。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1486`

### English

In choosing to leave the world leaders of §Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§! to their own devices, they are now wholly responsible for their destiny. Unfortunately, it appears they are headed for war.\n\nSupposedly irreconcilable differences have driven the planet's culture groups to take up arms against one another. The coming conflict represents a dark period in their development.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1836`

## fircon.1395.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1395.name&lang=en)

### 中文

灾难得到避免

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1487`

### English

Catastrophe Averted

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1837`

## fircon.1395.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1395.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[FromFromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!的各领袖接受了我们的建议，放下了他们的武器。很难说这样的和平会持续多久，但至少现在，他们不太可能将自己从他们的行星表面抹除了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1488`

### English

The leaders of §Y[FromFromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§! have taken our advice and put down their weapons. It is impossible to say if the peace will last, but for now at least, they are unlikely to wipe themselves from the face of their planet.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1838`

## fircon.1400.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1400.name&lang=en)

### 中文

灾难

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1489`

### English

Catastrophe

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1839`

## fircon.1400.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1400.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的接入对§Y[FromFromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!宛如火上浇油。他们的整个星球陷入了战争。然而，如果他们没能互相消灭的话，这种局势也许也有积极的一面：这颗行星上的科技进步成果卓绝。\n\n也许，当战争的硝烟散去，他们就能妥善地把这些科技用到正道上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1490`

### English

Our intervention may have added fuel to the proverbial fire on §Y[FromFromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!. Their world is now at war. However, if they fail to eradicate one another, there may be an upside to the situation: the technological progress being made on the planet is remarkable.\n\nPerhaps, when and if the smoke of battle clears, they will put some of this technology to good use.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1840`

## fircon.1410.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1410.name&lang=en)

### 中文

和平繁荣

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1491`

### English

Peace and Prosperity

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1841`

## fircon.1410.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1410.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!科学技术得到了进一步的发展，因为工业化使他们的社会达到了新的高度。他们在工程方面的最新突破彻底改变了他们对交通的运用，这使他们离行星统一更近了一步。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1492`

### English

Technology continues to progress on §Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!, as industrialization takes their society to new heights. Their most recent breakthrough in engineering has revolutionized their use of transportation and brought them a step closer to planetary unification.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1842`

## fircon.1410.desc.regular

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1410.desc.regular&lang=en)

### 中文

$fircon.1410.desc.base$\n\n与我们模型预期的不同，§Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!现在成为了一个统一的星球。他们将资源统一调配，人们则将为整个星球的社区的福祉作为首要任务，生活水平大幅提升。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1493`

### English

$fircon.1410.desc.base$\n\nDefying our prediction models, §Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§! is now a unified world. Pooling their resources, the people have prioritized the wellbeing of the global community and living standards are rising.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1843`

## fircon.1410.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1410.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$fircon.1410.desc.base$\n\n§Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!的居民们齐心协力，建设出了一个团结的星球。他们统一调配行星资源，每个蜂巢都将整个星球社区的共同利益放在首位，他们集体的生活水平超乎寻常。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1494`

### English

$fircon.1410.desc.base$\n\nWorking together, the inhabitants of §Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§! have built a unified industrial world. Pooling world resources, the individual hives have prioritized the wellbeing of the entire global community, and their collective living standards appear exceptional.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1844`

## fircon.1410.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1410.a&lang=en)

### 中文

引人注目。这非比寻常。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1495`

### English

Remarkable. This is highly uncommon.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1845`

## fircon.1410.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1410.b&lang=en)

### 中文

他们拥有的资源比实际需要的要多……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1496`

### English

They have more resources than they need...

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1846`

## fircon.1410.b.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1410.b.tooltip&lang=en)

### 中文

我们可以帮助他们缓解资源过多的问题。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1497`

### English

We could alleviate them of some of their resources.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1847`

## fircon.1415.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1415.name&lang=en)

### 中文

共同致富

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1498`

### English

Prosperity for All

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1848`

## fircon.1415.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1415.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!上的生活正在蓬勃发展，繁荣的经济为我们提供了一些……千载难逢的机会。一个新近建造的陆路货运网络就是一个理想的目标。从理论上讲，我们的特工人员可以很容易地登上这些货车，夺取货物。然而，根据我们目前的观测姿态，我们必须要采取额外的预防措施，以保持不被更多人发现。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1499`

### English

Life on §Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§! is thriving, and the booming economy offers us certain... unique opportunities. A newly constructed network of land freighters is an ideal target. In theory, our operatives could easily board one of these freighters and seize the cargo. However, in accordance with our current observation stance, we would have to take extra precautions to remain undetected by the wider population.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1849`

## fircon.1415.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1415.a&lang=en)

### 中文

停下。我们除了研究别无其他需求。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1500`

### English

Stand down. We require nothing from them but research.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1850`

## fircon.1415.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1415.b&lang=en)

### 中文

打劫？该我们上了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1501`

### English

A heist? We're in!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1851`

## fircon.1425.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1425.name&lang=en)

### 中文

全速前进！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1502`

### English

Full Steam Ahead!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1852`

## fircon.1425.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1425.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[FromFromFromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!上的货物打劫行动是一场成功。我们的特工夺得了一小部分合金，同时在下面的时候还学到了很多有关[GetPreFTLLower]转运的知识。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1503`

### English

The cargo heist on §Y[FromFromFromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§! was a success. Our agents secured a small cache of alloys, and learned a lot about [GetPreFTLLower] transit while they were down there.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1853`

## fircon.1425.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1425.a&lang=en)

### 中文

打劫成功！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1504`

### English

The heist was a success!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1854`

## fircon.1430.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1430.name&lang=en)

### 中文

到此为止！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1505`

### English

End of the Line!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1855`

## fircon.1430.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1430.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[FromFromFromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!上的货物打劫行动宣告失败。我们的特工在某艘陆地货船上的行动中惨遭抓获。在接下来的冲突中，双方都遭受了伤亡，我们的特工身份暴露。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1506`

### English

The cargo heist on §Y[FromFromFromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§! was a failure. Our operatives were apprehended mid-operation aboard a land freighter. During the ensuing altercation, casualties were recorded on both sides, and our agents were exposed.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1856`

## fircon.1430.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1430.a&lang=en)

### 中文

打劫失败了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1507`

### English

Our heist was a failure.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1857`

## fircon.1445.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1445.name&lang=en)

### 中文

冲突！这有什么好处？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1508`

### English

Conflict! What is it Good For?

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1858`

## fircon.1445.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1445.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一场战争在§Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!上爆发了。最近这些年，这颗星球上的某些派系设法积累了足够的财富，对生产资料实施了垄断。与之对应，其他人对于日益严重的不平等进行了反抗。现在，一场席卷一切的阶级战争爆发了。对于§Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!来说，这是一个黑暗的时代，后果将持续很长时间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1509`

### English

A war has broken out on §Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!. In recent years, certain factions on the planet managed to accrue enough wealth to monopolize the means of production. In turn, others revolted against the growing inequality. Now, an all out class war has broken out. It is a dark time for §Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!. This will have lasting consequences.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1859`

## fircon.1445.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1445.a&lang=en)

### 中文

那些革命者非常勇敢，我们哀悼他们的抗争。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1510`

### English

Those brave revolutionaries. We lament their struggle.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1860`

## fircon.1445.a.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1445.a.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

我们很牵挂§Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!。观察这场战争是深入了解他们社会的宝贵机会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1511`

### English

Our thoughts are with §Y[From.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!. Observing this war should provide invaluable insights into their society.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1861`

## fircon.1445.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1445.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们将在他们的政治事务中保持中立。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1512`

### English

We will remain impartial in their political affairs.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1862`

## fircon.1445.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1445.c&lang=en)

### 中文

工人远远不够格……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1513`

### English

Workers are far too entitled...

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1863`

## fircon.1450.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1450.name&lang=en)

### 中文

无法诉说的哀伤

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1514`

### English

Unspoken Grief

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1864`

## fircon.1450.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1450.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!上的战争结束了。革命者们一个接一个失败了。主导派系继续从因自身贪婪引起的战争中获利，四处尸山血海。\n\n一切恢复了原状，革命的呼声也一时噤若寒蝉。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1515`

### English

The war on §Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§! is over. One by one, the revolutionaries fell. As the bodies piled high, the dominant faction continued to profit from a war triggered by their own greed.\n\nThe status quo has been restored. Cries for revolution have gone silent.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1865`

## fircon.1450.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1450.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们为他们的损失感到哀伤。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1516`

### English

We grieve for their loss.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1866`

## fircon.1450.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1450.b&lang=en)

### 中文

这是不可接受的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1517`

### English

This is unacceptable.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1867`

## fircon.1451.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1451.name&lang=en)

### 中文

战争结束了！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1518`

### English

War Is Over!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1868`

## fircon.1451.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1451.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们选择不干涉[GetPreFTLLower]文明的政治，§Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!上的战争也按自己的步调结束了。压迫者势力轻松地碾压了革命者。\n\n一切恢复了原状，革命失败了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1519`

### English

After we chose not to involve ourselves with [GetPreFTLLower] politics, the war on §Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§! ended of its own accord. The oppressive powers steamrolled the revolutionaries with ease.\n\nThe status quo has been restored. The revolution is dead.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1869`

## fircon.1452.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1452.name&lang=en)

### 中文

首都凯旋

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1520`

### English

Capital Triumph

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1870`

## fircon.1452.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1452.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!上怠忽的职员们把自己忙得够呛，他们傲慢的反抗以失败告终。执行官们通过巧妙的公司改组和绩效管理手段再度控制住了局面。\n\n一切恢复了原状。所有人再次回到了自己的工作岗位上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1521`

### English

The delinquent employees on §Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§! have tired themselves out, and their arrogant revolt has ended in failure. The executives regained control of the situation through tactful corporate restructuring and performance management.\n\nThe status quo has been restored. Everyone is returning to their designated workspaces.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1871`

## fircon.1453.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1453.name&lang=en)

### 中文

经济不安全

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1522`

### English

Economic Insecurity

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1872`

## fircon.1453.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1453.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

超出模型预期，§Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!上这些行为不端的雇员们粗暴地驱逐了管理层并取而代之。\n\n这是一场灾难，我们的工人从中获取了灵感，并意识到了自己的处境。我们的投资陷入了前所未有的危险。我们本应更彻底地进行尽职调查的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1523`

### English

Defying prediction models, the delinquent employees on §Y[FromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§! ousted their executive tier in a hostile takeover.\n\nThis is a disaster, as our own workers have taken inspiration and are now getting ideas above their stations. Our investments are now at risk like never before. We should have been more thorough in our due diligence.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1873`

## fircon.1455.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1455.name&lang=en)

### 中文

沉默的抵抗

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1524`

### English

The Silent Resistance

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1874`

## fircon.1455.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1455.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在§Y[FromFromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!上，不满的声音被权势滔天的精英阶层给压制住了。然而，如果我们现在进行干预，肯定可以扭转局势。我们在这个星球上的存在可以为残余的抵抗组织提供支援。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1525`

### English

On §Y[FromFromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!, dissenting voices have been silenced by the powerful elite. However, if we intervene now we could certainly turn the tide. Our presence on the planet could bolster what is left of the resistance.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1875`

## fircon.1455.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1455.a&lang=en)

### 中文

没有希望的徒劳。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1526`

### English

A hopeless endeavor.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1876`

## fircon.1455.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1455.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们没什么可输的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1527`

### English

We have nothing to lose.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1877`

## fircon.1460.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1460.name&lang=en)

### 中文

镇压压迫者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1528`

### English

Oppressing the Oppressors

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1878`

## fircon.1460.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1460.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的军队刚在§Y[FromFromFromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!上登陆，立即取得了对当地土著军队在物质上和战略上的全面优势。日薄西山的精英阶层已经撤退，革命者们准备好接过权力的衣钵。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1529`

### English

Landing on §Y[FromFromFromFrom.Owner.Capital.GetName]§!, our forces demonstrated an immediate material and strategic advantage over the native troops. The former elite has withdrawn, and the revolutionaries stand poised to assume the mantle of power.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1879`

## fircon.1460.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1460.b&lang=en)

### 中文

这真是最骇人听闻的消息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1530`

### English

This is most devastating news.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1880`

## fircon.1470.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1470.name&lang=en)

### 中文

寻找天种

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1531`

### English

Seeking Spacemen

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1881`

## fircon.1470.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1470.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]§!的居民已经开始积极地寻找他们星球之外的智慧生命迹象。随着他们准备大胆地前往许多文明已经拜访过的地方时，可能我们是时候要重新评估我们的第一次接触协议了，做好准备，让更广泛的星海共同体迎接新的成员。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1532`

### English

The inhabitants of §Y[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]§! have begun actively searching for signs of intelligent life beyond their world. As they prepare to boldly go where many have already gone, it may be time for us to reassess our first contact protocols, in preparation for a new addition to the wider galactic community.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1882`

## fircon.1470.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1470.a&lang=en)

### 中文

当他们准备好时，我们会欢迎，但那之前还不行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1533`

### English

We will greet them when they are ready, and not before.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1883`

## fircon.1470.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1470.b&lang=en)

### 中文

在恐惧中毁灭吧，你们这些小蠢蛋！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1534`

### English

Quake with fear, you tiny fools!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1884`

## fircon.1475.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1475.name&lang=en)

### 中文

隔墙有耳

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1535`

### English

They're Listening

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1885`

## fircon.1475.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1475.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]§!上的[GetPreFTLLower]文明不知通过何种手段截取并破解了一段发自我们轨道观测站的通讯信号。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1536`

### English

The [GetPreFTLLower] civilization on §Y[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]§! has somehow intercepted and deciphered a series of communications emanating from our orbital observation post.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1886`

## fircon.1475.desc.regular

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1475.desc.regular&lang=en)

### 中文

$fircon.1475.desc.base$\n\n他们正在研究一份在观测站上的工作者发往我们首都的一位未知收信人的极具个性化的信息。\n\n这一意外的泄露暴露了一处我们安保协议的漏洞。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1537`

### English

$fircon.1475.desc.base$\n\nThey are currently studying a message of a very personal nature between an agent aboard the observation post, and an unknown recipient from our capital.\n\nThis unfortunate leak has highlighted gaps in our security protocols.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1887`

## fircon.1475.desc.gestalt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1475.desc.gestalt&lang=en)

### 中文

$fircon.1475.desc.base$\n\n他们收到了我们的敏感信息。我们的安保存在漏洞。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1538`

### English

$fircon.1475.desc.base$\n\nThey are in receipt of our sensitive data. We have gaps in our security.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1888`

## fircon.1475.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1475.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们应该再小心点。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1539`

### English

We should have been more careful.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1889`

## fircon.1475.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1475.b&lang=en)

### 中文

找到泄露源。遥控擦除他们的数据仓库。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1540`

### English

Identify the leaks. Remotely scrub their data repository.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1890`

## fircon.1480.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1480.name&lang=en)

### 中文

他们现在认为只剩自己了

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1541`

### English

They Think They're Alone Now

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1891`

## fircon.1480.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1480.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]§!的居民搜寻智慧生命的努力一无所获。\n\n他们投入了大量时间和资源在繁星中搜寻生命，但很沮丧地发现除了虚空以外一无所有。\n\n目前这项研究还在进行，但可以肯定他们很快就对项目失去了希望。不必多说，我们将继续在每一个任务参数中对他们隐藏自身的存在。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1542`

### English

The inhabitants of §Y[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]§! have had no success in their search for intelligent life.\n\nHaving dedicated time and resources in the search for life amongst the stars, they were despondent to discover nothing but void.\n\nFor now, the search continues, but it is clear they are swiftly losing hope in the project. Needless to say, we will continue to hide our existence from them, per mission parameters.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1892`

## fircon.1480.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1480.a&lang=en)

### 中文

他们还没准备好同我们会面。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1543`

### English

They're not ready to meet us just yet.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1893`

## fircon.1485.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1485.name&lang=en)

### 中文

截取陈旧信号

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1544`

### English

Stale Signals Intercepted

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1894`

## fircon.1485.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1485.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]§!上荒谬的居民们居然用他们羸弱的科技截获了一些我们过去的无线电信号。\n\n解码和理解我们的广播足够让他们忙上几个世代。以他们目前的发展水平，我们尚且没有暴露的风险。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1545`

### English

The ridiculous inhabitants of §Y[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]§! have used their weak tech to intercept some of our historic radio signals.\n\nDeciphering and interpreting our old broadcasts should keep them busy for a few generations. With their current level of development, we are not at risk of being exposed.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1895`

## fircon.1485.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1485.a&lang=en)

继续观测。

### English

Continue observations.

## fircon.1490.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1490.name&lang=en)

### 中文

失控的捣蛋鬼

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1547`

### English

Rogue Prankster

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1897`

## fircon.1490.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1490.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在他们搜寻外行星智慧的过程中，§Y[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]§!的居民向整个星系发去了广播。他们收到的信息是，“嘘，别让他们听到了。”\n\n这当然是我们一位特工的所作所为。结果是，这个年轻的太空民族现在正为外太空究竟蛰伏着什么恐怖动的东西而瑟瑟发抖。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1548`

### English

In their search for extra-planetary intelligence, the inhabitants of §Y[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]§! called out to the galaxy. What they received was a message which said, 'Be quiet or they will hear you.'\n\nThis was the action of one of our own agents. As a result, the young spacefarers are now terrified of what might lurk beyond their system.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1898`

## fircon.1490.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1490.a&lang=en)

### 中文

他们就应该这样做。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1549`

### English

And so they should be.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1899`

## fircon.1500.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1500.name&lang=en)

### 中文

推进力学支持者宣言

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1550`

### English

Propulsion Proponent Proclamation

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1900`

## fircon.1500.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1500.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们收到了报告，是关于在§Y[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]§!上进行超光速航天器推进装置的事项。当地居民似乎打算成为一个彻底的太空文明。然而，他们在星空中发现的东西，可能会证明当下阶段他们还无法理解。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1551`

### English

We have received reports of superluminal spacecraft propulsion on §Y[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]§!. The inhabitants seem intent on becoming a full-fledged spacefaring civilization. What they discover in the stars may prove too much for them to comprehend.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1901`

## fircon.1505.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1505.name&lang=en)

### 中文

获得很多，但是失去的更多

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1552`

### English

Much To Gain, Yet Much to Lose

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1902`

## fircon.1505.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1505.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

现已证实，§Y[event_target:pre_ftl_planet.GetName]§!确有一艘理论上存在超光速能力的飞船。他们已经开始进行测试，以期尽快突破光障。\n\n一旦成功，他们在技术上便已具备涉及星系外交的能力。这样的境遇必然对他们的社会造成或好或坏的深远影响。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1553`

### English

The existence of a theoretically FTL-capable craft on §Y[event_target:pre_ftl_planet.GetName]§! is now confirmed. They have moved on to testing, hoping to soon break the light speed barrier.\n\nIf they are successful, they will technically be ready to engage in galactic diplomacy. Such an encounter is sure to have profound effects on their society, for good or ill.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1903`

## fircon.1505.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1505.a&lang=en)

他们必须自力更生。保持距离进行观测。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1554`

### English

They must learn on their own. Observe from a distance.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1904`

## fircon.1505.b

### English

They're not ready. Sabotage their mission.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1905`

## fircon.1505.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1505.d&lang=en)

### 中文

我们开始厌烦了。让他们在自己的道路上快马加鞭。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1557`

### English

We grow weary. Speed them along on their way.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1907`

## fircon.1510.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1510.name&lang=en)

### 中文

完全脱离

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1558`

### English

Complete Separation

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1908`

## fircon.1510.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1510.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]§!上雄心勃勃的宇航员们试图突破光障。整个星球的目光都聚焦在这艘飞船向着星间启航。\n\n发射成功了。一切都按预期的参数运行中。随后他们启动了曲速引擎，事情发生了转折。\n\n他们成功地扭曲了时空连续统（这是一种迷人但存在缺陷的超光速航行方式），然而他们只在舰船尾部成功了。随之而来的在案超乎了§Y[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]§!的一切想象。乘员们被剧烈地扭曲撕扯，身体的一部分停留于原地，而同时另一部分被扯入了未来。\n\n他们可得再多花一些时间才能进行下一次尝试了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1559`

### English

The aspiring spacefarers on §Y[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]§! attempted to break the light speed barrier. The whole world looked on as the spacecraft launched towards the stars.\n\nLiftoff was a success. Everything was operating within expected parameters. Then they engaged their warp drive and things took a turn.\n\nThey successfully distorted the shape of the space-time continuum (a fascinating but flawed approach to breaking the light speed barrier), however they were only successful in doing this in the aft section of the craft. The resulting carnage was beyond anything the inhabitants of §Y[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]§! could have ever imagined. The crew was violently distorted, fixed in one part of space, whilst simultaneously having other parts of themselves stretched into the future.\n\nIt could be some time before they are ready for a second attempt.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1909`

## fircon.1510.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1510.a&lang=en)

### 中文

要是我们早点警告他们，他们现在早就成为太空文明了……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1560`

### English

Had we warned them, they would be spacefarers now...

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1910`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1510.b&lang=en)

### English

As expected. Their failure was inevitable.

## fircon.1515.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1515.name&lang=en)

### 中文

步入太空文明

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1562`

### English

Spacefaring Commences

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1912`

## fircon.1515.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1515.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]§!的人们在实验性跃迁引擎方面取得了巨大的成功，然而他们在关于时空扭曲产生器的投射距离计算方面犯了严重的错误。通过与他们的直接沟通，我们得以引导他们通过超空间航道这种广为接受的方式来进行超光速航行。\n\n依照我们的建议，[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]很快就能加入我们的星系社会了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1563`

### English

The people of §Y[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]§! have made remarkable progress on their experimental jump drive, however there were some glaring mistakes in their calculations relating to the projected range of their space-time distortion generator. By engaging in direct communication, we were able to guide them towards the accepted method of faster than light travel via hyperlane network.\n\nFollowing our advice, [pre_ftl_planet.GetName] will soon join us as a galactic spacefaring society.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1913`

## fircon.1515.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1515.a&lang=en)

我们静候他们的到达，加入更广泛的星海共同体。

### English

We await their arrival into the wider galactic community.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1914`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.1520.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]§!上的居民们作为一个宇航文明加入了星海共同体。\n\n我们的观测任务结束了，他们已经拥有了超光速运行能力。

### English

The inhabitants of §Y[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]§! have joined the wider galactic community as a spacefaring society!\n\nOur observation mission now concludes, as they are now FTL capable.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1916`

## art_is_dead_desc

## fircon.2001.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2001.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

使用隐身[GetIndividualNamePlural]和牵引光束，我们能够成功地收集、分析和研究毫无防备的[pre_ftl_species.GetName]个体，之后再将其送还到自己的居所。不幸的是，牵引光束对于[pre_ftl_species.GetAdj]的生理机能产生了预料之外的效果。在返回家中以后，这些样品开始遭受疖子、疹子以及不受控制地打喷嚏，在回到家中的三个小时之后其外表皮还会发生溶解。这种神秘的溶肤病已经酿成了全国范围内的卫健紧急事件，而验尸报告毫无疑问会揭示外部干预的证据。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1614`

### English

Using stealthy [GetIndividualNamePlural] and tractor beams, we were able to successfully collect, analyze and study unsuspecting [pre_ftl_species.GetName] individuals and return them to their dwellings. Unfortunately, the tractor beams had an unexpected effect on the [pre_ftl_species.GetAdj] physiology. After being returned to their homes, the specimens started suffering from boils, rashes and uncontrollable sneezing, experiencing a total dissolution of their outer tissue layers within three hours from their return. The mysterious skin-dissolving illness has already become a national health emergency, and autopsies will doubtlessly reveal proof of external tampering.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1969`

## fircon.2001.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2001.a&lang=en)

重新绑架样品，用来研究我们的光束的效果！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1615`

### English

Re-kidnap the specimens to study the effects of our beams!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1970`
