### English

It's Raining [pre_ftl_species.GetNamePlural]!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1974`

## fircon.2011.base.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2011.base.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

从[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]上收集活体样本的时候发生了事故。由于计算错误，我们的[GetIndividualNamePlural]上配备的牵引光束强度不足。不仅没有得到样本，我们只能短暂地让它们悬浮在空中，然后立马掉到地面上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1619`

### English

An accident occurred while collecting live samples from planet [pre_ftl_planet.GetName]. Due to a miscalculation, our [GetIndividualNamePlural] were equipped with tractor beams of insufficient strength. Instead of acquiring the specimen, we only managed to lift them in the air and drop them to the ground shortly after.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1975`

## fircon.2011.noavian.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2011.noavian.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$fircon.2011.base.desc$\n\n其中一些样本掉到了建筑和树的顶部，当地人困惑不已，各种各样的古怪理论冒了出来。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1620`

### English

$fircon.2011.base.desc$\n\nSome of the specimens ended up on top of buildings and trees, baffling the locals and giving life to all sorts of weird theories.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1976`

## fircon.2011.avian.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2011.avian.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$fircon.2011.base.desc$\n\n我们试图收集的鸟类样本发现这样的事故特别好玩。这些胆子很大的样本甚至开始追逐我们的[GetIndividualNamePlural]，希望也能被带走，而且完全是出于寻求乐子。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1621`

### English

$fircon.2011.base.desc$\n\nThe avian specimens we were trying to collect found the accident highly amusing. The bolder specimens even started chasing after our [GetIndividualNamePlural] in a bid to be lifted up too, just for the thrill.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1977`

## fircon.2505.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2505.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

虽然[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]的感性个体都从属于一个有机的格式塔意识，但[pre_ftl_planet.GetPlanetHabitat]有数个不同的“蜂巢”。这些蜂巢是互相独立的子个体集合体，相互竞争着资源。偶尔，两个群体之间会爆发冲突，有时还会升级为全面战争。我们目前正在目睹一场对蜂巢首都的全力攻击，这场面真是令人毛骨悚然。由于不受移情作用的阻碍，子个体的效率会让任何将军感到自豪。\n\n守军节节败退，但根据我们的估计，它们应该还能坚持几天。\n\n然而就在片刻之后，所有战斗都停止了，守军与对手联合起来，开始拆除自己的首都，寻找一切有价值的东西。看来，它们是瞬间被彻底同化到了进攻方的蜂巢网络中。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1629`

### English

While all of the sentient population of [pre_ftl_planet.GetName] belongs to an organic gestalt consciousness, the [pre_ftl_planet.GetPlanetHabitat] is home to several different 'Hives'. These hives are isolated collections of drones and are competing with each other for resources. Occasionally, conflict erupts between two groups, and can sometimes escalate to total war. We are currently witnessing an all out assault on a hive's capital, and it is a gruesome business. The efficiency of the drones, unhindered by empathy would make any general proud.\n\nThe defenders are quickly losing ground, but by our estimations they should hold for a few more days.\n\nYet, moments later, all fighting ceases, as the defenders join forces with their opponents and begin dismantling their capital for anything of value. It appears that they have been completely and instantly assimilated into the network of the attacking hive.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1988`

## fircon.2515.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2515.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]上的[pre_ftl_country.GetName]最近开始在这颗星球的新地区定居下来。当地的动植物群落起初与定居地区的动植物群落大不相同，但现在已经完全被集体所控制，它们采取了一种激进却有条不紊的方法来调节当地的生态系统。某些动物物种被无情地消灭，用其他物种取而代之，而入侵性植物扼杀了本地树木的生命。当地土著对生态的掌控令人惊叹，但它们在气候异常地区不可避免地遭遇了一些挫折，在这些地区，引进的生命形式无法长期生存。我们相信，它们很快就会找到克服这些障碍的办法，最终整个星球将统一起来，为集体的繁荣提供最佳条件。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1634`

### English

The [pre_ftl_country.GetName] on [pre_ftl_planet.GetName] has recently started settling new regions of the planet. The local flora and fauna, initially vastly different from what can be found in the settled territory, are being completely taken over by the collective, who is taking a radical but methodical approach to regulating the local ecosystem. Certain animal species are ruthlessly exterminated only to be replaced by others, while invasive vegetals choke out the life of the indigenous trees. The native's ecological mastery is remarkable, yet they cannot avoid certain setbacks in areas of unusual climate, where the introduced lifeforms cannot survive long. We believe that they shall soon find a way around these obstacles and eventually, the whole world will be uniform, presenting optimal conditions for the collective to thrive in.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:1994`

### English

The Fires of Industry

## fircon.2525.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2525.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]上的[pre_ftl_country.GetName]最近开发了新技术，它们的工业开始疯狂地增长。当地蜂巢的头脑中有了部分自动化的概念，工厂、冶炼厂、装配线和燃料提取器已在[pre_ftl_planet.GetPlanetHabitat]的各地建成。黑烟在天空中飘荡，许多子个体由于接触到有毒的化学物质而开始出现退化的迹象。很快，蜂巢将不得不解决这些问题，然而，不受影响的子个体仍在照常工作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1639`

### English

The [pre_ftl_country.GetName] on [pre_ftl_planet.GetName] has recently developed new technologies, and their industry has started to grow at a frantic pace. Factories, smelters, assembly lines, and fuel extractors have been built across the [pre_ftl_planet.GetPlanetHabitat] as partial automation has found its way into the mind of the local hive. Dark smoke streaks the skies, and many drones are starting to show signs of degeneration due to the toxic chemicals they are in contact with. Soon the hive will have to address these issues, yet the unfazed drones keep working as usual.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2000`

### English

Requiem for a Drone

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2010`

## fircon.2535.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2535.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

发生了一系列意外事件，[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]似乎被悲伤所笼罩。在一个子个体的死亡之后，我们目睹了整个蜂巢出现了前所未有的工作停顿。我们不清楚这个子个体有什么与众不同之处，但蜂巢似乎正在举行某种仪式，死去的子个体几乎被虔诚地切成碎片，然后这些碎片被送到各个新生子个体的培育所。几分钟后，整个蜂巢又开始运转起来。这非常令人困惑，很像一个具有社会意义或精神意义的事件。 

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1648`

### English

In an unexpected turn of events, grief seems to have taken over [pre_ftl_planet.GetName]. We are witnessing an unprecedented halt in the work of an entire hive, in the aftermath of the death of a singular drone. We are unsure why this drone is different from the others, but the hive seems to be holding some kind of ceremony, where the drone is almost religiously cut to pieces, before those pieces are taken to various nurseries for newborn drones. Moments later, the entire hive springs in motion again. This is highly confusing, and looks a lot like an event of social or spiritual importance. 

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2011`

新生共同体

### English

Nascent Collective

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2016`

## fircon.2545.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2545.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]上观察到的原生蜂巢已经在进化中迈出了重要一步。在[pre_ftl_planet.GetPlanetHabitat]的大部分地区，小规模的个体群体已经开始组织成小规模的蜂巢，但是没有完全自主，目前这些子个体还缺乏完全成熟的集体应有的团结度和协调性。\n\n这种情况在[pre_ftl_planet.GetPlanetHabitat]的北部地区发生了变化，那里有个正在征服附近子个体的奇点，而一个子个体似乎是这种情况的中心点。这个“优异子个体”目前正在星球上旅行，并把被征服的子个体送到一个定居点，对这个技术水平很低的星球来说规模相当庞大。在那里，子个体们似乎专注于建造房屋和粮仓，并尽可能地扩大食物生产，以容纳新来者。自主性消失了，对它们来说，现在只有集体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1653`

### English

The proto hive that we have been observing on [pre_ftl_planet.GetName] has taken a significant step in its evolution. Over most of the [pre_ftl_planet.GetPlanetHabitat], small groups of individuals had started to organize in small-scale hives, but, while not fully autonomous, these drones were so far lacking the cohesion and coordination of a fully realized collective.\n\nThis has changed in the northern reaches of the [pre_ftl_planet.GetPlanetHabitat], where an individual appears to be the nexus for a singularity that is subjugating the nearby drones. This 'Drone-Prime' is currently traveling the planet, and sending the subjugated drones to gather in a settlement of massive proportions given the low technological level of the world. There, the drones seem to be focused on building houses, silos, and expanding their food production as much as possible to accommodate the newcomers. Gone is the autonomy, for them, there is only the collective now.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2017`

### English

## fircon.2546.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2546.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]的“优异子个体”已经成功捕获并使其进入睡眠状态。尽管我们已经非常小心，但肯定还是被[pre_ftl_planet.GetPlanetHabitat]上的其他子个体发现了，每次我们的空间站运行到靠近它们居住地的位置，它们都好像能够感应到优异子个体接近一般。\n\n我们研究优异子个体的大脑在跨维度沟通方面得到了有趣的结果，但我们尚未摸清它控制其他子个体和吞噬萌芽意识的具体能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1660`

### English

The 'Drone-Prime' from [pre_ftl_planet.GetName] has been abducted successfully and put to sleep. Despite our care, the other drones on the [pre_ftl_planet.GetPlanetHabitat] have definitely taken notice, and every time our station's orbit brings us close to their settlement, they display aggressive behaviors, as if they could sense that the Drone Prime was closer.\n\nOur studies of the prime's brain have yielded interesting results about transdimensional communication, but the details of its abilities to take over drones and subsume the budding consciousness still escapes our grasp.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2025`

## fircon.2547.name

灵能传染病

### English

Psychic Plague

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2029`

## fircon.2547.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2547.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]的“优异子个体”已经成功捕获并使其进入睡眠状态。不幸的是，在我们研究这个对象的脑电波模式时，它醒了过来。随后，我们收到了一系列令人费解的报告，似乎都指向该对象逐一打破了观测站人员的思想，并征服了大多数人。幸运的是，唯一能够超光速航行的舰船在混乱中被摧毁了，但站内系统显示，优异子个体正在通过新的子个体们迅速吸收知识，并表现出完全可以使用我们技术和设备的能力。\n\n隔离协议规定，应立即远程摧毁该站点及其中的所有人员！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1664`

### English

The 'Drone-Prime' from [pre_ftl_planet.GetName] has been abducted successfully and put to sleep. Unfortunately, the subject woke up as we were studying the patterns in its brain waves. We have then received a confusing collection of reports that all seem to point at the subject breaking the minds of the observation post's crew, one by one, and subjugating most of them. Fortunately, the only FTL-capable vessel was destroyed in the confusion, but the station systems show that the Drone Prime is quickly assimilating knowledge from its new drones, and has shown full capabilities of using our tech and equipment.\n\nContainment protocols dictate the remote destruction of the station and all its occupants at once!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2030`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2547.a&lang=en)

### 中文

执行远程摧毁！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1665`

### English

Execute remote destruction!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2031`

[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]轨道上的观测站已经摧毁。

### English

The Observation Post in orbit of [pre_ftl_planet.GetName] is destroyed.

## fircon.2555.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2555.name&lang=en)

### 中文

离开的权利

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1667`

### English

The Right to Leave

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2034`

## fircon.2555.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2555.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]的观察在继续，最近我们发现了最初被误认为是失控了的子个体。它们生活在当地蜂巢定居点的外围，似乎已经拥有部分、甚至完全的自主性。它们似乎彻底脱离了集体，有些甚至很享受悠闲的时光，舒适地无所事事。\n\n然而，它们似乎仍然依赖蜂巢获取资源，本质上是与集体进行交易。我们尚不明了为什么蜂巢不认为应该消灭这些失控子个体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1668`

### English

Our observation of [pre_ftl_planet.GetName] proceeds, and we have recently discovered what we first mistook as rogue drones. These individuals live in the periphery of the local hive settlements, and seem to have acquired partial, if not full autonomy. They seem entirely disconnected from the collective, and some even seem to enjoy leisurely moments, comfortable doing nothing.\n\nThey however appear to still depend on the hive for resources, and are basically trading with the collective. Why the hive has not seen fit to exterminate these rogue drones remains a mystery.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2035`

## fircon.2555.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2555.a&lang=en)

### 中文

应该消灭反常现象。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1669`

### English

Deviancy should be crushed.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2036`

个性很没有效率。

和蜂巢共生是很罕见的。

## fircon.2605.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2605.name&lang=en)

### 中文

行星际飞行

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1673`

### English

Interplanetary Flight

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2041`

## fircon.2605.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2605.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

天气条件：§G完美无瑕§!\n核热火箭：§G燃料齐全§!\n飞行计划：§G已优化§!\n\n今天，当[pre_ftl_country.GetName]启动首次行星际任务时，[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]上的所有广播都在播放这些指示。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1674`

### English

Weather Conditions: §GFlawless§!\nNuclear Thermal Rocket: §GFully Fueled§!\nFlight Plan: §GOptimized§!\n\nThese were the indicators broadcast all over [pre_ftl_planet.GetName] today as the [pre_ftl_country.GetName] launched its first interplanetary mission.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2042`

## fircon.2605.desc.regular

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2605.desc.regular&lang=en)

### 中文

$fircon.2605.desc.base$这些为了这项任务经过多年训练的航天员，于当地时间的今天早上登上了火箭。现在他们已经翱翔于那片我们和他们共享的天空。让我们追随他们的征程吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1675`

### English

$fircon.2605.desc.base$ The astronauts who had trained for years to go on this mission boarded the rocket early in the morning, local time. Now they soar the space we share with them. Let us follow their journey.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2043`

## fircon.2605.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2605.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

$fircon.2605.desc.base$经特异性进化后的航天子个体比普通的子个体更加独立，随时准备探索它们所在的恒星系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1676`

### English

$fircon.2605.desc.base$ The specially evolved astronaut drones are more independent than regular ones, ready to explore their solar system.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2044`

## fircon.2605.a.mean

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2605.a.mean&lang=en)

### 中文

飞吧，烧吧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1677`

### English

Fly and burn.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2045`

## fircon.2605.a.nice

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2605.a.nice&lang=en)

### 中文

好怀念啊！

### English

How nostalgic!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2046`

## fircon.2615.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2615.name&lang=en)

### 中文

达阵

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1679`

### English

Touchdown

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2048`

## fircon.2615.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2615.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过尝试和试错，[from.owner.GetSpeciesAdj]的第一次行星际旅行以成功告终。尽管这次旅途并非一切顺利，但是他们证明了自己作为太空探索者的潜力。\n\n[from.owner.GetName]受此项任务的激励，正迫不及待地准备迎接前往繁星的旅途上可能遇到的下一项挑战。我们或许很快就有机会与他们进行平等的对话了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1680`

### English

Through trials and tribulations, the first [from.owner.GetSpeciesAdj] interplanetary journey ended in success. While not the smoothest of trips, they proved their potential as space explorers.\n\nThe [from.owner.GetName] have been inspired by this mission and are eagerly awaiting their next challenge in their journey to the stars. We may soon have the chance to speak to them, as equals.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2049`

## fircon.2615.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2615.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[from.owner.GetName]§!：§G+20%§!科技进步。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1681`

### English

§Y[from.owner.GetName]§!: §G+20%§! Technological Progression.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2050`

## fircon.2620.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2620.name&lang=en)

### 中文

[from.owner.capital_scope.GetName]，我们遇到麻烦了

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1682`

### English

[from.owner.capital_scope.GetName], We Have a Problem.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2052`

## fircon.2620.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2620.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

第一艘[from.owner.GetSpeciesName]的行星际飞船上的机械故障导致它困死在了太空中。这艘船没有任何办法调整自己的轨迹，很快就会超出救援范围。[from.owner.capital_scope.GetName]正在努力寻找解决方案，但机会渺茫。不过，我们也许有机会进行干预。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1683`

### English

Mechanical failure aboard the first interplanetary [from.owner.GetSpeciesName] vessel has left it dead in space. No longer capable of adjusting its trajectory, the ship will soon be beyond saving. [from.owner.capital_scope.GetName] is working on a solution, yet the odds are not good. However, we may have an opportunity to intervene.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2053`

## fircon.2620.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2620.a&lang=en)

### 中文

将它们带离那艘船！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1684`

### English

Get them off that ship!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2054`

## fircon.2620.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2620.b&lang=en)

### 中文

利用我们潜入其社会的特工提供一个解决方案。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1685`

### English

Use our agents embedded in their society to plant a solution.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2055`

## fircon.2520.b.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2520.b.tt&lang=en)

### 中文

这将会增加正面结果发生的可能性。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1686`

### English

This will increase the likelihood of a positive outcome.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2056`

## fircon.2620.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2620.c&lang=en)

### 中文

我们能不能在它们的太空中心附近，留下一条包含解决方案的信息？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1687`

### English

Couldn't we leave a message with a solution near their space center?

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2057`

## fircon.2620.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2620.d&lang=en)

### 中文

修复飞船，将它们的记忆擦除。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1688`

### English

Repair their ship and wipe their memories.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2058`

## fircon.2620.e

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2620.e&lang=en)

### 中文

让它们自己想办法解决这个问题。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1689`

### English

Let them figure it out on their own.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2059`

## fircon.2625.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2625.name&lang=en)

### 中文

救回船员

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1690`

### English

Crew Retrieved

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2061`

## fircon.2625.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2625.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

这艘注定要毁灭的探索船的船组人员们现在安全地登上了我们的观测站。不难理解，他们现在极度地恐惧和困惑。尤其是因为我们切断了它们和母星的联系，然后把它们留在了一个异星货舱里。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1691`

### English

The crew of the doomed exploratory vessel is now safely aboard our observation outpost. They are, understandably, incredibly scared and confused. Especially after we cut them off from their homeworld and left them in an alien cargo hold.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2062`

## fircon.2625.desc.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2625.desc.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

$fircon.2625.desc.base$\n\n在仔细研究了当地的蜂群如何交流之后，我们设法建立了一个代理主脑，让这些工蜂独自思考。我们或许不得不安排它们生活在我们这里。它们可以从远处观察自己的家园，但却永远无法回去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1692`

### English

$fircon.2625.desc.base$\n\nHaving carefully studied how the local hive communicates, we have managed to set up a proxy Overmind, leaving these drones alone with their thoughts. We may have to consign them to living here with us. They can observe their home from afar, but never return.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2063`

## fircon.2625.desc.regular

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2625.desc.regular&lang=en)

### 中文

$fircon.2625.desc.base$\n\n我们或许不得不安排它们生活在我们这里。它们可以从远处观察自己的家园，但却永远无法回去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1693`

### English

$fircon.2625.desc.base$\n\nWe may have to consign them to living here with us. They can observe their home from afar, but never return.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2064`

船组人员的神秘失踪将使得§Y[fromfrom.owner.GetName]§!科技进步减少§R-50%§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1695`

### English

The mysterious loss of their crew will reduce the Technological Progression of the §Y[fromfrom.owner.GetName]§! by §R-50%§!.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2066`

## fircon.2630.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2630.name&lang=en)

任务重大失败

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1697`

### English

Critical Mission Failure

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2069`

## fircon.2630.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2630.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[fromfromfrom.owner.GetSpeciesName]的行星际飞船已失败，没有幸存者。\n\n这次失败后，[GetPreFTLLowerPlural]似乎不那么有动力探索星空。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1698`

### English

The [fromfromfrom.owner.GetSpeciesName] interplanetary craft has failed, there were no survivors.\n\nAfter this failure, the [GetPreFTLLowerPlural] seem to be less driven to explore the stars.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2070`

### English

§Y[fromfromfrom.owner.GetName]§!: §R-20%§! Technological Progression.

## fircon.2635.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2635.name&lang=en)

### 中文

奇迹般的修复

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1702`

### English

Miraculous Repair

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2075`

## fircon.2635.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.2635.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

使用一块布、一个营养剂容器和一些铜线路，这艘注定要毁灭的飞船的船员成功重新获取了主推进系统的控制权。现在，有母星在身后进行支持和敬佩，它们正在继续完成任务的旅途上。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1703`

### English

Using a piece of clothing, a nutrient container, and some copper wiring, the crew of the doomed vessel managed to regain control over their main thrusters. They are now on their way to complete their journey with the support and adoration of their homeworld behind them.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2076`

## inadvertent_jailer

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainadvertent_jailer&lang=en)

### 中文

疏忽导致的囚犯

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1704`

### English

Inadvertent Jailer

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2078`

## inadvertent_jailer_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ainadvertent_jailer_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

尽管我们不是故意的，我们现在不得不妥善安置那些我们曾经希望在观测站进行研究的个体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1705`

### English

Though not our intention we now have to house the very subjects we wish to study on our observation outpost.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2079`

## pre_ftl_research_outpost

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apre_ftl_research_outpost&lang=en)

前超光速研究哨站

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1706`

### English

Pre-FTL Research Outpost

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2080`

## pre_ftl_research_outpost_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apre_ftl_research_outpost_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这个基地设立于一次成功的行星际旅行之后。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1707`

### English

The base set up after a successful interplanetary jaunt.

## preftl.1020.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreftl.1020.name&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1710`

### English

Robotic Uprising

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2086`

## preftl.1020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreftl.1020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.Capital.GetName]的外星文明遭到了机器人子个体的攻击。它们对人工智能的实验造成了毁灭性的后果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1711`

### English

The alien civilization on [From.Capital.GetName] is under attack from robotic drones. Their experiments into AI have had disastrous results.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2087`

## preftl.1035.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreftl.1035.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在对自己创造的机械发动了一场席卷全球的战争后，[From.Capital.GetName]的外星文明成功打败了威胁自身安全的机器人叛乱。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1716`

### English

After waging a global war against machines of their own creation, the alien civilization on [From.Capital.GetName] have successfully defended themselves from the robotic uprising.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2093`

## NON_BELIEVERS_PROJECT_DESC

### 中文

[non_believers_country.GetSpeciesName]担心穴居人可能会发动一场入侵。这样的担忧是毫无根据的，但是提供额外的部队能够帮助我们赢取他们的信任。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1794`

### English

The [non_believers_country.GetSpeciesName] are worried about a possible subterranean invasion. It's a baseless fear, but providing additional troops will help us gain their trust.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2188`

尽管武器简陋，但它们精通地下战斗，并以不屈的意志保卫自己的家园，因而成为了强大的防御者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1796`

### English

Though armed with crude weapons, their mastery of subterranean warfare and unyielding resolve make them formidable defenders of their homeland.

## fircon.6000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6000.name&lang=en)

### 中文

那里有生命存在吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1801`

### English

Is There Life In There?

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2197`

## fircon.6000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

即使在会面之后，[non_believers_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural]依然拒绝接受外星人存在的事实。他们宣称我们并非星际访客，而是穴居人，想要从地上的某个洞穴里面钻出来占领行星的地面。他们的行星，[non_believers_planet.GetName]，被认为是中空的，并且他们坚信我们使用自己的宇宙飞船在中央的空穴中飞来飞去。\n\n这些令人困惑的信仰阻碍了我们的外交工作。任何我们说的或者做的都遭到了彻底的怀疑。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1802`

### English

Even after meeting us, the [non_believers_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural] refuse to accept the existence of extraterrestrial life. They claim we're not interstellar visitors, but subterranean dwellers who climbed to the planet's surface through a hole in the ground. Their planet, [non_believers_planet.GetName], is thought to be hollow, and they believe we fly inside this central cavity using our spaceships.\n\nThese baffling beliefs are hindering our diplomatic efforts. Everything we say or do is met with the utmost suspicion.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2198`

## fircon.6000.desc.subterranean

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6000.desc.subterranean&lang=en)

### 中文

$fircon.6000.desc$。\n\n虽然事实上就是他们错了，但是解释只会让局势变得进一步复杂。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1803`

### English

$fircon.6000.desc$.\n\nWhile technically they're not wrong, explaining the matter would only complicate things further.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2199`

## fircon.6000.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6000.a&lang=en)

### 中文

将他们最偏激的怀疑论者带到太空。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1804`

### English

Bring their most influential skeptics to space.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2200`

## fircon.6000.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6000.b&lang=en)

### 中文

[non_believers_planet.GetName]才不是中空的！给他们看计算结果！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1805`

### English

[non_believers_planet.GetName] is not hollow! Show them the calculations!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2201`

## fircon.6000.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6000.c&lang=en)

### 中文

承认我们就是地底入侵者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1806`

### English

Admit we are subterranean invaders.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2202`

## fircon.6005.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6005.name&lang=en)

### 中文

怀疑论者错了

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1807`

### English

Proving Skeptics Wrong

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2204`

## fircon.6005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

结果[non_believers_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural]并不相信太空，或者说：他们认为天空是一块镶嵌着镜子的穹顶，群星是太阳光束折射到镜子上形成的，其他天体同理。\n\n哪怕我们将他们带到了我们的首都，他们可能也会宣称自己从未离开过母星，或者其他同样离谱的事情。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1808`

### English

Turns out the [non_believers_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural] don't believe in space, either: they think the sky is a glass dome filled with mirrors: stars are rays of sunlight reflected from the mirrors, as are the other celestial bodies.\n\nEven if we were to bring them to our capital, they would probably claim they had never left their home planet, or something equally absurd.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2205`

## fircon.6005.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6005.a&lang=en)

### 中文

肯定有法子说服他们……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1809`

### English

There must be a way to sway them...

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2206`

## fircon.6015.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6015.name&lang=en)

### 中文

怀疑论者错了

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1810`

### English

Proving Skeptics Wrong

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2208`

## fircon.6015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[non_believers_planet.GetName]传来纷乱的新闻：尽管[non_believers_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural]不再认为我们是来自他们[non_believers_planet.GetPlanetMoon]地底中心的访客，他们现在坚信自己的星球是平的，像我们这样的人是住在吊篮一样的城市里，位于[non_believers_planet.GetName]的地壳下面。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1811`

### English

Mixed news from [non_believers_planet.GetName]: while the [non_believers_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural] don't think we are visitors from the center of their [non_believers_planet.GetPlanetMoon] anymore, they are now convinced their world is flat, and people like us live on hanging cities beneath [non_believers_planet.GetName]'s crust.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2209`

## fircon.6020.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6020.name&lang=en)

### 中文

怀疑论者对了

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1812`

### English

Proving Skeptics Right

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2211`

## fircon.6020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[non_believers_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural]出于我们的诚实向我们表达感谢。当然他们尚未完全相信我们，因为他们认为我们是来自中空的[non_believers_planet.GetName]的入侵者，但是至少他们理解我们没有敌意，希望他们能同我们开展合作。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1813`

### English

The [non_believers_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural] thank us for our honesty. Of course they can't fully trust us yet, since they still believe we are invaders from the Hollow [non_believers_planet.GetName], but at least they understand we wish them no harm and are looking forward to collaborating with us.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2212`

## fircon.6025.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6025.name&lang=en)

### 中文

地底入侵防御部队

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1814`

### English

Subterranean Invasion Defense Force

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2214`

## fircon.6025.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6025.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在下定决心认为我们是中空的[non_believers_planet.GetName]的秘密住民之后，[non_believers_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural]开始担忧[non_believers_planet.GetPlanetMoon]的地表之下到底还藏着什么东西。军队开始在洞穴、喷泉和火山口的附近驻扎，随时准备用武力对抗任何从地下涌现出来的入侵者。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1815`

### English

After having determined we are the secret inhabitants of Hollow [non_believers_planet.GetName], the [non_believers_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural] have started worrying about what else may be lurking under the surface of [non_believers_planet.GetPlanetMoon]. Military forces have been stationed near caves, faults and volcanoes, poised to meet any further underground invaders with force.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2215`

## fircon.6025.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6025.a&lang=en)

### 中文

一起玩呗。给他们提供军事支援。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1816`

### English

Play along. Offer them military support.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2216`

## fircon.6025.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6025.b&lang=en)

### 中文

这也太笑死人了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1817`

### English

This is ludicrous.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2217`

## fircon.6030.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6030.name&lang=en)

### 中文

突然袭击！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1818`

### English

Sudden Attack!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2219`

## fircon.6030.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6030.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[non_believers_planet.GetName]传来难以置信的消息：真的有人藏在地表之下。属于某个先前未知物种的地下军队从洞穴里面涌现了出来，他们正在进攻[non_believers_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural]。这是一场全面的入侵！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1819`

### English

Incredible news from [non_believers_planet.GetName]: someone really was hiding under the surface! Subterranean armies of a previously unknown species are pouring out of caves, and they're attacking the [non_believers_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural]. It's a full-scale invasion!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2220`

## fircon.6030.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6030.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这感觉似曾相识……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1820`

### English

This feels familiar...

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2221`

## fircon.6031.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6031.name&lang=en)

### 中文

曾为天人，今为尘土

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1821`

### English

As Above, So Below

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2223`

## fircon.6031.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6031.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在经历了一场残酷的世界大战之后，[non_believers_country.GetName]被住在[non_believers_planet.GetName]地表下面的穴居物种彻底消灭。新近成为主导的物种注意到了我们的存在，出于某种混乱的好运，他们显然没有认为我们是来自他们行星的中心。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1822`

### English

After a vicious world war, the [non_believers_country.GetName] has been annihilated by the subterranean race living under [non_believers_planet.GetName]'s surface. The newly dominant species is aware of our presence, and in something of a mixed blessing, obviously does not believe we come from the center of their planet.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2224`

## fircon.6031.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6031.a&lang=en)

### 中文

终于，新邻居能够讲道理了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1823`

### English

Finally, reasonable neighbors.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2225`

## fircon.6032.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6032.name&lang=en)

### 中文

突然爆发！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1824`

### English

Sudden Eruption!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2227`

## fircon.6032.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6032.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

[non_believers_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural]有理由感到害怕：尽管这颗行星的核心不是任何地下物种的家园，但是熔融的核心结果是十分危险的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1825`

### English

The [non_believers_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural] were right to be afraid: while not home to any underground race, the molten core of their planet has proven dangerous enough.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2228`

## fircon.6032.desc.troops

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6032.desc.troops&lang=en)

### 中文

某个表面看上去已经死寂的火山突然喷发，摧毁了这个区域的基础设施。然而，幸亏我们的军队就出现在事发点附近，大多数受灾群众都被安全地撤离。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1826`

### English

The sudden eruption of a seemingly extinct volcano was devastating to the region's infrastructure. However, thanks to our military presence near the scene, most of the affected population was evacuated safely.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2229`

## fircon.6032.desc.notroops

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6032.desc.notroops&lang=en)

### 中文

某个表面看上去已经死寂的火山突然喷发，正在摧毁一切，[non_believers_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural]绝望地寻求帮助。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1827`

### English

The sudden eruption of a seemingly extinct volcano was devastating, and the [non_believers_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural] are desperate for help.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2230`

## fircon.6032.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6032.a&lang=en)

### 中文

至少他们现在信任我们了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1828`

### English

At least they trust us now.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2231`

## fircon.6033.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6033.name&lang=en)

### 中文

异常气候

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1829`

### English

Anticlimactic

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2233`

## fircon.6033.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6033.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

地底入侵防御部队的士兵驻扎在最大的洞穴和火山口旁边，他们报告称完全没有任何东西从地表下面冒出来。或许这是最好的情况，但是其中一些士兵明显十分失望。 

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1830`

### English

The Subterranean Invasion Defense Force troops stationed near the biggest caves and volcanoes report that absolutely nothing has emerged from beneath the surface. It's probably for the best, although some of the soldiers look visibly disappointed. 

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2234`

## fircon.6035.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6035.name&lang=en)

### 中文

观点的变动

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1831`

### English

A Change Of Perspective

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2236`

## fircon.6035.desc.start

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6035.desc.start&lang=en)

### 中文

[non_believers_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural]希望向我们表达感谢，以及正式的道歉。他们现在明白我们，事实上，并不是地底穴居人，而是来自另一个地方，那里是：

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1832`

### English

The [non_believers_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural] would like to offer us their thanks, as well as an official apology. They now understand we are not, in fact, subterranean dwellers, but we come from another place entirely:

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2237`

## fircon.6035.desc.water

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6035.desc.water&lang=en)

### 中文

大海。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1833`

### English

the ocean.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2238`

## fircon.6035.desc.sun

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6035.desc.sun&lang=en)

### 中文

他们星辰的正中央。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1834`

### English

the center of their star.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2239`

## fircon.6035.desc.end

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6035.desc.end&lang=en)

### 中文

他们对于宇宙奥妙的掌握还是不够完美，但至少他们准备相信我们了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1835`

### English

Their grasp of cosmology is still less than perfect, but at least now they are ready to trust us.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2240`

## fircon.6035.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6035.a&lang=en)

### 中文

从社会学角度来看，很有趣；从精神上来看，真是精疲力竭的体验。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1836`

### English

A sociologically interesting but mentally exhausting experience.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2241`

## fircon.6038.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6038.name&lang=en)

### 中文

滚回地下！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1837`

### English

Go Back Underground!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2243`

## fircon.6038.desc.troops

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6038.desc.troops&lang=en)

### 中文

经过一场漫长的、难解难分的战斗，我们的联合部队战胜了地底入侵者，将他们赶回了[non_believers_planet.GetName]的地表之下！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1838`

### English

After a long, hard-fought battle, our combined forces triumphed over the subterranean invaders, forcing them back under [non_believers_planet.GetName]'s surface!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2244`

## fircon.6038.desc.notroops

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.6038.desc.notroops&lang=en)

### 中文

经过一场漫长的、难解难分的战斗，[non_believers_country.GetName]迫使地底入侵者撤退回到了[non_believers_planet.GetName]的地表之下！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1839`

### English

After a long, hard-fought battle, the [non_believers_country.GetName] forced the subterranean invaders to retreat under [non_believers_planet.GetName]'s surface!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2245`

## fircon.7500.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.7500.name&lang=en)

### 中文

填充[Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]玩具

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1840`

### English

Stuffed [Root.GetSpeciesNamePlural]

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2247`

## fircon.7500.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.7500.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

今天，§Y[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]§!上传来了令人意想不到的消息，一张熟悉的面容向我们问好：那是一张我们自己的脸。\n\n最近，[pre_ftl_country.GetSpeciesAdj]似乎迷上了我们的外表。我们的[Species.GetEarNamePlural]似乎让他们联想到了他们的幼体。事实上，他们形容我们非常“可爱”。一种长着夸张[Species.GetEarNamePlural]的填充玩具在他们的城市里已经成了一等一的抢手货。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1841`

### English

Unexpected news from §Y[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]§! today as a familiar face greets us: our own.\n\nRecently, the [pre_ftl_country.GetSpeciesAdj] have become captivated by our form. Something about our [Species.GetEarNamePlural] seems to remind them of their young. In fact, they say that we are 'Cute.' Already, stuffed toys with exaggerated [Species.GetEarNamePlural] have become a wide-spread commodity in their cities.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2248`

## fircon.7500.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.7500.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们当然是这样！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1842`

### English

Of course we are!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2249`

## fircon.7500.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.7500.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不§R可爱§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1843`

### English

We are not §Rcute§!.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2250`

## fircon.7500.c

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.7500.c&lang=en)

### 中文

但是——嗯——我们不——没有人曾经……

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1844`

### English

But- um- a- we are not- no one has ever...

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2251`

## fircon.7500.d

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.7500.d&lang=en)

### 中文

真是浪费时间。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1845`

### English

What a waste of time.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2252`

## fircon.7500.e

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.7500.e&lang=en)

### 中文

我们……我们能拥有一个吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1846`

### English

Could... could we have one?

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2253`

## fircon.7500.f

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.7500.f&lang=en)

### 中文

对任何使用我们形象的东西都要征收版税。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1847`

### English

We will impose royalties on any use of our likeness.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2254`

## fircon.7505.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.7505.name&lang=en)

### 中文

万象归一

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1848`

### English

All is One

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2256`

## fircon.7505.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.7505.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]§!上正在发生有趣的社会变革。\n\n由于他们只有我们作为参考，那里的人们开始相信宇宙中的所有生命都像我们一样：互相连接、合而为一。在别无其他参照的情况下，[pre_ftl_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural]急切地希望我们的[root.GetIndividualNamePlural]能进入他们的机构。他们的误解给予我们提升他们文明的良机。然而，如果得知了外面世界的真相（包括非格式塔物种的存在），他们必定会降低对我们的信任。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1849`

### English

Intriguing sociological changes are taking place on §Y[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]§!.\n\nGiven they have only ourselves as a reference, the people there have come to believe that all life in the universe must be as we are: connected. One. Without another frame of reference, the [pre_ftl_country.GetSpeciesNamePlural] are more than willing to allow our [root.GetIndividualNamePlural] access to their institutions. Their misunderstanding could enable us to advance their civilization considerably. However, if the truth about the wider galaxy (and the existence of non-gestalt species) were to become known, they might become less trusting of us.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2257`

## fircon.7505.a.hive

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.7505.a.hive&lang=en)

### 中文

是的，我们是一个整体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1850`

### English

Yes, we are all.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2258`

## fircon.7505.a.machine

## fircon.7510.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.7510.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

我们希望能够促进对蜂巢的教育计划，这一举措取得了成功，我们计划继续教他们一些基本的化学知识。差不多是立刻，他们就推导出了基于这些原理的最具破坏的应用，我们前脚还没离开他们的大气层，后脚他们就开始对邻近的蜂巢发动战争。现在，数以百万计的子个体正在死亡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1859`

### English

After the success of our educational initiative with the hive we wish to promote, we proceeded to teach them some basic chemistry. Almost immediately, they extrapolated the most destructive applications of these principles, and started waging war against a neighboring hive before we had even left their atmosphere. Now, millions of drones are dying.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2269`

### English

Our [Root.GetScientistPlural] were successful with their task on §Y[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]§!. $fircon.7510.desc.base$

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2270`

## fircon.8025.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8025.name&lang=en)

### 中文

建立自由港

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1879`

### English

Freeport Established

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2292`

## fircon.8025.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8025.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

根据我们同[freeport_country.GetName][GetPreFTLUpperPlural]的贸易协议，我们在他们的主要定居点附近建造了一个小型太空港。我们的商人不受规定约束，能够赚取巨额利润，但是禁止离开那片特区。\n\n结果是，走私贩和有组织的帮派建立了一个繁荣的黑市经济。到目前为止，[freeport_species.GetAdj]选择睁一只眼闭一只眼，但是如果任由其发展，这个自由港将不可避免地被犯罪给拖垮。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1880`

### English

Per our trade agreement with the [freeport_country.GetName] [GetPreFTLUpperPlural], we have established a small spaceport near one of their major settlements. Free from regulation, our merchants are reaping massive profits, but are forbidden of leaving the complex.\n\nAs a result, smugglers and organized gangs have established a thriving black market economy. So far, the [freeport_species.GetAdj] have turned a blind eye, but if things continue, the freeport will inevitably be overrun by criminals.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2293`

## fircon.8025.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8025.a&lang=en)

### 中文

要求开放额外的自由港。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1881`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2294`

## fircon.8030.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8030.name&lang=en)

### 中文

甜牙

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1889`

### English

Sweet Tooth

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2305`

## fircon.8030.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8030.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在与[sweet_country.GetName][GetPreFTLUpperPlural]的经贸往来中向他们出口一种可口的普通美食，至少对于我们来说是这样的。不过，已经被证实的是，这种普通的甜品对于[sweet_species.GetName]来说居然能产生精神方面的影响。它具有高度成瘾性，而且非常危险。无良的商人们以高价大量出口这种商品，以至于许多当地居民陷入昏迷乃至死亡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1890`

### English

One of the commodities we have been trading with the [sweet_country.GetName] [GetPreFTLUpperPlural] is tasty, but very ordinary food. At least to our species. However, this basic sweetener has proven to be a very powerful psychotrope for the [sweet_species.GetName]. It is highly addictive, and extremely dangerous. Unscrupulous merchants have been moving absurd quantities of the product at exorbitant prices. As a result, many of the locals are plunging into stupor, and in the worst cases, death.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2306`

## fircon.8030.desc.lith

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8030.desc.lith&lang=en)

### 中文

我们在与[sweet_country.GetName][GetPreFTLUpperPlural]的经贸往来中向他们出口一种可口的普通矿物香料，至少对于我们来说是这样的。不过，已经被证实的是，这种普通的调味品对于[sweet_species.GetName]来说居然能产生精神方面的影响。它具有高度成瘾性，而且非常危险。无良的商人们以高价大量出口这种商品，以至于许多当地居民陷入昏迷乃至死亡。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1891`

### English

One of the commodities we have been trading with the [sweet_country.GetName] [GetPreFTLUpperPlural] is tasty, but very ordinary mineral-based spice. At least to our species. However, this basic sweetener has proven to be a very powerful psychotrope for the [sweet_species.GetName]. It is highly addictive, and extremely dangerous. Unscrupulous merchants have been moving absurd quantities of the product at exorbitant prices. As a result, many of the locals are plunging into stupor, and in the worst cases, death.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2307`

## sweet_tooth_medecine_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asweet_tooth_medecine_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们调拨了医疗资源，用于治疗[sweet_planet.GetName]上的成瘾病症。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1897`

### English

We have pledged our medical resources to the treatment of addiction on [sweet_planet.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2314`

## fircon.8035.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8035.name&lang=en)

### 中文

抢地运动

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1903`

### English

Land Grabs

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2322`

## fircon.8035.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8035.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[landgrab_planet.GetName]传来了令人担忧的经济报告，我们的贸易商正在此地同[landgrab_country.GetName][GetPreFTLUpperPlural]进行贸易。当条约签署的时候，我们采取了一些措施来保障当地人的国家主权，但是我们中的一些人绕过法律，从当地的草场人手中购买了大片的土地。以目前的速率，要不了一代人[landgrab_planet.GetName]就会完全被私人贸易商给买空了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1904`

### English

Concerning economic reports from [landgrab_planet.GetName], where our merchants have been trading with the [landgrab_country.GetName] [GetPreFTLUpperPlural]. When the deal was signed, measures were put in place to ensure the sovereignty of the locals, but some of our own people are skirting the law to buy up vast quantities of land through local strawmen. At the current rate, within a generation [landgrab_planet.GetName] will be wholly owned by private traders.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2323`

## fircon.8035.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8035.a&lang=en)

撤回这些交易，对罪犯进行罚款。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1905`

### English

Revoke the sales and fine the culprits.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2324`

## fircon.8040.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8040.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们同[fruitful_coop_country.GetName]签署的贸易协定带来了累累硕果。双方都遵守协定条款，贸易正在流通。一些商人呼吁在规章制度方面进一步放宽，但总体来说协定给双方都带来了益处。 

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1914`

### English

The trade deal we signed with the [fruitful_coop_country.GetName] has proven extremely lucrative. The terms of the deal are being respected and trade is flowing. Some merchants continue to call for more relaxed regulation, but overall the deal has proven beneficial for both parties. 

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2335`

## fircon.8045.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8045.name&lang=en)

### 中文

被奴役

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1922`

### English

Enslaved

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2345`

## fircon.8045.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8045.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们自[space_slavers_country.GetName][GetPreFTLUpperPlural]收到了令人动摇的报告：肆无忌惮的奴隶贩子伪装成商人，正在滥用我们的贸易协定，降落到该星球上大肆绑架当地人。当一大批[space_slavers_species.GetName][GetPreFTLUpperPlural]出现在某个地下奴隶贸易市场上的时候，我们才了解到这一行径。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1923`

### English

Disturbing reports from the [space_slavers_country.GetName] [GetPreFTLUpperPlural]: posing as merchants, unscrupulous slavers have been abusing our Trade Deal to land on the planet and abduct the local population. We only learned of the practice when a large group of [space_slavers_species.GetName] [GetPreFTLUpperPlural] was listed on an off-grid slave market.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2346`

### English

Rescue the abductees.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2347`

被奴役

### English

Enslaved

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2353`

## fircon.8050.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8050.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们自[preftl_slavers_country.GetName][GetPreFTLUpperPlural]收到了令人动摇的报告：在这颗星球的偏远地区，有几个太空旅商，主要是走私贩，连同飞船一起消失了。通过谨慎的调查，我们发现他们被迫在矿井里工作，远离窥探的视线。\n\n令人重视的是，[preftl_slavers_country.GetName]当局并不对此负责，这些事情是由当地的几个军阀和犯罪头子干的，此外所有被绑架的个体都违反了我们同[GetPreFTLUpperPlural]签订的协议。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1930`

### English

We have received disturbing reports from the [preftl_slavers_country.GetName] [GetPreFTLUpperPlural]: in remote regions of the planet, several spacefaring merchants, mainly smugglers, have disappeared along with their ships. A discreet investigation revealed that they were pressed into work in the mines, far from view.\n\nIt is important to note that this was not done by the [preftl_slavers_country.GetName] authorities, but rather a few local warlords and criminals, and that all of the abducted individuals were in violation of the treaties we signed with the [GetPreFTLUpperPlural].

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2354`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8050.a&lang=en)

### English

## fircon.8055.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8055.name&lang=en)

### 中文

奇迹般的争论

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1936`

### English

Miracle Hagglers

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2361`

## fircon.8055.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8055.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[preftl_hagglers_country.GetName][GetPreFTLUpperPlural]用行动证明了自己是天生的贸易商。我们自己的商人通过艰难的教训认识到当地人是如此有悟性、能说会道，当然他们对此不是很高兴。\n\n另一方面，我们的数名经济学家相信[GetPreFTLUpperPlural]的这些行为是可以效仿的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1937`

### English

The [preftl_hagglers_country.GetName] [GetPreFTLUpperPlural] have proven to be incredibly adept traders. Our own merchants are learning the hard way that the locals are both savvy and persuasive, and they are not happy about it.\n\nOn the other hand, several of our economists are convinced that the behavior of the [GetPreFTLUpperPlural] could be learned from.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2362`

## fircon.8055.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8055.a&lang=en)

### English

## preftl_hagglers_mod

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreftl_hagglers_mod&lang=en)

### 中文
