Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1944`

### English

Predictive Trading Algorithms

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2370`

## preftl_hagglers_mod_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apreftl_hagglers_mod_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对[preftl_hagglers_country.GetName][GetPreFTLUpperPlural]开展的研究，以及他们侵略性的贸易手段，帮助我们建立了许多令人信服的经济模型。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1945`

### English

Our studies of the [preftl_hagglers_country.GetName] [GetPreFTLUpperPlural] and their aggressive trading practices have produced some compelling economic models.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2371`

## fircon.8500.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8500.name&lang=en)

### 中文

科技的滥用

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1947`

### English

Misuse of Technology

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2374`

## fircon.8500.desc.regular

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8500.desc.regular&lang=en)

### 中文

为了促进善意，我们同意与[preftl_misusers_planet.GetName]的居民分享我们的物质复制技术。当然，我们希望它们用它来缓解资源匮乏的问题，但现在我们收到的报告显示，这项技术被武器化了。\n\n居民们有了一个大胆的想法，那就是反转复制单元的功能，这样它就不是组装预编程的物质，而是在原子水平上拆解生命体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1948`

### English

To foster good will, we agreed to share our matter replication technology with the inhabitants of [preftl_misusers_planet.GetName]. Of course, we expected them to use it to reduce resource scarcity, but now we are receiving reports that the technology is being weaponized.\n\nThe inhabitants have arrived at the bold idea of reversing the functionality of a replication unit so rather than assembling pre-programmed matter, the device can now be used to disassemble living beings at the atomic level.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2375`

## fircon.8500.desc.xenophobe

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8500.desc.xenophobe&lang=en)

### 中文

我们与[preftl_misusers_planet.GetName]的居民分享了我们的物质复制技术。它们本来应该用它来缓解资源匮乏的问题，但它们愚蠢地把它武器化了。它们反转了复制单元的功能，开始制造一个可以用来在原子水平上拆解生命体的装置。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1949`

### English

We shared our matter replication technology with the inhabitants of [preftl_misusers_planet.GetName]. They were supposed to use it to reduce resource scarcity. But they're foolishly trying to weaponize it. By crudely reversing the functionality of a replication unit, they have started building a device which can now be used to disassemble living beings at the atomic level.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2376`

## fircon.8500.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8500.a&lang=en)

### 中文

没收这项技术，不能相信它们会正确使用它。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1950`

### English

Confiscate the tech. They cannot be trusted to use it correctly.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2377`

## fircon.8500.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8500.b&lang=en)

### 中文

它们决定如何使用这项技术与我们无关。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1951`

### English

What they choose to do with the tech is not our concern.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2378`

## fircon.8505.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8505.name&lang=en)

### 中文

没收

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1952`

### English

Confiscation

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2379`

## fircon.8505.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8505.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们的特工到达时，[preftl_misusers_species.GetName]正好在完成它们的手持式原子分解器。我们的人明确告知，赠送物质复制装置不是为了让它们这样使用的。\n\n不出所料，它们不愿意放弃这项技术。当地政府火冒三丈，将我们的没收称作侵略行为。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1953`

### English

Our agents arrived just as the [preftl_misusers_species.GetName] were finalizing their hand-held atomic disintegrator. They were told in no uncertain terms that this was not the intended use for the matter replication unit we gifted them.\n\nAs expected, they were reluctant to part with the technology. Tempers flared, and the local government has labeled the confiscation an aggressive act.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2380`

## fircon.8510.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8510.name&lang=en)

### 中文

毁灭倾向

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1954`

### English

Destructive Tendencies

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2381`

## fircon.8510.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8510.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

通过对我们的物质复制技术进行逆向工程，[preftl_misusers_species.GetName]成功地制造了一个手持式原子分解器，并利用它大肆摧毁[preftl_misusers_planet.GetName]。\n\n在启动进一步的贸易协议之前，我们最好记住它们的毁灭倾向。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1955`

### English

By reverse engineering our matter replication technology, the [preftl_misusers_species.GetName] have successfully built a hand-held atomic disintegrator, and are using it to wreak havoc upon [preftl_misusers_planet.GetName].\n\nWe'd do well to remember their destructive tendencies before initiating further trade deals.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2382`

## tech_abusers

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_abusers&lang=en)

### 中文

科技滥用者

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1956`

### English

Tech Abusers

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2383`

## tech_abusers_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_abusers_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这颗行星上的居民以滥用科技而闻名。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1957`

### English

The inhabitants of this planet have been known to abuse technology.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2384`

## opinion_tech_abusers

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aopinion_tech_abusers&lang=en)

### 中文

他们不同意我们的选择。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1958`

### English

They disagree with our choices.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2385`

## opinion_tech_abusers_pro

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aopinion_tech_abusers_pro&lang=en)

### 中文

他们因我们的信任而尊重我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1959`

### English

They respect us for trusting them.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2386`

## fircon.8565.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8565.name&lang=en)

### 中文

入侵物种

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1961`

### English

Invasive Species

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2389`

## fircon.8565.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8565.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

从最近一次外交访问[preftl_invasives_planet.GetName]的[preftl_invasives_species.GetName]返回时，我们发现了一些不寻常的偷渡者。我们的穿梭机上出现了一群无翅的、蠕动的小型节肢动物。\n\n它们移动速度很快，而且很小，我们的传感器无法探测到它们。由于它们繁殖十分迅速，控制它们的入侵变得越来越困难。\n\n不幸的是，对[preftl_invasives_planet.GetName]的人们来说，这个物种是富足的象征。我们的应对方式可能会影响我们与[preftl_invasives_species.GetNamePlural]的关系。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1962`

### English

When we returned from a recent diplomatic visit with the [preftl_invasives_species.GetName] of [preftl_invasives_planet.GetName], we discovered we'd picked up some unusual stowaways. An infestation of small, wingless, wriggling arthropods had found their way aboard our shuttle.\n\nThey move fast, and are tiny enough that our sensors can't detect them. Given their rapid breeding, it has become increasingly difficult to contain the invasion.\n\nUnfortunately, the species is a symbol of plenty to the people of [preftl_invasives_planet.GetName]. How we respond to this could affect our relations with the [preftl_invasives_species.GetNamePlural].

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2390`

## fircon.8565.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8565.a&lang=en)

### 中文

通知该星球的统治者，我们要灭掉这些害虫。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1963`

### English

Inform the planet's rulers that we will be exterminating the infestation.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2391`

## fircon.8565.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8565.b&lang=en)

### 中文

悄悄处理掉这一情况，没必要侮辱它们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1964`

### English

Deal with this quietly. We need not insult them.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2392`

## fircon.8570.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8570.name&lang=en)

### 中文

净化

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1965`

### English

Decontamination

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2393`

## fircon.8570.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8570.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们把正在进行中的节肢动物入侵事件告知了[preftl_invasives_planet.GetName]的外交官。虽然我们的直率令它们吃惊，但它们最终还是感谢了我们的诚实。\n\n它们为净化过程中的疏忽而道歉，并提供给我们一种经过试验和测试的人道驱虫剂，在[preftl_invasives_planet.GetName]很受欢迎。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1966`

### English

We confronted the [preftl_invasives_planet.GetName] diplomats regarding the ongoing arthropod infestation. While taken aback by our directness, they ultimately appreciated our honesty.\n\nThey apologized for not being more thorough in their decontamination processes, and offered us a tried and tested cruelty free pest repellent, popular on [preftl_invasives_planet.GetName].

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2394`

## fircon.8570.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8570.a&lang=en)

### 中文

诚实就是最好的政策。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1967`

### English

Honesty is the best policy.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2395`

## fircon.8575.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8575.name&lang=en)

### 中文

虫害除尽

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1968`

### English

Bugs Repelled

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2396`

## fircon.8575.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8575.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

由于不想引起外交争端，我们试图悄悄地处理最近[preftl_invasives_planet.GetName]害虫泛滥的问题。\n\n由于这种生物繁殖快速，并且它们喜好吃非有机物质，这项工作并不轻松。\n\n当我们的外交官穿着被虫蛀得破烂不堪的衣服出现在一个会议上时，[preftl_invasives_species.GetNamePlural]一眼就知道发生了什么。\n\n它们似乎为这种生物感到尴尬，于是给我们提供了一种人道的驱虫剂。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1969`

### English

Not wishing to cause a diplomatic issue, we attempted to quietly deal with the recent infestation of [preftl_invasives_planet.GetName] pests.\n\nThis has not been a simple task given the rapid breeding of the creatures, and their penchant for eating through non-organic materials.\n\nWhen our diplomats turned up to a conference in tattered, bug-eaten attire, the [preftl_invasives_species.GetNamePlural] knew exactly what had happened.\n\nSeemingly embarrassed about introducing the creatures, they provided us with a cruelty-free pest repellent.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2397`

## fircon.8575.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8575.a&lang=en)

### 中文

污染已经消除了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1970`

### English

The infestation is neutralized.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2398`

## straight_talkers

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astraight_talkers&lang=en)

### 中文

有话直说

### English

Straight Talkers

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Astraight_talkers_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们实话实说。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1972`

### English

We tell it as it is.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2400`

## no_worries

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ano_worries&lang=en)

### 中文

无需担心

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1973`

### English

No Worries

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2401`

## no_worries_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Ano_worries_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们不是爱闹事的人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1974`

### English

We're not ones to fuss.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2402`

## fircon.8531.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8531.name&lang=en)

### 中文

政府的特色

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1976`

### English

Flavors of Government

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2405`

## fircon.8531.base.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8531.base.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[preftl_different_flavors_planet.GetName]上的[preftl_different_flavors_species.GetNamePlural]震惊地发现，我们的政府组织形式与他们的政府不同。令人难以置信的是，纵观整个行星的历史，当地人从未经历过这种权力结构。显然，人们对于我们是否具备开展有效外交的能力产生了担忧。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1977`

### English

The [preftl_different_flavors_species.GetNamePlural] on [preftl_different_flavors_planet.GetName] were shocked to learn that our government differs from their own. Incredibly, throughout the planet's history, its people have never experienced such a power structure. Obviously, this raises concerns regarding our ability to engage in effective diplomacy.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2406`

## fircon.8531.democratic.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8531.democratic.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$fircon.8531.base.desc$\n\n他们不知道民主带给了我们什么样的好处，也不知道自由的真正味道。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1978`

### English

$fircon.8531.base.desc$\n\nThey know nothing of the benefits that democracy can bring, or the true taste of freedom.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2407`

## fircon.8531.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8531.a&lang=en)

### 中文

将我们的三观传授给他们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1979`

### English

Teach them our ways.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2408`

## fircon.8531.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8531.b&lang=en)

### 中文

求同存异。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1980`

### English

Respect their differences.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2409`

## fircon.8535.imperialist.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8535.imperialist.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$fircon.8531.base.desc$\n\n他们必须了解是什么让我们雄伟和强大的国度如此的雄伟强大。他们尚未品尝过帝国主义的滋味。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1981`

### English

$fircon.8531.base.desc$\n\nThey must learn what makes our great and powerful domain so great and powerful. They have yet to experience the taste of empire.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2410`

## fircon.8540.dictatorship.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8540.dictatorship.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$fircon.8531.base.desc$\n\n我们应该将他们从这种愚行中解放出来。独裁政权的滋味将让他们重新放飞自我。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1982`

### English

$fircon.8531.base.desc$\n\nWe ought to liberate them from such folly. The taste of dictatorship shall set them free.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2411`

## fircon.8545.oligarchy.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8545.oligarchy.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$fircon.8531.base.desc$\n\n将权力集中在精心挑选的一小部分人手中是统治的唯一方法。他们是如何意识不到这一点的，这真是一个谜。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1983`

### English

$fircon.8531.base.desc$\n\nConcentrating powers among a select few is the only way to rule. How they have not come to realize this on their own is a true mystery.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2412`

## fircon.8550.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8550.name&lang=en)

### 中文

渐行渐近

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1984`

### English

More Like Us

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2413`

## fircon.8550.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8550.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在将我们的政治哲学传授给他们之后，[preftl_different_flavors_planet.GetName]上的[preftl_different_flavors_species.GetNamePlural]沿袭我们的方式，开始了政体改革。\n\n这些计划正在全球范围内推广，迎来[preftl_different_flavors_species.GetNamePlural]的一个新时代。为了纪念这一政治意义上翻天覆地的巨大变革，新的[preftl_different_flavors_species.GetNamePlural]政府举行了一场向我们致敬的游行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1985`

### English

After instructing them in our political philosophy, the [preftl_different_flavors_species.GetNamePlural] on [preftl_different_flavors_planet.GetName] have taken steps towards reforming their government along the lines of our own.\n\nThese initiatives are taking place on a planetary scale, ringing in a new era for the [preftl_different_flavors_species.GetNamePlural]. To mark this dramatic political overhaul, the new [preftl_different_flavors_species.GetNamePlural] government held a parade in our honor.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2414`

## fircon.8555.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8555.name&lang=en)

### 中文

外交分歧

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1986`

### English

Diplomatic Differences

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2415`

## fircon.8555.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8555.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在学习了我们如何运用权力之后，[preftl_different_flavors_species.GetNamePlural]选择维持[preftl_different_flavors_planet.GetName]上的现状，认为这才是最好的进步方式。\n\n他们已经宣誓要守住自己的本心，绝不因外部势力的影响而动摇。\n\n外交仍然在继续进行，但毫无疑问，他们不像之前那么信任我们了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1987`

### English

Having learned of our approach to power, the [preftl_different_flavors_species.GetNamePlural] maintain that status quo on [preftl_different_flavors_planet.GetName] remains the best way forward.\n\nThey have vowed to stand their ground, and not be swayed by external forces.\n\nDiplomacy continues, but there can be no doubt they trust us less.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2416`

## fircon.8560.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8560.name&lang=en)

### 中文

友谊长存，生生不息

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1988`

### English

Live Long And Let Live

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2417`

## fircon.8560.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8560.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

相较于向对方施加压力，我们更乐于冷静地向[preftl_different_flavors_planet.GetName]上的[preftl_different_flavors_species.GetNamePlural]解释我们的政体权力制度背后的相关理论。\n\n他们深深地折服于我们就不同形式政府模式所阐述的观点，对于我们不曾尝试强迫他们服从这一点，他们表示了感激。\n\n双方各自的外交官已经缔结了长期的联系纽带，一同庆祝一场共享的宴会。基于互惠的条款，外交持续推进。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1989`

### English

Rather than applying any pressure outright, we calmly explained the theory behind our governing authority to the [preftl_different_flavors_species.GetNamePlural] on [preftl_different_flavors_planet.GetName].\n\nThey were intrigued by the insights we provided into alternative styles of government, and were vocal in their appreciation that we made no attempt to force them into compliance.\n\nOur respective diplomats have formed lasting ties, celebrating together over a shared banquet. Diplomacy continues on good terms.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2418`

### English

Respect is a two way hyperlane.

## fircon.8000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8000.name&lang=en)

### 中文

仇外心理

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1995`

### English

Xenophiliacs

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2426`

## fircon.8000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在一个有趣的跨文化交流例子中，[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]上相当数量的年轻人对[owner.GetAdj]的所有事物产生了消费性的兴趣。这些充满激情的年轻人急切地争抢与我们文化相关的任何东西，现在他们构成了这颗星球上一个充满活力和不断发展的亚文化群体。\n\n我们甚至得知即将到来的粉丝大会将举办一场服装大赛。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:1996`

### English

In an amusing example of cross-cultural exchange, a significant number of young people on [pre_ftl_planet.GetName] have developed a consuming interest in all things [owner.GetAdj]. Eagerly snatching up anything even remotely connected to our culture, these passionate youngsters now comprise a vibrant and growing subculture on the planet.\n\nWe've even been told that an upcoming fan convention will feature a costume contest.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2427`

## fircon.8000.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8000.a&lang=en)

### English

### English

### English

## fircon.8001.name

扮装文化

### English

Costume Culture

## fircon.8001.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8001.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]上的[owner.GetAdj]大会取得了令人振奋的成功！人们不分老幼都聚集在这颗星球上最大的城市，欢庆[owner.GetName]的一切事物。除了大会带来的好意以外，它还为当地经济提供了急需的推动力。\n\n最精彩的也许是扮装比赛，大奖颁发给了我们[owner.GetRulerTitle][owner.GetRulerName]的死忠粉丝。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2003`

### English

The [owner.GetAdj] convention on [pre_ftl_planet.GetName] has been a rousing success! Young and old alike gathered in the planet's largest city to celebrate all things [owner.GetName]. Apart from the good will the convention generated, it also provided a much needed boost to the local economy.\n\nBest of all perhaps was the costume contest, in which the grand prize was awarded to a particularly devoted fan of our own [owner.GetRulerTitle] [owner.GetRulerName].

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2435`

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8001.a&lang=en)

### English

## fircon.8002.name

### English

## fircon.8002.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8002.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在我们利用影响力遏制了[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]上蓬勃发展的[owner.GetAdj]粉丝群之后，那里的人们都团结起来以传统方式表达自己的文化。因此，艺术和文化部门的产出有了明显的改善。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2007`

### English

After we used our influence to curtail the burgeoning [owner.GetAdj] fandom on [pre_ftl_planet.GetName], the people there have rallied around traditional expressions of their own culture. Accordingly, output in the arts and culture sectors has markedly improved.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2440`

## pre_ftl_diplomacy_02

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Apre_ftl_diplomacy_02&lang=en)

### 中文

艺术百花齐放。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2008`

### English

Flourishing of the arts.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2441`

## fircon.8003.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8003.name&lang=en)

### 中文

再无粉丝

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2009`

### English

Fans No More

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2443`

## fircon.8003.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8003.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对[pre_ftl_planet.GetName]上的[owner.GetAdj]粉丝采取的强硬态度引起了巨大的反弹。这颗星球上的许多人开始推崇自己的文化和传统，激烈反对[owner.GetAdj]。\n\n这将如何影响两颗星球的外交关系，还有待观察

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2010`

### English

Our hardline approach to the [owner.GetAdj] fandom on [pre_ftl_planet.GetName] has precipitated a significant backlash. Rallying around their own culture and traditions, many on the planet have grown virulently anti-[owner.GetAdj].\n\nHow this may impact diplomatic relations between our worlds remains to be seen

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2444`

## fircon.8003.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8003.a&lang=en)

我们不需要他们的喜爱。

### English

We don't need their love.

## fircon.8060.name

### 中文

市场波动

### English

Market Fluctuations

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2450`

## fircon.8060.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8060.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

自己在宇宙中并不孤单，这一发现让[pre_ftl_country.Capital.GetName]上的许多人感到震惊。毫不奇怪，这一事件产生了严重的经济影响。随着整颗星球上的金融机构暴跌不止，生活水平明显下降。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2015`

### English

The revelation that they were not alone in the universe came as a shock to many on [pre_ftl_country.Capital.GetName]. Not surprisingly, the event has had severe economic effects. With financial institutions across the planet in free-fall, there has been a marked decrease in the planet's standard of living.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2451`

## fircon.8060.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8060.a&lang=en)

### English

### English

## fircon.8065.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8065.name&lang=en)

### 中文

市场已稳定

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2018`

### English

Markets Stabilized

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2455`

## fircon.8065.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8065.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们为[pre_ftl_country.Capital.GetName]提供的金融刺激措施已经得到了丰厚的回报。他们的传统货币和经济机构在我们的支持下，终于稳定了下来。\n\n也许更重要的是，公众对我们继续留在该星系中的支持度空前高涨。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2019`

### English

The financial stimulus we provided to [pre_ftl_country.Capital.GetName] has paid off handsomely. With their traditional currencies and economic institutions bolstered by our support, conditions have finally stabilized.\n\nPerhaps more importantly, public approval for our continued presence in the system has never been higher.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2456`

## fircon.8065.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8065.a&lang=en)

代价很大，但是必要的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2020`

### English

Costly, but necessary.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2457`

## opinion_pre_ftl_diplomacy_02

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aopinion_pre_ftl_diplomacy_02&lang=en)

### 中文

金融天才。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2021`

### English

Finance wizards.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2458`

## fircon.8070.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8070.name&lang=en)

### 中文

市场知道什么最好

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2022`

### English

Market Knows Best

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2460`

## fircon.8070.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8070.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对市场仁慈的信任得到了丰厚的回报。\n\n摆脱了令人窒息的监管，[pre_ftl_country.Capital.GetName]的金融机构已经稳定下来。那里的人们知道自己的经济前景一片大好，可以放心了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2023`

### English

Our trust in the benevolence of the market has paid off handsomely.\n\nFreed from the imposition of stifling regulations, financial institutions on [pre_ftl_country.Capital.GetName] have stabilized. The people there can rest secure in the knowledge that their economic future is assured.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2461`

## fircon.8075.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8075.name&lang=en)

### 中文

世纪大崩溃

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2024`

### English

Once in a Generation Collapse

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2463`

## fircon.8075.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8075.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对[pre_ftl_country.Capital.GetName]市场自我纠正的信任变成了一场灾难，实在令人费解。事实上，星球上的状况前所未有的糟糕，人们吵着要政府加强对金融部门的监督。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2025`

### English

Inexplicably, our trust in the market to right itself on [pre_ftl_country.Capital.GetName] has been a disaster. In fact, conditions on the planet have never been worse, and the people there are clamoring for increased government oversight in the financial sector.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2464`

## economic_whoopsie_tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aeconomic_whoopsie_tt&lang=en)

### 中文

糟糕。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2026`

### English

Oops.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2465`

## fircon.8515.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8515.name&lang=en)

### 中文

孤独子个体

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2039`

### English

The Lone Drone

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2480`

## fircon.8515.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8515.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在[preftl_lone_drone_planet.GetName]地表进行外交访问时，有一个孤独的[preftl_lone_drone_species.GetName]接近了我们。虽然[preftl_lone_drone_species.GetNamePlural]是一个格式化的物种，但这个子个体认为自己是一个个体，现在向我们请求庇护。\n\n它看到我们后显然是受到了启发，希望融入我们的社会。当被询问时，它反复说自己无法融入蜂巢，并声称自己生来就一直拥有独立的意识。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2040`

### English

While on a diplomatic visit to the surface of [preftl_lone_drone_planet.GetName], we were approached by a lone [preftl_lone_drone_species.GetName]. Although the [preftl_lone_drone_species.GetNamePlural] are a gestalt species, the drone in question identifies as an individual, and is now requesting asylum.\n\nApparently inspired by what they have seen of us, they wish to integrate themselves into our society. When questioned, they repeatedly stated that they are unable to fit in within the hive, and claim to have possessed an individual consciousness all their life.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2481`

## fircon.8515.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8515.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们现在承担不起激怒蜂巢的后果。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2041`

### English

We cannot afford to antagonize the hive.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2482`

## fircon.8515.b

同意庇护。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2042`

### English

Grant the asylum.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2483`

## fircon.8520.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8520.name&lang=en)

### 中文

再同化

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2043`

### English

Reassimilation

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2484`

## fircon.8520.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8520.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们拒绝了孤独的[preftl_lone_drone_species.GetName]子个体的庇护请求。我们认为这对双方都是最好的。\n\n没有得到我们的合作，[preftl_lone_drone_species.GetName]回到了同胞身边，乞求得到独立。\n\n[preftl_lone_drone_species.GetNamePlural]向我们保证这只是一个反常子个体，它后来恢复了理智，并回到了集体中。这个所谓的个体不会再出现在我们面前。\n\n与[preftl_lone_drone_planet.GetName]的外交继续进行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2044`

### English

We refused the lone [preftl_lone_drone_species.GetName] drone's request for asylum. We believed it was in the best interest of both parties.\n\nWithout our cooperation, the [preftl_lone_drone_species.GetName] returned to their people, to plead for their independence.\n\nThe [preftl_lone_drone_species.GetNamePlural] have assured us this was merely a deviant drone who has since seen sense, and has returned to the collective. We won't hear from the so-called individual again.\n\nDiplomacy with [preftl_lone_drone_planet.GetName] continues.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2485`

## fircon.8525.name

### 中文

加入我们

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2045`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2486`

## fircon.8525.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.8525.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

起初，来自[preftl_lone_drone_planet.GetName]的孤独子个体被巨大的转变所吓倒。然而，在最初的文化冲击消退后，子个体现在正在适应作为个体的生活。也许具有讽刺意味的是，它已经变得像我们一样了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2046`

### English

At first, the lone [preftl_lone_drone_species.GetName] drone from [preftl_lone_drone_planet.GetName] was overwhelmed by the scale of the transition. However, after the initial culture shock subsided, the drone is now adjusting to life as an individual. Perhaps ironically, they have become like us.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2487`

## site_the_chosen_backstory_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Asite_the_chosen_backstory_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

一座环绕着§Y[From.Planet.MoonOf.GetName]§!的废弃观测站。从中可以一窥在这个行星上的前超光速的§Y$NAME_Charynoi$§!文明的起源。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2072`

### English

An abandoned observation outpost orbits §Y[From.Planet.MoonOf.GetName]§!. It may shed some light on the origins of the pre-FTL §Y$NAME_Charynoi$§! civilization on the planet below.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2519`

## arcsite.3000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aarcsite.3000.name&lang=en)

### 中文

$NAME_Charynoi$博物馆

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2073`

### English

The $NAME_Charynoi$ Museum

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2521`

## arcsite.3000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aarcsite.3000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[From.Planet.MoonOf.GetName]上的废弃观测站似乎在报废前不久被改为他用。尽管上面还有用于监视行星居民的设备的证据，但看来大部分都改成了$NAME_Charynoi$的历史博物馆。奇怪的是，明明这是一个好斗的物种，但是关于暴力的主题被刻意回避了，展品主要集中在一些关于和平和生育的仪式上。\n\n在中控室发现了一系列数据水晶。显然这就是我们想要找的东西。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2074`

### English

The abandoned observation post above [From.Planet.MoonOf.GetName] appears to have been repurposed shortly before it was abandoned. Though there is equipment to surveil the inhabitants of the planet in evidence, most of it seems to be dedicated to a museum of $NAME_Charynoi$ history. Curiously for such a warlike species, subjects relating to violence seem to have been avoided, and exhibits appear to show various rituals of peace and fertility.\n\nA series of data crystals have been left in the control room. It is evident that we were supposed to find them.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2522`

## arcsite.3000.A

## arcsite.3005.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aarcsite.3005.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

第一块数据水晶已经完成了翻译。其封面上写着“这是$NAME_Charynoi$民族的记录，系女先知伊蒂卡里奥斯所留，这样那些可能找到它的人能原谅我们的罪过。”\n\n其后是一份历史、哲学和艺术工作的合辑，可能是由一个比较标准但稍微好斗的晚工业革命社会所创。我们的[Owner.GetLinguists]将其发回[Owner.Capital.GetName]以进行进一步研究。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2077`

### English

The first crystal has been translated. It is prefaced with the words "This is the record of the $NAME_Charynoi$ people, left behind by the Prophetess Ithikarios, so that those who might find it may forgive us our sins."\n\nWhat follows is a collection of historical, philosophical and artistic works as may have been created by a fairly standard, if somewhat belligerent, late industrial society. Our [Owner.GetLinguists] have sent it back to [Owner.Capital.GetName] for further analysis.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2526`

## arcsite.3010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aarcsite.3010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

“现在我将叙述一切是如何走向错误——我们最初陷入傲慢罪恶的地方，”伊蒂卡里奥斯宣称。\n\n第二颗晶体记录了$NAME_Charynoi$是如何逐渐揭开太空飞行的奥秘，似乎比[Root.Owner.GetName]早了大约一百年。他们在自己的星系中发现了两颗美丽的行星，并得出结论认为这些行星是神灵留给他们的产业，是他们与生俱来的权利，他们现在正在索取这些权利。\n\n伊蒂卡里奥斯用惊恐的语气讲述了这些早期探索的黑暗面：在他们的新殖民地中，初生的文明并不愿意放弃他们的家园。他们被打败并被奴役，最终逐渐消亡，死于$NAME_Charynoi$不屑于治疗的疾病。\n\n我们的[Owner.GetLinguists]正在努力翻译第三个也是最后一个数据晶体。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2080`

### English

"Now I shall recount where it all went wrong - where we first fell to the sin of arrogance," declaims Ithikarios.\n\nThe second crystal recounts how the $NAME_Charynoi$ gradually uncovered the mysteries of space flight, seemingly about a hundred years before the [Root.Owner.GetName]. They found two beautiful planets in their own system, and concluded that those had been bequeathed to them by the Gods as their birthright, which they were now claiming.\n\nIn horrified tones, Ithikarios recounts the darker side to these early exploits: nascent civilizations on their new colonies had not willingly yielded their homes. Beaten into submission, they were enslaved and eventually dwindled, falling to diseases the $NAME_Charynoi$ did not care to cure.\n\nOur [Owner.GetLinguists] are hard at work translating the third and final data crystal.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2530`

叛徒的诡计

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2081`

### English

The Traitor's Gambit

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2532`

## arcsite.3015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aarcsite.3015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

最终，我们耗尽了我们所拥有的空间。我们知道虫洞的存在，但是它们无法被我们研究。我们的领土物产丰富景色优美，很容易让我们相信自己是天之选民。虫洞是一种考验，只要通过了考验，我们就能将自己的种族传遍整个星系，这是我们的神圣使命。\n\n我曾经是那些试图履行这个职责的首领之一。当灵感降临在我身上——我意识到掌握了打开虫洞的钥匙——我便在祖先神社中祈祷了几天。最终得到了启示：我们不配。如果我们穿过虫洞，我们只会找到痛苦。$NAME_Aspharelle$将被清除，作为我们所犯罪行的惩罚。我决定销毁我的研究，并寻找一条不同的路。\n\n要找到可以信任的人很难。如果我们被发现了，我们肯定宁可快速死亡。虽然我比以往任何时候都更加坚信我们是正确的，但我们会被视为叛教者。最终，我们启动了一个大胆的计划：在假装盲目飞越虫洞的愚蠢尝试的掩护下，我们建造了一艘方舟船，航行到未知的地方，朝着一个遥远的星球。我们经历了多年穿越空旷的太空，直到我们到达目的地。现在，我们只希望被留在和平中过上品德高尚的生活，以证明并非所有$NAME_Charynoi$都是邪恶的。\n\n伊蒂卡里奥斯为她的兄弟们对我们造成的任何伤害深表歉意，她提到她试图在[From.Planet.MoonOf.GetName]周围竖起一个坚不可摧的盾牌，这样她的人民就不会再受到诱惑去寻找星辰。她失败了，但是我们的[Owner.GetResearchers]已经能够解决她的问题。现在，如果我们愿意，我们现在就可以竖起这个盾牌。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2082`

### English

"Eventually, we exhausted the confines of the space we had been bequeathed. We knew of the existence of the Wormhole, but it defied our study. Our realm was so bountiful, so full of beauty, that it was not hard to convince ourselves that we were the Chosen. That the Wormhole was a test, and once we were worthy, it would be our Holy Purpose to spread our kind across all the galaxy.\n\n"I was once chief amongst those seeking to fulfill this duty. When inspiration came to me - when I realized I held the key to the opening of the Wormhole - I prayed for days at the Shrine of my Ancestors. And eventually: Revelation. We were not worthy. If we went beyond the Wormhole, we would find only pain. $NAME_Aspharelle$ would be scoured, out of punishment for the crimes we had committed. I resolved to destroy my research and seek a different path.\n\n"It was hard to find those who I could trust. If we were discovered, we would surely have longed for a quick death. Though I am more convinced than ever that we are right, we would have been seen as apostates. Eventually, we launched a daring plan: under the guise of making a fool attempt to fly the wormhole blind, we built an arkship to sail into the unknown, towards a distant star. Many years, we traveled through empty space, until we came to our destination. Now we wish nothing more than to be left in peace to live virtuous lives, so that we may prove that not all $NAME_Charynoi$ are evil."\n\nApologizing profusely for any harm done to us by her brethren, Ithikarios mentions her attempts at erecting an impenetrable shield around [From.Planet.MoonOf.GetName], so that her people would never again be tempted to seek the stars. She failed, but our [Owner.GetResearchers] have been able to work out where she went wrong. We could erect the shield now, if we so desire.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2533`

## fircon.3510.name

### 中文

天选民

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2105`

### English

The Chosen

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2564`

## fircon.3510.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.3510.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们被困在虫洞里面太久了。但等等！终于，上天的恩赐帮助我们我们突破了施加于身上的桎梏。我们终于通过了考验。星系的其他地区都在等待着我们去征服。\n\n不要反抗。从长远来看，这样会更好。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2106`

### English

For too long have we been trapped behind the wormhole. But behold! Finally, we have breached the restrictions placed upon us by our celestial heritage. Now, at last, we know we are worthy. The rest of the galaxy is ours to claim.\n\nDo not resist. It will be better in the long run, that way.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2565`

## fircon.3510.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.3510.A&lang=en)

### 中文

好吧，真是出乎意料。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2107`

### English

Well that was unexpected.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2566`

## fircon.3515.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.3515.name&lang=en)

### 中文

天选民

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2108`

### English

The Chosen

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2568`

## fircon.3515.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.3515.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

什……？你……突破了虫洞？怎么做到的？我们的科学家已经研究了很多年都没有成功。这肯定哪里出问题了。\n\n……\n……\n……\n\n这肯定是场考验。我不信任你们！我们必须从星系中清除你们这些东西！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2109`

### English

Wha-? You... breached the wormhole? But how? Our best scientists have been working on it for years, without success. There must be some kind of mistake.\n\n...\n...\n...\n\nThis must be a test. You are not to be trusted! We must cleanse our stars of your kind!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2569`

## eager_explorers_base_effect

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aeager_explorers_base_effect&lang=en)

### 中文

开局拥有2艘§H$explorer_vessel_plural$§!，以及一艘配备§H亚空间引擎§!的§H$engineer_vessel$§!，这使它们无法进行超空间旅行，但能提供有限的跃迁引擎能力。\n提升§H超光速旅行§!科技的发现概率。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2120`

### English

Start with 2 §H$explorer_vessel_plural$§! and an §H$engineer_vessel$§! equipped with §HSubspace Drives§!, making them incapable of Hyperlane travel but granting limited Jump Drive abilities.\nGain an increased chance to discover §HFTL Travel§! Technologies

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2584`

## civic_eager_explorers_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_eager_explorers_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$CHALLENGING_CIVIC$出于对星空的渴望，这个文明在好几个发展阶段都加速了进程，比大多数国家更早地达到了探索星海的时代。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2123`

### English

$CHALLENGING_CIVIC$Yearning for the stars, this civilization sped through several stages of development, reaching the age of galactic exploration far earlier than most.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2588`

### English

## civic_privatized_exploration_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_privatized_exploration_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$CHALLENGING_CIVIC$多年来，这个文明被告知超空间引擎几乎就要完成了。他们耐心地等待着，然而一直以来，负责该项目的子公司都在悄悄地将资金转入他们自己的私人账户。当欺诈行径最终被揭露时，公司只能仓促之下提供实验性、探索性的船只。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2126`

### English

$CHALLENGING_CIVIC$For years, this civilization was told that the hyperdrive was almost finished. Patiently they waited, yet all the while, the subsidiary in charge of the project was quietly diverting the funds into their own, private accounts. When the fraud was finally revealed, the Corporation hastened to provide experimental, exploratory vessels.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2591`

## civic_privatized_exploration_effects

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_privatized_exploration_effects&lang=en)

### 中文

$eager_explorers_base_effect$\n开局就拥有§H$tech_starbase_3$§!科技，并且在母星系有一座已经建成的§H$tech_starbase_3$§!。\n£building£§Y$building_private_mining_consortium$§!$megacorp_civic_branch_office_building_effect$

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2127`

### English

$eager_explorers_base_effect$\nStart with the §H$tech_starbase_3$§! technology and a §H$tech_starbase_3$§! already built in your home system.\nThe £building£ §Y$building_private_mining_consortium$§! $megacorp_civic_branch_office_building_effect$

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2592`

## civic_hive_stargazers

### 中文

星眸人

### English

Stargazers

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2593`

## civic_hive_stargazers_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_hive_stargazers_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$CHALLENGING_CIVIC$在凝视着夜空的奇迹之后，这个蜂巢思维一直渴望向外探索。为了追求这一雄心壮志，其他形式的发展被搁置一边，并建造了一支能够进行有限亚空间旅行的小型船队。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2129`

### English

$CHALLENGING_CIVIC$Gazing with wonder at the night sky, this Hive has always yearned for exploration. In pursuit of this ambition, other forms of development were sidelined, and a small fleet of vessels capable of limited subspace travel was constructed.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2594`

## civic_hive_stargazers_effects

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_hive_stargazers_effects&lang=en)

### 中文

$eager_explorers_base_effect$\n添加§H$trait_stargazer$§!特质。\n开局时对起始星团的情报为低。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2130`

### English

$eager_explorers_base_effect$\nAdds the §H$trait_stargazer$§! trait.\nBegin with low Intel about your starting cluster.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2595`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2596`

## civic_machine_exploration_protocol_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_machine_exploration_protocol_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$CHALLENGING_CIVIC$这个机械智能中央程序的基石是对外行星生命进行编录的冲动。为了追求这一指令，它将自身资源的绝大多数都用于建造一支小型船队，进行有限的亚空间旅行。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2132`

### English

$CHALLENGING_CIVIC$The foundation of this Machine Intelligence's central programming is the drive to catalog exoplanetary life. In pursuit of this directive, it has dedicated much of its resources to crafting a small fleet of vessels capable of limited subspace travel.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2597`

## civic_machine_exploration_protocol_effects

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Acivic_machine_exploration_protocol_effects&lang=en)

### 中文

$eager_explorers_base_effect$\n观测前超光速文明可获得额外§G50%§!的洞见进度。\n每次完成建立第一次接触可获得£unity£§H凝聚力§!。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2133`

### English

$eager_explorers_base_effect$\nGain §G50%§! more Insight progress from observing pre-FTL Civilizations.\nGain £unity£§HUnity§! every time you finish establishing first contact.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2598`

## tech_subspace_drive_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Atech_subspace_drive_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

这些亚空间驱动引擎是在离开我们的家园的迫切愿望下诞生的一种方案，它的设计目的是用来进行有限的星际旅行。虽然它们只能进行小规模跃迁，但仍提供了一个千载难逢的机会来启动我们的星海探索。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2159`

### English

An insight borne of a desperate desire to leave our home, these Subspace Drive engines were designed to facilitate limited interstellar travel. While only allowing small-scale jumps, they provide a unique opportunity to jumpstart our galactic exploration.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2629`

## SUBSPACE_DRIVE_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASUBSPACE_DRIVE_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

亚空间引擎能够在时空上创造细微的裂缝，让舰船能够跃迁到临近的恒星系。如果跃迁的距离太长，这个过程会变得非常不稳定。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2163`

### English

The Subspace Drive creates microtears in spacetime, allowing vessels to jump to nearby systems. The process becomes incredibly unstable if longer jumps are attempted.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2635`

## fircon.5000.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.5000.name&lang=en)

### 中文

缺失轨道

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2188`

### English

Missing Orbitals

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2668`

## fircon.5000.desc.base

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.5000.desc.base&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[From.GetName]§!少了几颗星球。\n\n所有的计算都表明应该还有几个天体存在，但在这个星系中却找不到它们的踪迹。传感器已经重新校准，数字也重新核对过，但得出的结论被现实所推翻。我们已经在自己的星图上标记出了失踪小行星的位置。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2189`

### English

§Y[From.GetName]§! is missing planets.\n\nAll calculations point to the existence of several more celestial bodies, yet they are nowhere to be found in the system. Sensors have been recalibrated and numbers rechecked, but the theory stands disproven by reality. We have marked the missing planetoids positions on our star charts.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2669`

## fircon.5000.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.5000.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

$fircon.5000.desc.base$\n\n或许再靠近些扫描这个星系会得到更多信息。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2190`

### English

$fircon.5000.desc.base$\n\nPerhaps a closer scan of the system could reveal more.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2670`

## fircon.5005.desc.open

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.5005.desc.open&lang=en)

### 中文

$fircon.5000.desc.base$\n\n我们认为达恰星系中相邻的[owner.GetPreFTLLowerPlural]与这一状况有关。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2191`

### English

$fircon.5000.desc.base$\n\nWe gather that the neighboring [owner.GetPreFTLLowerPlural] in the Dacha system are connected to this.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2672`

## fircon.5005.desc.closed

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.5005.desc.closed&lang=en)

### 中文

$fircon.5000.desc.base$\n\n看来是过去达恰星系中相邻的[owner.GetPreFTLLowerPlural]造就了这一怪异的星系。然而通往那里的超空间航道却消失了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2192`

### English

$fircon.5000.desc.base$\n\nIt seems like the previously neighboring [owner.GetPreFTLLowerPlural] in the Dacha system created this strange system. Yet the hyperlane leading there has since disappeared.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2673`

## fircon.5010.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.5010.name&lang=en)

### 中文

星球大盗

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2193`

### English

Grand Theft Planet

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2675`

## fircon.5010.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.5010.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

那些星球是被移动了！\n\n我们在§Y[from.solar_system.GetName]§!星系探测到的异常超空间航道都连接着我们认为应该存在星球的位置。在研究了这些怪异的航道后，我们发现它们都通向同一个地方。一个叫做§Y达恰§!的恒星系，本以为是无法到达的，但如果把这些狭窄的超空间航道合并起来，我们就能送一艘科研船过去。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2194`

### English

The planets were moved!\n\nThe abnormal hyperlanes we detected in the §Y[from.solar_system.GetName]§! system were each tethered to where we previously had expected to discover worlds. After studying these strange lanes, we realize they all lead to the same place. A star system called §YDacha§!, which was thought unreachable, yet if we were to combine all these slender hyperlanes, we could pass a science ship through.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2676`

## fircon.5010.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.5010.a&lang=en)

### 中文

找到超空间航道通往何处！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2195`

### English

Find out where the hyperlanes lead!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2677`

## fircon.5010.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.5010.b&lang=en)

### 中文

我们真的想弄清楚吗？

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2196`

### English

Do we really want to know?

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2678`

## fircon.5015.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.5015.name&lang=en)

### 中文

丰饶诸星

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2197`

### English

Worlds of Plenty

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2680`

## fircon.5015.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.5015.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

围绕着§Y[From.GetName]§!星系三颗恒星的每颗行星都是生态天堂，即盖亚星球，每一颗都能维持有机生命。由于这些星球上没有污染，起初我们认为它们可能无人居住。然而在进行了更深入的扫描后，我们在每颗星球上都发现了横亘整颗星球的大型城市。\n\n更奇怪的是，似乎只有环绕中心恒星的行星是属于这个星系的。其他的恒星则由来自$NAME_Helito$和其他更遥远的星系的行星环绕。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2198`

### English

Orbiting each of the three stars in the §Y[From.GetName]§! system are ecological paradises, Gaia worlds, each capable of sustaining organic life. Initially, we thought these might be uninhabited, given the lack of pollution. Yet after performing deeper scans we discovered large sprawling cities spanning each planet from end to end.\n\nStranger still, it seems like only the central star has planets originally from this system. The others are orbited by planets from $NAME_Helito$ and other, more remote systems.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2681`

## fircon.5015.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.5015.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这……怎么可能？！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2199`

### English

But... How!?

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2682`

Those worlds would look better with [Owner.GetSpeciesNamePlural] on them.

## fircon.5020.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.5020.name&lang=en)

### 中文

遭遇哈宾特统一星球国

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2204`

### English

Habinte Unified Worlds Encountered

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2688`

## fircon.5020.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.5020.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

调查一颗新的盖亚星球通常是一个重大事件，但它将成为[Root.GetName]的船员在未来几个月里的例行公事。这些星球包含着一些非常奇怪的[Owner.GetPreFTLLowerPlural]，它们显然还没有实现宇宙飞行，但它们的可再生能源生产可以媲美我们最高效的反应堆。\n\n更奇怪的是资源的平均分配。每颗星球都有生存所需的一切，这应该是不可能的。我们看到只在一颗星球出产的货物抵达另一颗星球，却没有一艘运输船起飞。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2205`

### English

Surveying a new Gaia world is usually a momentous event but for the crew of the [Root.GetName] it is going to become routine for the next few months. The multiple worlds house some very strange [Owner.GetPreFTLLowerPlural], they obviously have not achieved spaceflight yet their renewable energy production rivals our most efficient reactors.\n\nA further oddity is the equal spread of resources. Every world has everything they need to survive, which should be impossible. We are seeing goods only produced on one planet arrive on another without a single transport ship taking flight.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2689`

## fircon.5020.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.5020.a&lang=en)

### 中文

我们要赶快建立一座观测哨站！

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2206`

### English

We need an observation outpost now!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2690`

## fircon.5026.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.5026.name&lang=en)

### 中文

星球之间的桥梁

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2207`

### English

Bridge Between Worlds

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2692`

## fircon.5026.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.5026.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§Y[From.solar_system.GetName]§!的[GetPreFTLLowerPlural]在某种程度上比我们更善于运用超空间航道理论。它们的每一颗星球都通过一座人造超空间航道网络相互连接。这些航道用来向其他星球运送必要的资源，如食物或建筑材料，而我们不知道它们是如何做到这一点的。\n\n要求更多的[Root.GetScientistPlural]来研究这些星球的请求已经得到批准。虽然从它们的行星监听站的活动来看，它们应该也在观察我们。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2208`

### English

The [GetPreFTLLowerPlural] of §Y[From.solar_system.GetName]§! are somehow more adept with hyperlane theory than we are. Every one of their worlds are linked to each other through an artificial hyperlane network. These lanes are used transport necessary resources like food or building materials to their other worlds just using planet-bound trains, and we have no idea how they did it.\n\nA request for more [Root.GetScientistPlural] to study these worlds has been approved. Though judging by the activity at their planetary listening posts; we are not the only ones looking at aliens.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2693`

## fircon.5036.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.5036.name&lang=en)

### 中文

温和种族之怒

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2209`

### English

## fircon.5036.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.5036.desc&lang=en)

我们以为那些能够穿越星空的人不会如此原始地诉诸暴力。\n\n§R请离开。§!

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2210`

### English

We thought those who could traverse the stars wouldn't be so primitive as to resort to violence.\n\n§RLeave.§!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2696`

## fircon.5036.tt

§R我们在§Y[From.solar_system.GetName]星系§!的所有舰船都在行动中失踪了。\n所有通往§Y[From.solar_system.GetName]星系§!的超空间航道都变得很不稳定。§!

## fircon.5040.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.5040.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们已经见识过了泛星系的真面目，并选择隐居避世。然而我们还是遭到了骚扰，你们真的想看看我们的宝藏吗？那就让你们见识一下。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2214`

### English

We have seen what kind of galaxy it is and chose to avoid it, yet again we are disturbed, do you really want to see our treasures? Then let us show you.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2701`

## fircon.5040.a.tt

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.5040.a.tt&lang=en)

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2215`

### English

§REvery system near the §Y[From.GetName] system§! has had new hyperlanes established.§!

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2702`

## fircon.5050.name

礼貌请求

### English

Polite Request

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2704`

## fircon.5050.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afircon.5050.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

试图通过与[event_target:solarpunk_country.GetSpeciesAdj]合作来获取超空间航道技术知识的进度一直缓慢得令人沮丧。要想像他们一样掌握局域超空间航道群，我们似乎需要花费数千年的时间来编制超空间波动的目录。我们收到了他们首席代表的新信息。\n\n“老实说，你们在这里让我们越来越不舒服了，既然我们已经帮你们完成了来这里的目的，我们得请你们离开。我们判断你我双方的生活方式不可相容，担心你们在未来可能会觊觎我们拥有的东西。为了避免显得过于粗鲁，我们愿意送给你们一颗星球。我们会负责运输的，不用担心。”

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2217`

### English

The insights we have gained into hyperlane technology through our cooperation with the [event_target:solarpunk_country.GetSpeciesAdj] has been frustratingly slow. It seems that to achieve the mastery of a local hyperlane cluster like they have, we would need to spend thousands of years cataloging hyper-fluctuations. We have received a new communication from their chief representative.\n\n"To be honest, your presence here has been grating, so now that we have taught you what you came for, we need to ask you to leave. Having judged that our ways of life are incompatible, we worry that you might covet what we have in the future. So to prevent any feelings of ill will, we wish to offer you one of our worlds. We will handle the transportation, don't worry."

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2705`

### English

## SOLARPUNK_CAT_DESC

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3ASOLARPUNK_CAT_DESC&lang=en)

### 中文

这个星系中有大量的超空间航道。通常情况下，我们会认为它们太小了，宇宙飞船无法在其中航行，但是它们的数量本身就很惊人。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2224`

### English

There are an unusual amount of hyperlanes in this system. Usually, we would dismiss these as too small to facilitate a spaceship, but the sheer amount is intriguing in and of itself.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2713`

### English

Our Detection Arrays discovered at least one cloaked, non-allied fleet and have forced it to Decloak.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2761`

## first_contact.1120.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1120.name&lang=en)

### 中文

邀请函

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2272`

### English

The Invitation

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2774`

## first_contact.1120.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1120.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!派遣了一艘舰船到我们的边境上，广播着重复的信号。\n\n翻译这些信息颇具挑战，但我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]相信他们是在邀请我们派出以为代表去往他们的舰船上参加一场持续多日的文化交流活动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2273`

### English

The §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! stationed a vessel at our border, broadcasting a repeating signal.\n\nIt was a challenge to translate their message, but our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] believe they are inviting one of our representatives to a multi-day cultural exchange event on their ship.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2775`

## first_contact.1120.desc.nomadic

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1120.desc.nomadic&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!停靠在我们舰队边上，广播着重复的信号。\n\n翻译这些信息颇具挑战，但我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]相信他们是在邀请我们派出一位代表去往他们的舰船上参加一场持续多日的文化交流活动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2274`

### English

The §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! drew alongside our fleet, broadcasting a repeating signal.\n\nIt was a challenge to translate their message, but our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] believe they are inviting one of our representatives to a multi-day cultural exchange event on their ship.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2776`

## first_contact.1120.a.proactive

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2275`

### English

What a trusting gesture.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2777`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2781`

## first_contact.1125.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1125.name&lang=en)

### 中文

餐前准备

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2280`

### English

Mise En Place

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2782`

## first_contact.1125.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1125.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[This.Owner.GetEnvoy][This.Leader.GetName]和我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]连蒙带猜地接受了外星人的文化交流邀请。§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的热烈反应应该是积极的。不久之后，他们发来了表示派对倒计时的象形文字。\n\n[This.Owner.GetEnvoyCap][This.Leader.GetName]正在利用剩余的时间准备并进一步学习我们目前获得的语言样本。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2281`

### English

With a little bit of guesswork, [This.Owner.GetEnvoy] [This.Leader.GetName] and our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] accepted the alien cultural exchange invitation. We interpreted the enthusiastic response from the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! as positive. Shortly after, they sent pictographs depicting a countdown to the party.\n\n[This.Owner.GetEnvoyCap] [This.Leader.GetName] is using the remaining time to prepare and further study the language samples we have obtained.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2783`

## first_contact.1125.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1125.a&lang=en)

### 中文

这是不可多得的学习机会。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2282`

### English

This is a unique learning opportunity.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2784`

### English

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2785`

## first_contact.1125.b

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1125.b&lang=en)

This would be a perfect chance to covertly gather intel.

### English

We agree, but will equip our envoy with our spy tech.

## first_contact.1130.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1130.name&lang=en)

### 中文

参会之前

### English

Pre-Game

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2788`

## first_contact.1130.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1130.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!文化交流活动的倒计时马上就到尾声了。[This.Owner.GetEnvoyCap][This.Leader.GetName]很快就要登上前往外星特使舰船的太空梭。出于尊重，宴会期间与我们的[This.Owner.GetEnvoy]的通讯将会暂停。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2287`

### English

The countdown to the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! cultural exchange event is nearing completion. [This.Owner.GetEnvoyCap] [This.Leader.GetName] is about to board the shuttle to the alien envoy ship. Communication with our [This.Owner.GetEnvoy] will pause for the duration of the party out of respect.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2789`

## first_contact.1130.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1130.a&lang=en)

我们应该尽可能地融入他们的文化。

### English

We should embrace their culture as best as possible.

## first_contact.1135.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1135.name&lang=en)

### 中文

第二次机会

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2294`

### English

Second Chances

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2796`

## first_contact.1135.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1135.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

我们对§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的邀请不闻不问冒犯到了他们。他们的态度发生了一百八十度大转弯。在后续的通讯中我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]翻译出了一些敌意性的言辞。\n\n他们似乎不想要我们拒绝。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2295`

### English

Our lack of interest in their invitation has offended the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!. Their tone has dramatically changed. Our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] translated some hostile language within their follow-up communication.\n\nIt seems they do not want to take no for an answer.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2797`

## first_contact.1135.a

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1135.a&lang=en)

### 中文

很好很好。我们会出席的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2296`

### English

Very well. We will attend.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2798`

## first_contact.1135.a.tooltip

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1135.a.tooltip&lang=en)

我们终究会为了顺畅的外交关系出席的。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2297`

### English

We will attend, after all, for the sake of smooth diplomatic relations.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2799`

### English

The answer is still no.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2800`

## first_contact.1135.b.tooltip

我们没有任何理由接受这个邀请

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2299`

### English

We will in no way accept the invitation.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2801`

## first_contact.1140.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1140.name&lang=en)

### 中文

谍报失误

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2300`

### English

Espionage Blunder

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2802`

## first_contact.1140.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1140.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的活动持续的时间远超预期。\n\n[This.Owner.GetEnvoy][This.Leader.GetName]的太空梭返回时汇报了任务失败。\n\n在活动过程中，主办方找到了谍报装置。[This.Owner.GetEnvoyCap][This.Leader.GetName]遭到了审问和训斥，并被遣返。通过后备间谍科技获取的数据，我们现在理解了他们的语言和科技，然而我们的外交前景一片渺茫。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2301`

### English

The §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!'s event lasted much longer than expected.\n\nWhen [This.Owner.GetEnvoy] [This.Leader.GetName]'s shuttle returned, they reported that the operation failed.\n\nDuring the event, the hosts found an espionage device. [This.Owner.GetEnvoyCap] [This.Leader.GetName] was interrogated, scolded and then asked to leave. Using the data from the back-up spy tech, we now understand their language and technology, although our diplomatic future will start on rocky ground.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2803`

## first_contact.1140.A

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1140.A&lang=en)

### 中文

真不幸。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2302`

### English

Unfortunate.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2804`

## first_contact.1145.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1145.name&lang=en)

### 中文

扫描完毕

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2303`

### English

Scan Complete

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2805`

## first_contact.1145.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1145.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的活动举办时间远超预期。\n\n乘坐太空梭返回后，[This.Owner.GetEnvoy][This.Leader.GetName]汇报了行动大获成功。不仅这种外星语言被大部分破解了，我们的扫描也揭示了他们科技能力的清晰图景。\n\n这将会极大地促进我们与§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!未来的关系，同时还让他们对我们对他们科技了如指掌一事蒙在鼓里。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2304`

### English

The §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!'s event lasted much longer than expected.\n\nWhen [This.Owner.GetEnvoy] [This.Leader.GetName]'s shuttle returned, they reported that the operation was a success. Not only did they successfully decipher most of the alien language, but our scan has given us a clear picture of their technological capabilities.\n\nThis will greatly enhance further relations with the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!, without them being aware of our understanding of their scientific knowledge.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2806`

## first_contact.1150.name

### 中文

宴会人生

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2305`

### English

Life Of The Party

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2807`

## first_contact.1150.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1150.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

敞开心胸来到这场持续多日的盛会，[This.Owner.GetEnvoy][This.Leader.GetName]参加了音乐、戏剧和舞蹈的表演，并尝试了许多餐饮传统。他们对各种体验的接受得到了§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的热烈欢迎。\n\n离开时，[This.Owner.GetEnvoyCap][This.Leader.GetName]对他们的语言获得了非常深切的理解，对他们的文化也颇为赞赏。这段经历将会为我们未来与§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!更加积极正面的外交奠定基础。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2306`

### English

Entering the multi-day event with an open mind, [This.Owner.GetEnvoy] [This.Leader.GetName] joined in musical, theater, and dance performances and explored various culinary traditions. Their receptiveness to experience was met enthusiastically by the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!.\n\n[This.Owner.GetEnvoyCap] [This.Leader.GetName] has left with an intimate understanding of their language and a deep appreciation of their culture. Their experience will pave the way to more positive diplomatic interactions with the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2808`

### 中文

来自新朋友的邀请真是亲切啊。

### English

A kind invitation from new friends.

## first_contact.1155.name
