### 中文

略煞风景

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2308`

### English

Threw In The Towel

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2810`

## first_contact.1155.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1155.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

[This.Owner.GetEnvoyCap][This.Leader.GetName]在这场持续多日的活动中客气地参观着文艺活动，小试着他们的饮食文化。不温不火的态度让§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!颇感沮丧。根据我们对他们语言的理解，他们说我们的特使“很无聊”。\n\n我们的特使被护送回到太空梭上并被示意离开。特使已经掌握了他们的语言，并对他们的文化有了初步的了解，我们可以在未来的外交互动中以此为基础。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2309`

### English

[This.Owner.GetEnvoyCap] [This.Leader.GetName] spent the multi-day event reservedly observing artistic shows and sampling the culinary traditions. The lack of enthusiasm frustrated the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!. According to our understanding of their language, our envoy was considered boring.\n\nEscorted back to their shuttle and signaled to leave, our envoy has left with competence in their language and a surface understanding of their culture, something we can build upon for future diplomatic interactions.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2811`

### 中文

特使累了。

### English

The envoy tried.

## first_contact.1160.name

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1160.name&lang=en)

### 中文

“渴”望联系

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2311`

### English

Hungry For Connection

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2813`

## first_contact.1160.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1160.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

在这场持续数天的活动这，§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!对营养需求的差异的沮丧显而易见。幸好[This.Owner.GetEnvoy][This.Leader.GetName]还是积极参与到了其他的娱乐活动中，并建立了互相的尊重。\n\n[This.Owner.GetEnvoyCap][This.Leader.GetName]离开时对§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!的语言文化获得了基本的了解。值得赞扬的是，他们似乎对这一饮食灾难泰然自若，并为我们提供了许多礼仪手册。虽然乏味，但这些知识应该有助于顺利进行未来的外交互动。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2312`

### English

The §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!'s disappointment in the difference between our nutritional needs couldn't have been more apparent over the multi-day event. Thankfully [This.Owner.GetEnvoy] [This.Leader.GetName] did their best to take an active part in the entertainment events, and mutual respect was established.\n\n[This.Owner.GetEnvoyCap] [This.Leader.GetName] has left with a decent understanding of the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!'s language and culture. To their credit, they seemed to take this culinary mishap in stride, and have provided us with a number of etiquette manuals. While tedious, this knowledge should help smooth future diplomatic interactions.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2814`

理解是外交的基石。

### English

Understanding is the cornerstone of diplomacy.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2816`

## first_contact.1165.desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Afirst_contact.1165.desc&lang=en)

### 中文

第二次拒绝§H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§!让我们前往他们的舰船的邀请得到了情绪激烈的回复。我们收到了一份来自多名外星船员长达数个小时的通讯，我们的[This.Owner.GetLinguists]认为他们似乎是在因我们的不愿出息而发出谴责。\n\n这些通信，加上从他们船上截获的信号，使我们能够破译足够多他们的语言以产生有意义的理解。尽管他们对这一事件持消极态度，但我们现在可以建立更适当的外交关系了。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/first_contact_l_simp_chinese.yml:2315`

### English

The second rejection by the §H[This.ContactCountry.GetName]§! to invite us to their ship was met with a strong emotional response. We received communication from multiple alien crew members for several hours, in what our [This.Owner.GetLinguists] believe was an attempt to guilt us for not attending.\n\nThese communications, along with intercepted signals from their ship, have allowed us to decipher enough of their language to have a working understanding. We can now initiate more appropriate diplomatic connections, although they have taken this initial incident negatively.

Source: `localisation/english/first_contact_l_english.yml:2817`

这些外星人可真顽固。

### English

These aliens are persistent.
